### 233 subject events

- **1912.02** `vienna-life-birth-letter-from-the-dead/the-letter-is-real` tags: infancy, family, secret, letter, drama
  邮差把一封边角磨白的信塞进门缝，收信人是一个已经死了三年的名字。Josef Bauer把Klara放在膝上，拆开信，里面只有一句：“孩子像不像他？”Hermann Kral在门口探头，问是不是寄错了；Josef Bauer把信折回去，说“没有寄错”。
  _effects: flag +letter_from_dead_relative; knowledge “一封写给死去亲人的信问：孩子像不像他。”; mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/smile-at-the-lamp` tags: infancy, family, first, warm
  Josef Bauer把Klara抱到窗边，煤汽灯罩上有一圈晃动的光。孩子盯着看了很久，忽然笑出声，口水滴在Josef Bauer的袖口上。屋里的人都停了一下，像是听见了什么了不起的消息。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; health 0.009999999999999898 (a good day)_

- **1913.01** `iw18-infancy.ceiling-shadows::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-shadow-bows` tags: infancy, night, wonder, gaslight, fear, family, vienna, urban
  煤气灯芯拧到最小，天花板上一件挂着的大衣被风推得晃了晃。Klara躺在摇篮里忽然笑出声，朝那片影子伸出两只手。Josef Bauer抬头看了很久，只看见衣钩和一块掉漆的墙皮。
  _effects: mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +laughed_at_ceiling_shadow_

- **1913.02** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-nonsense` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Klara说了一长串谁也听不懂的话，最后用力点头，像是在宣布一件已经决定的事。Josef Bauer认真听完，也点了点头；孩子满意地爬走了，留下大人们互相看着，忍不住笑。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; skill_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-toddler-wrong-cradle/the-blanket-is-wrong` tags: infancy, family, rumour, identity, comic, drama
  Emil Weiss盯着Klara的摇篮看了很久，说这床蓝被子不是这家人的。Josef Bauer把被子翻过来，角落绣着一个陌生的姓；Klara却抓着被角不放，像是认得它。Emil Weiss退后一步，说“我什么都没说”。
  _effects: flag +wrong_cradle_blanket; knowledge “摇篮里的蓝被子绣着一个陌生的姓。”; mood -0.03_

- **1915.02** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  黑楼梯间只有门缝透进来的一点光。Klara踩空时伸手去抓扶手，手臂先撞在铁端上，整个人滚下三级。Josef Bauer把Klara抱到灶边，拿湿冷毛巾按住肿起的地方，又撕了一条旧布缠紧。第二天Klara举着胳膊下楼，照样去院子里玩。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/lost-relative` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josef Bauer在收拾箱子时翻出一张没有署名的照片，说那是家里一个“去了很远地方”的人。Klara问还回不回来，Josef Bauer把照片翻过去，说先把名字记住，别在饭桌上问。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一张没有署名的照片，照片上的人去了很远的地方。”; flag +family_legend_photo; mood -0.01_

- **1916.02** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/stolen-marble` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Rosa把Klara的玻璃弹珠藏进鞋里，被当场看见。Rosa说只是“替你拿着”，Klara没有揭穿，也没有再拿出第二颗。两个人蹲在墙根，把一只蚂蚁看了很久，谁也没有先回家。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood -0.01_

- **1916.02** `vienna-life-child-inherited-wish/the-caregiver-cries` tags: childhood, family, wish, grief, uncanny, drama
  Josef Bauer没有回答，起身去洗碗，水开得很大。Klara跟到厨房，拽了拽衣角，说“你别哭，我不说了”。Josef Bauer蹲下来，把孩子抱得很紧，说“你说，你继续说”。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +inherited_wish_grief_

- **1917.04** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-lose` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  铁环滚进沟沿，Klara扑过去抓，膝盖先着了地。Mitzi Lechner把铁环拎起来，在手里转了一圈，说不能白捡。Klara摸出一颗石弹子递过去，Mitzi Lechner这才松手。回家路上Klara一瘸一拐，裤膝上沾着石屑和血，经过Mitzi Lechner家门口时特意绕到街对面。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.039999999999999994; condition_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Klara拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Berta Weiss弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Klara接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1918.12** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-recover` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Klara在洗衣房的长凳上睡着，醒来时喉咙像塞了棉花。Josef Bauer把最后的干净布巾浸了凉水，敷在Klara额头上，整夜换了七八回。第四天Klara能坐起来喝粥，下嘴唇脱下一小片皮，Josef Bauer用指尖替Klara拈掉。
  _effects: condition; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Klara后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Klara没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Klara用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.030000000000000013; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Berta Bauer的鞋掉在井台边，Klara先去捡鞋，回头才看见Berta Bauer摔在湿石头上。Josef Weiss用围裙裹住Berta Bauer，血从膝盖渗出来。Josef Bauer夜里把Klara叫到灶台前，数落了很久，最后把锅里剩下的半勺汤倒回大锅，只给Klara留了半碗。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (在井台边摔了一跤); mood -0.06; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Klara把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Klara踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Klara单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1921.05** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-confiscated` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Klara把书压在算术本底下，只露出书脊。Grete Stein走过来，抽走算术本，书也跟着滑出来。折角那页画着一艘帆船。Grete Stein合上书，说放学后擦黑板，擦一星期；书被锁进讲台抽屉，没往校长室送。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Berta Bauer哭得打嗝，手指一直朝井台方向指。Klara蹲下把Berta Bauer驮上背，沿着墙根走，数砖缝给Berta Bauer听：一、二、三……数到第四圈，背上的呼吸才匀了。天黑时Klara的胳膊麻得抬不过肩，Josef Bauer回来接过孩子，Klara扶着门框甩了好一会儿手。
  _effects: skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001037:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1922.10** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-ok` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  市场里的菜摊一字排开，Klara把十五克鲁泽排在掌心，先买了土豆，又去盐摊上要了一小包。摊主用旧报纸把盐裹好，Klara把它塞进围裙内袋，土豆兜在身前。回家时Josef Bauer接过铜板一枚枚数，土豆一颗没少。
  _effects: food; cash -0.15 (市场花销); relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1924.02** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Klara在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Klara用牙咬断，重新来。Josef Bauer坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Klara把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1924.02** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/coins-into-box` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Anna Bauer的箱子摊在床板上，里面是两件衬衫、一双旧靴和母亲塞的一小袋面粉。Klara把攒了半年的零钱数到第三遍，一共一克朗二十赫勒，全塞进箱角的袜子里。Anna Bauer看见了，没说话，只把箱子扣上，铜扣响了一声。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (把攒下的零钱塞给离家的兄姐); relation kin with person-anna-bauer; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1924.02** `p9-childhood-adolescence.church-club/club-one-visit-only` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Klara去了一个下午，看那些旧歌本里夹着褪色的圣像卡，工厂来的几个年轻人在最后一排用手指敲长凳打拍子。第二个星期天Klara没再去，在院子里帮母亲晾被单。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.010000000000000009; skill_

- **1925.08** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/give-up-for-overtime` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  工头说这个月赶印一批账簿，晚上加班每小时多给二十赫勒。Klara站在教室门口听完课程表，转身回厂里搬纸。Helene Winter 2追出来说座位可以保留两周，Klara点点头，手已经摸上了作坊的门把，夜里机器的声音把课本的事盖了过去。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (夜间加班多挣的工钱); skill; mood -0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/went-once-and-left` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Klara交了第一个月的会费，进去坐了一刻钟。屋里太吵，下棋的争论盖过了念书的声音，他借的书也被人翻得卷了边。下个月他路过门口，没再进去交十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (只交了一个月会费); relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber; flag +workers_youth_quit; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1926.05** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Klara把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Josef Bauer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Klara那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/joined-and-borrowed` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Klara跟着Luise Weiss 2走进印刷厂后面的屋子，交了十赫勒会费，在借书簿上按了手印。他挑了一本讲蒸汽机的册子，封面烫金已经磨掉；管理员说下月十赫勒要按时交，书才能续借。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (工人青年会月费); skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; flag +workers_youth_member; mood 0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1928.03** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne先看Klara的手，又让Klara推两下刨子试试。Klara用力过猛，刨刀啃进木头，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne皱眉，让再来一遍。第二遍木花薄而匀。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说“留下”，当面点了三克朗给Josef Bauer，算头一周饭钱，又指了指墙上的钟，说迟到一刻钟扣半天。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

  Elisabeth Bauer走后，靠墙那张床空了出来，Klara把自己的铺盖搬过去，也接过那本记着欠账的旧账本：面包店三克朗、煤铺一克朗八十赫勒。晚上Klara坐在床沿一笔笔抄清，第一次知道家里的钱是怎么缺的。
  _effects: relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer; skill; mood -0.04999999999999999; cash -0.5 (替兄姐补齐欠煤铺的零头)_

  _effects: relation_end partner with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; reputation -0.07; mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1930.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money

  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费); relation friend with person-berta-weiss_

  回信没有到Klara手里。Hedwig的母亲先拆了它，连信封上的邮戳一起摊在洗衣房的桌上，说有人写字来缠自家孩子。话传到院子里时已经添了油，Berta Weiss隔着栏杆把原话学了一遍，说得很难听；Hedwig再见到Klara时，眼睛先往母亲那边看。Klara站在水桶边，知道这封信已经被拆成了两家的脸面。

  Klara说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Berta Huber没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Klara听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1935.10** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-cart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  车夫把价钱咬得很死，Klara还到五克朗又添了二十赫勒，才说定跑一趟。Berta Huber扶着床架，车过路口时煤箱歪了一下，细灰撒在路面上。到了地方，东西卸在门厅，天已经大亮。
  _effects: cash -6 (雇马车搬家具一趟的工钱); residence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1936.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting

  旧账本摊开，纸页上的铅笔字迹深浅不一。Elisabeth Bauer念一笔，Klara就在边上记一笔，Grete Bauer的钢笔悬在遗嘱上方没有落。Josef Bauer咳了一声，说屋顶那八克朗是自己看着修的。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; flag +demands_estate_accounting_

  首演夜，Klara在第二首歌的中间忘了两句词，干脆停下来听乐队多走了一个小节，再接着唱。台下有人以为这是新加的停顿，笑了一声，随后整间屋子都放松了。Berta Weiss在后台说，今晚之后，节目单上可以印Klara的名字。
  _effects: state_add fame state:world-vienna-1912:409; cash 2 (首演酬劳与加演分成); role; reputation 0.06; skill; flag +debut_success_

  _effects: role; flag +friendship_broken; reputation -0.03999999999999998_

- **1937.01** `curse15-bargain.borrowed-name::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-price-is-a-name` tags: wish, power, curse, ritual, bargain, office, irony, vienna
  Alois Winter 2只要Klara在一张空白委任状上写下真名，说剩下的由办事处去办。一个月过去，职位没有来，Klara的名字却出现在一份领款单上，金额和签名都不属于Klara。Alois Winter 2调走了，窗口换了人，没有人愿意查那张纸是从哪一叠里抽出来的。
  _effects: cash -2 (打点办事处的钱); flag +name_used_without_office; reputation -0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007489_

- **1937.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::pharmacy-war-requisition/pharmacy-war-flour-powder` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, crisis
  药房的后门开着，一个穿军大衣的人按包卖药：退烧粉、止血粉、止痛粉。Klara买了三包，回家发现其中一包是面粉；再去找人，后门已经锁了。他把那包面粉倒进碗里，又倒回纸袋，没舍得扔。
  _effects: cash -15 (three packets bought from a man in an army coat); condition; role; knowledge “后门按包卖的药可能是面粉；再回去找人，门已经锁了。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1937.03** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara把Friedrich Hofer 2和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Klara把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

  Klara记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Alois Winter 2说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Alois Winter 2陪着Klara去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; mood -0.09999999999999998_

  梦里，Klara看见地窖的水漫过脚踝，木箱一只只浮起来。第二天一早，Klara把箱子和煤筐搬到床上。中午，楼下的水管裂了，水沿着墙根流进地窖，只浸湿了一只空桶。Ferdinand Winter说Klara运气好，Klara没有解释。
  _effects: cash -1 (搬箱子时打碎了一只罐子); relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter; knowledge “我梦见地窖进水，第二天水管真的裂了。”; flag +df18_moved_crates_before_flood; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.01** `df18-dream.water-in-the-cellar::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/tell-the-landlord` tags: dream, omen, home, water, coincidence, eerie, everyday, vienna
  Klara把梦告诉房东。房东笑了一声，说老房子下水慢，做梦都带水汽。第二天，房东带人来查，真在墙里找到一条裂缝，顺手把地窖的租金减了一克朗。Klara把省下的钱买了一盏新灯，放在楼梯口。
  _effects: cash 1 (房东减掉的一克朗地窖租金); reputation 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000094:child; knowledge “房东因为我的梦查了墙，减了地窖租金。”; flag +df18_landlord_fixed_crack; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.03** `p22-goat-heir::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/goat-keeps-the-building` tags: inheritance, absurd, comic, goat, tenement, legal, rumor, public
  公证人念完遗嘱，整栋楼归一只叫“伯爵”的山羊所有，条件是它继续住在天井里。Klara和Emil Weiss把租金记在羊的名下，每天轮流挤奶；半年后，房东太太的侄子来争产，看见山羊站在楼梯口，竟先脱帽行了个礼。那天夜里，羊睡在门房，钥匙挂在它的角上。
  _effects: state_add p22-wild-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:833; state_add p22-scandal-engine state:world-vienna-1912:834; cash 4 (rent surplus held in the goat name); relation friend with person-emil-weiss; flag +goat_kept_building; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +wild_fortune_active; flag +scandal_engine_active_

- **1942.09** `fa17-cabaret.debut::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/booed-off` tags: art, performance, cabaret, debut, reputation, failure, theatre, vienna
  Klara的假发在第二段副歌时滑到耳边。前排有人喊“下去吧”，随后整个大厅都跟着拍手，节奏比乐队还齐。Friedrich Hofer 2在幕布后没有出来，Klara自己走回后台，经过化妆镜时看见脸上还留着半层没卸干净的粉。
  _effects: state_add fa17-starving-artist state:world-vienna-1912:835; state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:836; reputation -0.06000000000000005; mood -0.08999999999999997; flag +booed_on_debut_

- **1943.01** `r15-wish.power-offer::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/puppet-office` tags: politics, power, wish, office, status, vienna, urban, crisis
  报纸把Klara称作“新任主席”，照片很大，办公室很漂亮。真正的决定在另一间屋里做出。Klara每天签字，签的都是别人写好的东西。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation 0.07999999999999996; position 名誉主席 @org-sdap; flag +puppet_office_

- **1943.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011896_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:p22-wild-fortune/the-story-gets-taller/the-crowd-remembers` tags: p22-wild-fortune, wild_fortune, fortune, reversal, absurdity, inheritance, luck, public
  一年过去，Klara的荒唐故事没有变小，反而多了一个愿意作证的人。债主没有立刻上门，亲戚也还愿意来吃饭；Klara把新添的银器擦了一遍，发现其中一只底下刻着别人的名字。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-wild-fortune.height 55 -> 59; state_parameter p22-wild-fortune.absurdity 80 -> 83; state_parameter p22-wild-fortune.witnesses 60 -> 65; state_parameter p22-wild-fortune.fragility 45 -> 47; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:p22-scandal-engine/the-rumor-learns-new-words/the-story-gets-a-new-witness` tags: p22-scandal-engine, scandal_engine, scandal, rumor, secret, leverage, shame, network
  又过了一年，流言没有熄，却学会了新的说法。Klara在咖啡馆里听见自己的故事被讲成三个版本，每个版本都比上一个多一个证人；没有人来追问，也没有人真正忘记。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.heat 30 -> 27; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.reach 45 -> 49; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.leverage 35 -> 38; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.shame 15 -> 17; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:fa17-starving-artist/rent-and-pages/a-year-of-work` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition
  又一年过去，Klara把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Klara把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 30 -> 34; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 5 -> 8; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 70 -> 68; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Klara想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1943.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Franz Huber 3在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Franz Huber 3敲开Klara的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1943.01** `state_reaction:public-scandal/gossip` tags: public-scandal, scandal, reputation, social, state, state_reaction, home, family
  Klara走进咖啡馆时，邻桌的声音低了一拍。没有人当面说什么，但每个人都知道该说什么。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; reputation -0.030000000000000027_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012681_

- **1943.03** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara把Franz Huber 3和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Klara把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101_

- **1943.05** `state_reaction:p22-wild-fortune/a-witness-laughs` tags: p22-wild-fortune, wild_fortune, fortune, reversal, absurdity, inheritance, luck, public
  Klara的荒唐运气成了酒馆里的笑话。有人学着Klara走路的样子，有人问下一次什么时候轮到自己；Klara笑着应付，回家后把窗帘拉得比平时早。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-wild-fortune.absurdity 83 -> 89; state_parameter p22-wild-fortune.witnesses 65 -> 61; state_parameter p22-wild-fortune.fragility 47 -> 52; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012736_

- **1943.12** `ad20-loan::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/loan-refused` tags: loan, debt, credit, everyday, obligation, money, vienna, urban
  Franz Huber 3把Klara的收入证明翻来覆去看了两遍，说数目不够。Klara问能不能少借一点；Franz Huber 3说“少借也要还得起”。走出门时，Klara把证明信重新叠好，发现上面有一道自己刚才捏出的折痕。
  _effects: flag +loan_refused; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101; cash -1 (paperwork and a wasted morning)_

  仪式做完，Ferdinand Bauer把铜钥匙放进Klara手里，钥匙却烫得像刚从炉子里夹出来。Klara松手时掌心已经起泡；第二天，聘书上的名字褪成一片空白，只有Klara的手还疼。Ferdinand Bauer说这算成了，也算没成。

  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Grete Stein攥着Klara的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-grete-stein_

  苏联士兵进城时，Klara躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Klara去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

  报纸在头版登了Klara和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Klara把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.030000000000000027; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:955; flag +scandal_weathered; knowledge “照片是真的，合同也是真的；报纸没有再追，但档案里留了底片。”; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1945.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Emil Huber 2从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。

  _effects: cash -0.5 (请洗衣妇代洗工服); mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Klara去了Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，坐在后排听完了关于环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsam的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-building:person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:006250-1938 as participant; knowledge “我参加了环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Ga。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.04** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/speak-with-rent-numbers` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Klara没有喊口号，只问在场谁家的煤还够烧三天。十几只手举起来，连Josef Lechner也举了。书记说这些数字比演说有用，散会时几个人围上来问自己那条街怎么算。
  _effects: reputation 0.05999999999999994; skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000708:child_

- **1947.09** `ml18-inheritance.uncles-press::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sell-the-subscription-list` tags: inheritance, family, business, debt, legal, money, vienna, urban
  公证人把一封厚信推到桌子中央：Klara的叔父在第二区留下一间小印刷所、一台坏掉的压机和十四克朗的欠账。Rosa Moser说那台压机早该当废铁，但订阅名单上还有三百个名字。Klara把名单卖给对街的印厂，替叔父还了债，剩下的钱刚好够换一套冬衣。
  _effects: cash 12 (sale of the print shop subscription list); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:1100; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112; skill; flag +inherited_print_shop; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112; flag -sudden_fortune; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1948.01** `r15-wish.power-offer::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/stage-understudy` tags: politics, power, wish, office, status, vienna, urban, postwar
  正式职位没有落到Klara头上，但剧院正在排一出关于权力的戏。Berta Bauer 3说Klara的形象合适，可以在舞台上扮演那位即将被处决的国王。
  _effects: state_add stage-king state:world-vienna-1912:1099; reputation 0.050000000000000044; flag +stage_king; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1948.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Klara想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1948.04** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stairwell-rumor/stairwell-rumor.warn-the-latch` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Klara没传话，只在二楼门口站了一会儿，提醒Otto Winter夜里把门闩好，说楼梯间有人爱嚼舌头。Otto Winter点点头，把门闩推过去，发出很轻的一声。此后送活的人还是照来，闲话却渐渐没人接。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1948.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Klara胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `absurd15-wish.crown-for-a-day::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/festival-king-only` tags: wish, power, crown, office, irony, festival, vienna, urban
  代表团没有带来任命书，只带来一套戏服：市庆游行需要一个“国王”，一天两场，管一顿饭。Klara穿上戏服时，围观的人鼓起掌来。掌声是真的，王冠是假的，晚饭也是真的。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:1160; cash 2 (游行演出的酬劳); flag +hired_as_festival_king; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

  画店老板把Klara的作品推回来，说尺寸不合墙面，题材也不合买主的口味。Klara抱着画走回阁楼，路上经过一家亮着灯的咖啡馆，玻璃里映出一个抱着旧画框的人，像一个迟到的搬运工。

- **1949.03** `r2-law.lottery-ticket::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/small-prize` tags: finance, lottery, gambling, luck, neighbors, vienna, urban, postwar
  Maria Winter 2把开奖名单钉在柜台上，Klara的号码中了末等奖，够买两袋面粉。Klara把烟盒上的号码对了两遍，Maria Winter 2说末等奖不用去总行，在这里就能兑。Mitzi Weiss 3在旁边说，先把上次借的面粉钱还了；Klara数出钱，剩下的刚好够买一张新彩票。
  _effects: cash 6 (中奖号码写在烟盒上); flag +won_lottery_consolation_prize; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; knowledge “consolation prizes can be redeemed at the sales agent”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1949.04** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1949.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1949.07** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.caretaker-notice` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Klara去找看门人，说院子里四面墙，声音绕不出去。第二天楼梯间的木板上贴了一张纸：晚上十点后请勿放留声机，鞋匠铺也请收锤。纸条没有落款，只有房东事务所的戳。Josefine Hofer路过时看了很久，把留声机的盖子合上了。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1949.09** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1949.11** `ml18-investment.transylvanian-mine::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sell-the-shares-to-a-baron` tags: investment, mining, fraud, money, risk, business, vienna, urban
  矿山还没出金，Klara就把股份转给了一个想给女儿做嫁妆的男爵，赚了二十五克朗。半年后，假金矿的消息见报，男爵在俱乐部里拦住Klara，问知不知道岩芯的事。Klara说不知道，手却一直放在酒杯后面。
  _effects: cash 25 (profit from selling the mining shares early); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:1215; reputation -0.020000000000000018; knowledge “男爵买下股份时，矿山还没有出过一克金。”; mood 0.040000000000000036; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1950.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016835_

  Klara把Alois Winter 2的名字写在“联络员”后面，月薪一克朗，职责是每周来两次、把外面的消息带进来。Berta Bauer 3说这个岗位没有编制，Klara说先按临时用工算；第一个月，Alois Winter 2带来的消息比文件还多。
  _effects: position 联络员 @morgue:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35-evt:world-vienna-1912:010204; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; flag +appointed_ally; knowledge “联络员不在正式编制里，工资走临时用工。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `initiative:found_process:org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:005626`
  权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。
  _effects: historical process 权力俱乐部的持续运动; historical development:initiative-org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:005626-1950 as organizer; historical development:initiative-org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:005626-1950 as organizer; knowledge “权力俱乐部的持续运动不是一次会议，而是我们这些年积累出来的持续组织。”; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

  历史进程《权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先》从 emerging 进入 mobilizing。

  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1950.07** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  手推车每走十几步就要停一下，楼道里的门半开着，有人探头看热闹。Klara把照片铁盒交给Emil Huber 2抱着，自己低头把最后一箱锅具拖上楼。天擦黑时，新屋里才勉强归置出下脚的地方。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

  捐款交出去三个月，诊所还没有开门，先来了一辆新的弹簧马车和一张桃花心木办公桌。Gustav Lechner 2把账本摊在桌上，说委员会认为“体面的门面”也是诊所的一部分。Alois Lechner 2在门口骂了一句，Klara把账本翻到最后一页，发现自己捐的钱只剩一半。

  Klara把灵车和两匹马卖给了城西的殡仪馆，拿到的钱正好抵掉欠账，只剩下一盒黑手套和半瓶擦铜油。Berta Bauer 3说这笔遗产算是结清了；Klara却总觉得，自己像替一个陌生人做了一场没有尸体的买卖。

- **1953.11** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.buy-coal` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  炉门一开，黑烟先扑出来，Klara眯着眼把纸团往里塞。火苗舔着碎煤慢慢亮起来，Helene Huber隔着门喊当心一氧化碳。水壶开始咕噜作响，屋里才有点热乎气。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1953.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Grete Huber拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

  一个市民代表团真的来到Klara门口，带来一份任命书：公共礼仪官，负责节庆、吊唁和接待。职位不大，薪水也不高，但上面有印章，有任期，有一间可以锁门的办公室。Klara把任命书读了三遍，才确认这不是戏票。

- **1954.06** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.remake-coat/remake-coat.short-jacket` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, mending, clothing, household
  Klara把外套的肩线拆开，往里收了四指，袖口剪掉一截，改出一件到胯骨的短上衣。剩下的布头拼成两只手套，针脚粗，戴着能挡风。她在镜子前转了一圈，肩还是有点空，但总算能穿出门。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1954.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Klara胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙)_

- **1955.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Grete Huber嫌Klara的床单滴水滴到Grete Huber家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Klara下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Klara把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Grete Huber那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:020404_

  分房名单又往后推了一年，Klara一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Maria Lechner送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); condition; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000781:child_

  Luise 2在走廊里凑够了票数，把Klara负责的那间办公室转给了一个更听话的人。会议记录写得很干净：因工作需要。Klara回到办公室时，门牌已经换了，钥匙还能插进去，只是转不动。
  _effects: reputation -0.07999999999999996; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003582:child; condition; flag +office_quietly_taken; cash -1 (重新置办办公用品的钱)_

- **1955.01** `initiative:found_process:org-sdap`
  Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)在Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。
  _effects: historical process Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)的持续运动; historical development:initiative-org-sdap-1955 as organizer; historical development:initiative-org-sdap-1955 as organizer; historical development:initiative-org-sdap-1955 as organizer; historical development:initiative-org-sdap-1955 as organizer; historical development:initiative-org-sdap-1955 as organizer; knowledge “Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)的持续运动不是”; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

  _effects: knowledge “我亲历或知晓「Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)”; knowledge “我亲历或知晓「Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)”; knowledge “我亲历或知晓「Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)”; knowledge “我亲历或知晓「Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)”; knowledge “我亲历或知晓「Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)”_

- **1955.01** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Katharina Moser把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Klara去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05000000000000001 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1955.04** `oj18-taxi.night-fare::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/coop-keys` tags: taxi, night, driver, passenger, work, odd_job, everyday, vienna
  合作社的调度Elisabeth Huber把一枚铜牌和一把车钥匙推过柜台，说夜班只跑环城路，十一点以后不接去普拉特的车。Klara把铜牌挂在后视镜上，第一晚就拉了七个客人，最后一个在车上睡着，打鼾像一台小马达。
  _effects: organization_found 第二区夜班出租合作社 (other); position 夜班司机 @taxi:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:022197; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:1685; cash 2 (night taxi fares); flag +taxi_night_shift; knowledge “夜班只跑环城路，十一点以后不接去普拉特的车。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1955.06** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Klara的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Klara家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  报纸的社会版把Klara家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.public true -> true; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 4; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +windfall_went_public_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:022250_

  木盆边的水渍映着天光，Klara跪在石阶上揉搓床单，肘弯发酸。晾绳上的湿布滴答作响，Katharina Weiss探身把自家被单也挂上去，两人没多说话。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000004 (长时间浸在碱水里，手部皮肤开裂); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1956.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:021354_

- **1956.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/resigned` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  Anton Hofer在门口堵了三天，第四天把Klara的辞职信全文登了出来。信只有四行，最后一行是“为避免机构继续为我的判断付出代价”。Klara把办公室的钥匙交给Gustav Lechner 2，没有带走任何文件。
  _effects: position_end 公共礼仪官 @org:absurd15-wish-office:evt:world-vienna-1912:006255; reputation -0.07999999999999996; mood -0.020000000000000018; condition; flag +resigned_over_scandal; knowledge “辞职信被全文刊登，接任者当天就搬进了办公室。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-123_

  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Klara把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Klara来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 4 -> 5; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  巡查簿在换班时不见了，第二天却出现在煤筐底下，最后几页被水泡得发皱。管事的说签字不能算数，扣了Klara半个月的夜班钱；Klara没有争，只在新的簿子第一页把日期写得很大。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 14; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 45; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +odd_job_scare_

### 11 subject events

- **1912.11** `p22-strange-doctor-prediction::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-doctor-is-optimistic` tags: infancy, family, doctor, illness, omen, strange, dark, drama
  医生说孩子只是瘦，开了一瓶味道很坏的补药，说“过了春天就好了”。Rosa Lechner把这句话记了很多年；每年春天，家里都要说一遍，像在核对一张旧账单，药瓶上还贴着手写的标签。
  _effects: mood 0.03; health 0.010000000000000009 (a tonic and a hopeful sentence); state_add p22-child-omen state:world-vienna-1912:13; flag +doctor_optimism_

- **1912.11** `state_reaction:p22-child-omen/a-stranger-touches-the-door` tags: p22-child-omen, child_omen, omen, strangeness, attention, protection, fear, superstition
  一个陌生人把手按在Mitzi家的门框上，说这样能把坏运气留在门外。大人没有拦，只等那人走远，才用湿布把门框擦了一遍，布上留下一点灰，门框上留下一道浅印。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-child-omen.attention 35 -> 40; state_parameter p22-child-omen.fear 20 -> 24; state_parameter p22-child-omen.protection 45 -> 47; mood -0.01_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000087_

- **1913.01** `state_tick:p22-child-omen/the-omen-returns/the-coincidence-repeats` tags: p22-child-omen, child_omen, omen, strangeness, attention, protection, fear, superstition
  又过了一年，同一件小事又发生了：钟停在同一个时刻，门口的狗对着空处叫。Mitzi正在学系鞋带，抬头问“又来了吗”；大人说没有，手却先去摸门框，谁也没有回答。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-child-omen.strangeness 40 -> 45; state_parameter p22-child-omen.attention 40 -> 44; state_parameter p22-child-omen.fear 24 -> 27; mood -0.01_

- **1913.02** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-nonsense` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Mitzi说了一长串谁也听不懂的话，最后用力点头，像是在宣布一件已经决定的事。Rosa Lechner认真听完，也点了点头；孩子满意地爬走了，留下大人们互相看着，忍不住笑。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; skill_

- **1913.04** `vienna-infant-household.croup/steam-kettle` tags: infancy, health, family, home
  Alois Lechner把铜壶烧开，提起壶嘴对着床头旧毛巾罩出的布篷，让蒸汽灌进去。Mitzi的犬吠样咳嗽慢慢松下来，天亮时睡着。Alois Lechner用掉一整天份的煤，手背被蒸汽烫红一片。
  _effects: health 0.021666666666666723 (蒸汽缓解了喉部痉挛); cash -1 (多烧了一天的煤)_

- **1913.10** `p22-warm-anonymous-gifts::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-giver-is-watched` tags: infancy, family, gift, poverty, neighbor, warm, mystery, drama
  邻居说，看见一个年轻女人在门口站了很久，手里提着东西，最后没有敲门。Alois Lechner追出去时，街上只剩卖栗子的推车；那天晚饭多了一小袋栗子，谁也没有问是谁买的。
  _effects: knowledge “匿名送东西的人可能是一个年轻女人。”; mood -0.010000000000000002; flag +anonymous_giver_seen; cash 2 (a bag of chestnuts)_

- **1913.12** `p22-warm-fathers-letter::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-letter-is-not-opened` tags: infancy, family, letter, uncle, warm, melancholy, memory, drama
  Alois Lechner把信塞进外套衬里，说等忙完再看。可那件外套整个冬天都挂在门后，袖口沾着印刷厂的墨；Mitzi会爬以后，最先够到的就是那只露出来的信封角，纸边已经被摸得发软。
  _effects: state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:33; flag +unopened_letter; mood -0.02; knowledge “门后那件外套里有一封没有拆开的信。”_

- **1913.12** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又有人问起Mitzi家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 55 -> 59; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 40 -> 42; mood 0.01_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000235_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-birth-letter-from-the-dead/the-letter-is-a-bill` tags: infancy, family, secret, letter, drama
  信里没有问候，只有一张旧账单：棺材、墓地、一趟从边境回来的车费。Alois Lechner把账单翻过来，背面用铅笔写着“别让孩子知道”。Mitzi伸手去抓纸角，Alois Lechner没有让。
  _effects: cash -4 (paid an old funeral debt（其中4克朗记入债务）); flag +family_debt_letter; mood -0.03_

- **1914.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/told-at-the-table` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，Mitzi家的故事在饭桌上换了新的讲法：多了一句笑话，少了一处哭声。听的人把汤匙放下，等下一句；讲故事的人却先去看窗子有没有关好，谁也没有先动筷子。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 42 -> 45; state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 59 -> 57; state_parameter p22-family-legend.promise 45 -> 47; mood 0.01_

- **1915.01** `statistical_death/accident` tags: death
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang附近出了事故。工友和路人把人抬到门口，等医生赶到时已经没有了呼吸。Franz Lechner在场。
  _effects: death (意外事故)_

### 8 subject events

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/colic-night` tags: infancy, family, first, warm, injury
  夜里Josef哭了很久，Josef Bauer抱着孩子在屋里来回走，数着地板缝。天快亮时孩子终于打了个嗝，安静下来；Josef Bauer坐在床沿，发现自己还保持着走路的姿势。
  _effects: mood -0.03; health -0.010000000000000009 (lost sleep)_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-birth-letter-from-the-dead/the-letter-is-a-bill` tags: infancy, family, secret, letter, drama
  信里没有问候，只有一张旧账单：棺材、墓地、一趟从边境回来的车费。Josef Bauer把账单翻过来，背面用铅笔写着“别让孩子知道”。Josef伸手去抓纸角，Josef Bauer没有让。
  _effects: cash -4 (paid an old funeral debt); flag +family_debt_letter; mood -0.03_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/lost-relative` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josef Bauer在收拾箱子时翻出一张没有署名的照片，说那是家里一个“去了很远地方”的人。Josef问还回不回来，Josef Bauer把照片翻过去，说先把名字记住，别在饭桌上问。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一张没有署名的照片，照片上的人去了很远的地方。”; flag +family_legend_photo; mood -0.01_

- **1916.02** `vienna-life-child-inherited-wish/the-dead-grandmothers-wish` tags: childhood, family, wish, grief, uncanny, drama
  Josef在饭桌上忽然说：“等我长大了，要把那间屋子买回来。”Josef Bauer的勺子停在半空，因为这句话是去世多年的外婆常说的，连“那间屋子”都指得一模一样。Josef说完就低头喝汤，好像只是随口。
  _effects: flag +inherited_wish_house; knowledge “孩子说要把“那间屋子”买回来，和去世的外婆说过的一模一样。”; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1916.02** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/imaginary-friend` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  院子里没有别的孩子，Josef给墙上的裂纹起了一个名字，每天吃饭时给它留一个位置。家里的人没有纠正，只在摆碗时多放一只空碗；Klara从门口经过，问Josef在等谁。
  _effects: skill; flag +imaginary_friend; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1917.02** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-mother-call` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  窗户上的玻璃响了一下，有人探出头，让Josef立刻回家。铁环还在Mitzi Lechner脚边，Josef蹲下去抽，抽不动。Mitzi Lechner说除非明天把铁钩带来。Josef点头，Mitzi Lechner把脚一抬，铁环滚到Josef手边，上面沾了一层鞋底灰。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.019999999999999997_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Maria Bauer哭得打嗝，手指一直朝井台方向指。Josef蹲下把Maria Bauer驮上背，沿着墙根走，数砖缝给Maria Bauer听：一、二、三……数到第四圈，背上的呼吸才匀了。天黑时Josef的胳膊麻得抬不过肩，Josef Bauer回来接过孩子，Josef扶着门框甩了好一会儿手。
  _effects: skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000675:child_

- **1919.01** `statistical_death/other` tags: death
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang突发急病，高热和虚弱持续了几天。医生没能给出明确诊断，Josef在一个夜里去世。Anna Bauer在场。
  _effects: death (急性疾病)_

### 24 subject events

- **1912.02** `vienna-life-infant-feeding/fed-enough` tags: infancy, food, household
  Rosa Lechner在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg给Rosa喂奶，随后把洗净的布尿片挂在窗边。孩子吃饱后睡着了，锅里还留着半碗燕麦糊。
  _effects: cash -2 (milk and oatmeal)_

- **1912.06** `iw18-infancy.ceiling-shadows::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-coat-is-just-a-coat` tags: infancy, night, wonder, gaslight, fear, family, rural, agriculture
  Alois Lechner把Rosa抱起来，走到衣钩下面，让孩子伸手去抓那件旧大衣。粗呢的袖口擦过掌心，Rosa先愣住，随后皱着鼻子哭了一声。Alois Lechner笑着把大衣掀开，说“看，只有衣服”，灯影也跟着晃了一下。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +learned_the_coat_rack_

- **1912.07** `vienna-life-birth-letter-from-the-dead/the-letter-is-real` tags: infancy, family, secret, letter, drama
  邮差把一封边角磨白的信塞进门缝，收信人是一个已经死了三年的名字。Alois Lechner把Rosa放在膝上，拆开信，里面只有一句：“孩子像不像他？”Rosa Lechner在门口探头，问是不是寄错了；Alois Lechner把信折回去，说“没有寄错”。
  _effects: flag +letter_from_dead_relative; knowledge “一封写给死去亲人的信问：孩子像不像他。”; mood -0.04_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-toddler-wrong-cradle/the-blanket-is-wrong` tags: infancy, family, rumour, identity, comic, drama
  Emil Weiss盯着Rosa的摇篮看了很久，说这床蓝被子不是这家人的。Rosa Lechner把被子翻过来，角落绣着一个陌生的姓；Rosa却抓着被角不放，像是认得它。Emil Weiss退后一步，说“我什么都没说”。
  _effects: flag +wrong_cradle_blanket; knowledge “摇篮里的蓝被子绣着一个陌生的姓。”; mood -0.03_

- **1913.06** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-dead-grandmothers-name` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban
  Rosa扶着桌沿站起来，指着门口的空处，清清楚楚叫出一个名字。屋里没人教过这个词；Alois Lechner手里的铝勺停在半空。那是去年冬天埋掉的老人的名字，窗外正好有送煤的车经过，铁轮压过石缝。
  _effects: mood -0.03; knowledge “孩子说出的第一个词，是去年死去的祖母的名字。”; flag +first_word_dead_name_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-more` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Rosa把空碗推回Alois Lechner面前，清清楚楚地说了一个字：“还要。”Alois Lechner愣住，随后把锅底最后一点糊刮进碗里。孩子学会了用一句话让世界再转一圈。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.03_

- **1914.02** `vienna-life-toddler-accident/safe-in-the-doorway` tags: infancy, accident, family, danger
  Alois Lechner在最后一刻抓住了Rosa的后领。孩子悬在两级台阶之间，鞋底蹭着地，手里还攥着那只线轴。Alois Lechner把孩子放回地上，蹲下来看了很久，才说：“再跑，我就把门关上。”
  _effects: mood -0.019999999999999997; skill_

- **1915.06** `p9-childhood-household.name-day-gift/wooden-horse` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  Alois Lechner把报纸包推到Rosa面前，包得紧，Rosa拆到第三层才看见木头。木马只有巴掌大，四条腿磨得圆滑，鬃毛是一刀一刀刻出来的。Rosa把它揣进衣襟，上楼时用手护着，晚上放在枕头边，袖口蹭了一道黑印。
  _effects: mood 0.08; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/stolen-inheritance` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Alois Lechner说，家里本来有一件东西，后来被“更会说话的人”拿走了。Rosa问是什么，Alois Lechner只说是一块表，表盖里刻着姓。孩子记住了“表盖里刻着姓”，却没有记住那个姓。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一块表盖里刻着姓的表，被一个更会说话的人拿走了。”; flag +family_legend_watch_

- **1916.02** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/stolen-marble` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Emil把Rosa的玻璃弹珠藏进鞋里，被当场看见。Emil说只是“替你拿着”，Rosa没有揭穿，也没有再拿出第二颗。两个人蹲在墙根，把一只蚂蚁看了很久，谁也没有先回家。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood -0.01_

- **1916.02** `vienna-life-child-inherited-wish/the-child-wants-a-dog` tags: childhood, family, wish, grief, uncanny, drama
  Rosa说那句话只是想要一只狗，而且要“会看门的、不咬人的、像外婆家那只一样”。Alois Lechner松了一口气，又有点失落；那天下午，两个人用旧毛线给一只并不存在的狗量了脖围。
  _effects: mood 0.04; skill_

- **1917.11** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/hospital-deposit` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  围巾从Rosa的头一直裹到脚，Rosa Lechner抱着人下楼，在电车踏板上差点滑倒。急诊窗口先收押金，Rosa Lechner把内袋里的票子一张张抹平，数出六克朗。Rosa躺在观察室的窄床上，头顶的灯管嗡嗡响，Rosa Lechner的手一直按着被角。
  _effects: cash -6 (医院急诊押金和电车票（其中6克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (在医院接受处理); mood -0.039999999999999994_

- **1918.01** `vienna-life-school-enrolment/kept-home` tags: childhood, school, fees
  Rosa Lechner在报名处门口站了一会儿，最后把Rosa带回家。家里需要有人看小妹妹，学校可以明年再去。Friedrich Lechner在名册上把名字划掉了。
  _effects: education home; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Friedrich Hofer 2把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Rosa伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Friedrich Hofer 2从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Rosa手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Rosa一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.school-slate/slate-homemade-wood-ash` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, supplies, education
  Alois Lechner把空口袋翻给Rosa看，里面只有线头和一枚扣子。Rosa没再要，去木料堆挑了块没毛刺的薄板，用指头抹上湿煤灰，写一个字，抹一次。到Leopoldstädter Volksschule时木板边角已经把书包染黑，后排有人笑了一声，Rosa把木板往桌肚里塞了塞。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.04; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1919.05** `p9-childhood-household.name-day-gift/passed-gift-to-younger` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  Rosa把木马推给弟妹，说“这个给你”，自己翻开那本旧图画书。纸页发黄，角上留着别人的铅笔字。Alois Lechner问真的不要木马吗，Rosa点头，眼睛没离开书页。此后几天，Rosa一有空就翻那本书，连书脊都摸得发亮。
  _effects: relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood 0.04; flag +likes_reading_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Maria Lechner的鞋掉在井台边，Rosa先去捡鞋，回头才看见Maria Lechner摔在湿石头上。Anton Moser 2用围裙裹住Maria Lechner，血从膝盖渗出来。Alois Lechner夜里把Rosa叫到灶台前，数落了很久，最后把锅里剩下的半勺汤倒回大锅，只给Rosa留了半碗。
  _effects: mood -0.06; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1920.10** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Rosa跑到第二圈时鞋带松了，索性踩着鞋后跟跑。最后一圈，Rosa从床单底下钻过去，先拍到墙。Josef Bauer喘着气，把两颗弹珠拍进Rosa手心。Rosa低头才发现外套少了一颗扣子；窗口的Josef Bauer扔下一根别针，说先别着。
  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1921.01** `vienna-life-child-labour/folds-notices` tags: childhood, labour, piecework
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一捆教区通告交给Rosa Lechner带回家。Rosa放学后在厨房桌上折页到夜里，手指沾满油墨，两周后交回六千张，拿到三克朗二十赫勒；钱交给Rosa Lechner时，Rosa的指尖已经洗不干净，指甲缝里留着一圈黑；家里从此多了一个能挣几个铜板的孩子。
  _effects: cash 3.2 (folding parish notices); condition; role_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-half-credit` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  “月底一起结。”掌柜说完，把半筐煤推到柜台外。Rosa用胸口顶住筐沿往家挪，煤灰落进领口，手心被麻绳磨得发亮。Rosa Lechner在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口等到人，先看了看筐底，再看了看Rosa的手，把漏掉的碎渣扫进簸箕。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1921.08** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-confiscated` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Rosa把书压在算术本底下，只露出书脊。Elisabeth Stein走过来，抽走算术本，书也跟着滑出来。折角那页画着一艘帆船。Elisabeth Stein合上书，说放学后擦黑板，擦一星期；书被锁进讲台抽屉，没往校长室送。
  _effects: mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1921.10** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-bullied` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  市场拱廊下晒不到太阳，Rosa却被堵出一头汗。带子越挣越紧，肩膀在柱子上磕了一下。Anton从人群里挤过来，先看了看Rosa的肩，又看了看柱子上的结，拿出小刀割断。两个大孩子骂了一句，Anton把Rosa拉到菜篮后面。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.05; condition_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Rosa拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Mitzi Lechner弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Rosa接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner_

- **1923.01** `statistical_death/violence` tags: death
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang卷入一场冲突，头部和胸口受了重伤。旁人把人抬回家，夜里伤势恶化。Mitzi Lechner在场。
  _effects: death (暴力)_

### 244 subject events

- **1912.05** `p22-strange-cradle-secret::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-ticket-is-redeemed` tags: infancy, family, cradle, secret, inheritance, strange, dark, drama
  Josef Bauer拿着当票去赎，取回一把银勺子，勺柄上刻着一个看不清的字母。回家后把勺子塞进Emil手里，说“用吧”；孩子敲着桌沿，勺子发出很轻的响声，像在回答什么。
  _effects: cash -5 (redeeming the pawn ticket); health 0.010000000000000009 (a silver spoon to cut the first teeth); state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:8; flag +spoon_returned_

- **1912.12** `p22-warm-fathers-letter::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-recipe-arrives` tags: infancy, family, letter, uncle, warm, melancholy, memory, drama
  信是叔叔死前写的，在邮袋里压了两年才到。Josef Bauer拆开时先笑出声：里面没有遗言，只有一张李子饺子的做法和一个关于祖母的笨笑话。那天晚上，全家真的吃了饺子。
  _effects: state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:7; mood 0.04000000000000001; knowledge “家里有一封叔叔留下的信，里面写着李子饺子的做法。”_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/colic-night` tags: infancy, family, first, warm, injury
  夜里Emil哭了很久，Josef Bauer抱着孩子在屋里来回走，数着地板缝。天快亮时孩子终于打了个嗝，安静下来；Josef Bauer坐在床沿，发现自己还保持着走路的姿势。
  _effects: mood -0.03; health -0.010000000000000009 (lost sleep)_

- **1913.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/a-detail-goes-missing` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，故事里最要紧的那件东西不见了：先是名字，后来是地点。Emil问起时，大人说“你记错了”；可柜子最底层那只空盒子，谁也没有扔，锁却换过了，钥匙也不见了。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 60 -> 64; state_parameter p22-family-legend.danger 25 -> 28; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 55 -> 53; mood -0.01_

- **1913.02** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-more` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Emil把空碗推回Josef Bauer面前，清清楚楚地说了一个字：“还要。”Josef Bauer愣住，随后把锅底最后一点糊刮进碗里。孩子学会了用一句话让世界再转一圈。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.03_

- **1913.02** `p22-strange-cradle-secret::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-neighbour-knows` tags: infancy, family, cradle, secret, inheritance, strange, dark, drama
  邻居说，这只摇篮是从另一户人家借来的，那家的孩子没活过第一个冬天。Josef Bauer想把它劈了烧火，斧子举起来又放下，最后靠在门后；那天夜里，孩子还是睡在里面。
  _effects: mood -0.039999999999999994; state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:30; flag +cradle_history_

- **1913.12** `statistical_infection`
  Emil在Bauer-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1913.12** `vienna-life-toddler-wrong-cradle/the-blanket-is-wrong` tags: infancy, family, rumour, identity, comic, drama
  Anna Moser盯着Emil的摇篮看了很久，说这床蓝被子不是这家人的。Josef Bauer把被子翻过来，角落绣着一个陌生的姓；Emil却抓着被角不放，像是认得它。Anna Moser退后一步，说“我什么都没说”。
  _effects: flag +wrong_cradle_blanket; knowledge “摇篮里的蓝被子绣着一个陌生的姓。”; mood -0.03_

- **1913.12** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又有人问起Emil家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 70 -> 74; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 25 -> 27; mood 0.01_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000221_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-birth-letter-from-the-dead/the-letter-is-real` tags: infancy, family, secret, letter, drama
  邮差把一封边角磨白的信塞进门缝，收信人是一个已经死了三年的名字。Josef Bauer把Emil放在膝上，拆开信，里面只有一句：“孩子像不像他？”Emil Weiss在门口探头，问是不是寄错了；Josef Bauer把信折回去，说“没有寄错”。
  _effects: flag +letter_from_dead_relative; knowledge “一封写给死去亲人的信问：孩子像不像他。”; mood -0.04_

- **1914.01** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又有人问起Emil家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 64 -> 68; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 53 -> 55; mood 0.01_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000218_

- **1914.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/a-detail-goes-missing` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，故事里最要紧的那件东西不见了：先是名字，后来是地点。Emil问起时，大人说“你记错了”；可柜子最底层那只空盒子，谁也没有扔，锁却换过了，钥匙也不见了。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 74 -> 78; state_parameter p22-family-legend.danger 65 -> 68; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 27 -> 25; mood -0.01_

- **1914.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Emil的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1914.06** `statistical_recovery`
  Emil熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1915.02** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  楼梯间的灯罩空着，第三级踏板比别处高。Emil一脚踩空，滚到平台，右小臂磕在铁扶手的弯头上。Josef Bauer听见哭声跑出来，把Emil抱回屋，用冷水毛巾敷那块青紫，再用旧布条缠了两圈。夜里Emil哭醒两次。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.05000000000000002_

- **1916.01** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又有人问起Emil家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 78 -> 82; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 25 -> 27; mood 0.01_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000533_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/happy-legend` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josef Bauer讲起自己小时候在乡下追一只白鹅，追到河边，鹅没追到，倒捡回一篮鸭蛋。Emil笑得从凳子上滑下来，Josef Bauer也跟着笑，说这个家不是只有欠账和葬礼。
  _effects: mood 0.04; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1916.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/told-at-the-table` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，Emil家的故事在饭桌上换了新的讲法：多了一句笑话，少了一处哭声。听的人把汤匙放下，等下一句；讲故事的人却先去看窗子有没有关好，谁也没有先动筷子。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 27 -> 30; state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 82 -> 80; state_parameter p22-family-legend.promise 30 -> 32; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1916.02** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/shared-toy` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Anton在楼道里滚一只铁环，Emil站在门口看。Anton把铁环推过来，说“你推一次，我推一次”。两个人从楼梯口推到院子里，铁环倒下的声音把午睡的大人吵醒了一排。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.05_

- **1916.02** `vienna-life-child-inherited-wish/the-child-wants-a-dog` tags: childhood, family, wish, grief, uncanny, drama
  Emil说那句话只是想要一只狗，而且要“会看门的、不咬人的、像外婆家那只一样”。Josef Bauer松了一口气，又有点失落；那天下午，两个人用旧毛线给一只并不存在的狗量了脖围。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; skill_

- **1917.02** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-mother-call` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  窗户上的玻璃响了一下，有人探出头，让Emil立刻回家。铁环还在Mitzi Lechner脚边，Emil蹲下去抽，抽不动。Mitzi Lechner说除非明天把铁钩带来。Emil点头，Mitzi Lechner把脚一抬，铁环滚到Emil手边，上面沾了一层鞋底灰。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1917.02** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-scarlet` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷子里一家接一家地病倒。Emil烧到说胡话，Josef Bauer把阁楼小屋腾出来，在门把手上系了块红布。医生隔着门看了Emil的喉咙，留下药粉，收下一克朗二十赫勒。Josef Bauer把家里留着买煤的钱数了两遍，刚好够。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (付医生诊费); condition_

- **1918.01** `state_expire:p22-family-legend` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  Emil的「家族传说」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:30_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Helene Hofer家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Emil递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Helene Hofer端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Emil把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  土豆滚进墙角的煤灰里，Emil弯腰去追，再抬头时Maria Bauer已经坐在井台边，裤腿湿了一片，哭声一抽一抽。Karl Kraus把孩子抱进灶间，Emil跟在后面，手指抠着门框。Josef Bauer回来没问经过，只把Emil的碗收走一半，说“看个人都看不住”。
  _effects: health -0.03 (在井台边摔了一跤); mood -0.06; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1919.02** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Emil想从床单底下钻过去，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在石板棱上。Helene Hofer听见哭声出来，先看了看伤口，又回去端水。手帕扎得很紧，Emil试着站起来，被Helene Hofer按回台阶上。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1919.08** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-lose` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  煤车刚过去，路心还留着几块碎煤。Emil的铁环撞上去，弹到水沟边。Lena先一步把铁环踩住，说要拿一颗石弹子来换。Emil蹲下去掏口袋，膝盖在石板棱上蹭掉一块皮，换回铁环时裤腿已经渗出血点；Emil没再争，只记住Lena踩铁环的那只脚。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.04; condition_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Hedwig Stein把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Emil伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Hedwig Stein从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Emil手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Emil一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-hedwig-stein; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  河水那边传来同伴的笑声，Emil站在院门口听了听，还是回去把木盆扶正。Mitzi Bauer睡醒哭了一声，Emil把最后半块面包掰碎泡进温水，一勺一勺喂。Josef Bauer回来时，Emil正蹲在盆边打瞌睡，手里还攥着喂饭的木勺。
  _effects: mood -0.019999999999999997; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000837:child; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1921.07** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳上的水一滴一滴落在石板上。Emil跑到第二圈，鞋底一滑，膝盖先着地。Emil Weiss从屋里端出半盆清水，蹲下冲掉伤口里的沙，又用手帕扎紧，说今天别跑了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Josefine Hofer把Emil拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Emil把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Emil蹲下把两包并排，按Josefine Hofer说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; skill_

- **1922.05** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  书在Emil的书包里藏了三天，课间才敢翻两页。还书那天，Emil把书角淋皱的事先说了，又把书页一张张抚平。Anna Bauer翻到最后一页，看了看书脊，说下次别带进院子，书却还是借给了Emil。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

- **1922.09** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Emil每天放学都在柜台前站一会儿，看玻璃板底下压着的烟画，顺手把歪了的报纸摞齐。Klara擦完柜台，从抽屉最里面摸出一张揉皱的风景卡，边角还沾着烟丝，塞过来时说了句“别挡着门”。Emil把卡夹进课本，第二天带了一张重复的来给Klara看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Berta Weiss把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Emil伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Berta Weiss从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Emil手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Emil一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.02; flag +wish:love:pursuing_

- **1924.02** `p9-childhood-adolescence.church-club/club-sign-up-hike` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Emil把十赫勒放在桌上的铁皮盒里，翻到歌本最后一页，用铅笔写下自己的名字。Mitzi Lechner在后面戳Emil的背，说乡下的草地能躺着听云。
  _effects: cash -10 (青年会远足车费); relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.03_

- **1924.02** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/coins-into-box` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Anna Bauer的箱子摊在床板上，里面是两件衬衫、一双旧靴和母亲塞的一小袋面粉。Emil把攒了半年的零钱数到第三遍，一共一克朗二十赫勒，全塞进箱角的袜子里。Anna Bauer看见了，没说话，只把箱子扣上，铜扣响了一声。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (把攒下的零钱塞给离家的兄姐); relation kin with person-anna-bauer; mood -0.03_

- **1924.02** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Emil在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Emil用牙咬断，重新来。Josef Bauer坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Emil把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1925.01** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/enroll-night-class` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Helene Winter 2在黑板上写下课程表和书本费：一学期三克朗，另加六十赫勒杂费。Emil下工后啃着冷土豆赶到教室，坐到公共长桌最靠煤气灯的一头。手指上还留着白天的油污，握笔时在本子上蹭出灰印，但座位号已经登记在册。
  _effects: cash -3.6 (夜校学费与书本杂费); education secondary; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1925.10** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-rejected` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  “太小，手也薄。”Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说完，把Emil推过的木板翻过来，指着上面一道啃痕。Josef Bauer把布包往肩上提了提，说打扰了。Emil在门口多站了一会儿，直到里面的刨子声把话头盖过去，才跟着Josef Bauer下楼。
  _effects: mood -0.08; reputation -0.05; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/went-once-and-left` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Emil交了第一个月的会费，进去坐了一刻钟。屋里太吵，下棋的争论盖过了念书的声音，他借的书也被人翻得卷了边。下个月他路过门口，没再进去交十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (只交了一个月会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; flag +workers_youth_quit; mood -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1926.10** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Emil把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Josef Bauer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Emil那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1927.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/kept-at-home` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship
  家里算了一笔账：学徒工钱扣掉车费和饭钱，剩不下多少。Emil留在家里接缝纫店的零活，顺便照看弟弟妹妹。
  _effects: role; cash 2 (home piecework)_

- **1927.05** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/give-up-for-overtime` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  工头说这个月赶印一批账簿，晚上加班每小时多给二十赫勒。Emil站在教室门口听完课程表，转身回厂里搬纸。Grete Stein追出来说座位可以保留两周，Emil点点头，手已经摸上了作坊的门把，夜里机器的声音把课本的事盖了过去。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (夜间加班多挣的工钱); skill; mood -0.039999999999999994_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/went-once-and-left` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Emil交了第一个月的会费，进去坐了一刻钟。屋里太吵，下棋的争论盖过了念书的声音，他借的书也被人翻得卷了边。下个月他路过门口，没再进去交十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (只交了一个月会费); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1928.08** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-rejected` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne捏了捏Emil的虎口，又让Emil张开手指，说骨节还没长开，推硬木会伤手。Josef Bauer想再问一句，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne已经转身去搬木料。Emil退到门槛外，听见刨子重新响起来，木花从门缝里卷到脚边。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; reputation -0.05; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Emil和Alois Lechner 2在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Alois Lechner 2在门后放了一根木棍，Emil把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; mood 0.04000000000000001; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1930.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.volksbibliothek` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Emil听Maria Hofer报出押金数目，把伸进内袋的手又抽了回来。问清免费阅览处的位置后，Emil穿过两条街，在 Volksbibliothek 的阅览室门口登记了姓名，坐在长桌边读了一下午；一个赫勒没花，只是不能把书带回家，天黑前就得还回去。
  _effects: mood 0.03; skill_

- **1930.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Luise Weiss 2一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Luise Weiss 2的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1930.02** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil把通知念给Luise Kral听，Luise Kral摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Emil去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.03_

- **1930.02** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  楼梯间只点一根蜡烛。Luise Weiss 2凑过来低声说，楼下老寡妇的喘病又重了，教区明早才能派修女来。Emil把汤碗接过去，碗底还温着。
  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1930.02** `state_reaction:wish-love/love-betrayal` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_reaction
  Emil反复回想最后一次见面。对方说过的话被拆成很多小块，每一块都能扎人。
  _effects: mood -0.06_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004070_

- **1930.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Emil说胡话时念着车间的排班表，Franz Lechner一句句应着，拿小勺往嘴里送温水。床头那半瓶退烧药早见了底，她没舍得倒最后一滴，留着天亮再说。
  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-franz-lechner_

- **1930.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Grete Huber说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Grete Huber陪着Emil去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; mood -0.09999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1930.12** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/shifts-with-sibling` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Emil和Klara把值班时辰写在门后的粉笔板上：Klara守前半夜，Emil守后半夜，医生说静养二十一天。煤油灯芯拧小，药粉分成小包摆在床头，便盆换水时尽量不碰出响。二十一天熬下来，Emil的眼皮肿了，走路发飘，但Josef Bauer每次起身总有人扶。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; flag +night_care_duty_

- **1931.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Luise Weiss 2抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Emil把床单对折，腾出一半，Luise Weiss 2用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; mood 0.01999999999999999_

  Emil把两克朗月费和徽章钱一起放在啤酒渍斑斑的桌上，Josef Bauer在名册上添了一行。钢琴缺的那个音在排练中途又卡住，Emil站在第二排跟着哼，声音被两边的人盖住，胸腔里却震得发麻；散场时能把副歌完整唱下来，也记住了怎么在人群里找准自己的声部。

  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用（其中3克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.02999999999999997_

  _effects: mood 0.06; cash -0.03 (买了三赫勒的糖（其中0.03克朗记入债务）)_

  你把已经掏出来的赫勒又放回口袋，说不赊了。老板哼了一声，把账本往柜台上一合。你拎着空筐跟邻居往火车站方向走，在货运站边的煤渣堆里捡了半袋碎煤和焦炭。手指冻得发僵，指甲缝里全是黑灰。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

  _effects: cash 10 (友人借给搬家应急的十克朗); relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金); health 0.05999999999999999 (住院处窗口的押金); condition; flag +hospitalized_third_class; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1934.05** `r14-soc.courtyard-rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/slapped-in-the-courtyard` tags: rumor, reputation, neighbor_conflict, shame, gender, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil在院子里拦住Luise Kral，让对方把话再说一遍。Luise Kral笑着重复，Emil的手先落下去。晾衣绳上的床单被扯下来，盖住了一只煤桶。Emil Winter尖叫着去叫看门人，孩子们趴在每一扇窗后面看。看门人赶来时，Luise Kral已经退到煤堆后面，脸上留着一道红印。看门人把两人分开，谁也没有去捡那条床单。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999; reputation -0.08; condition; flag +slapped_rumor_monger; relation rival with person-luise-kral_

  Emil看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Anton Weiss追出来问怎么不领了，Emil说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Emil把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; food_

  药房的后门开着，一个穿军大衣的人按包卖药：退烧粉、止血粉、止痛粉。Emil买了三包，回家发现其中一包是面粉；再去找人，后门已经锁了。他把那包面粉倒进碗里，又倒回纸袋，没舍得扔。

  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Elisabeth Weiss的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1937.01** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/signed-for-a-favor` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  Marie Huber在签字前把一张剧院包厢的票根留在桌上，说是“给孩子的”。Emil把票根推回去，却在许可证上签了名；排水图仍缺一页，但窗口当天就盖了章。扩建开工后，第一场雨把后院淹了半尺。
  _effects: relation friend with person-marie-huber; flag +permit_for_favor; knowledge “许可证少了一页排水图，盖章时没有补。”; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1937.01** `p10-family-tasks.arrange-childcare/arrange-childcare.sister` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, childcare
  姐姐把孩子裹在披肩里抱上电车，说城外有院子，一个月就送回来。Emil站在站台上看车走远。一个月后孩子会叫人了，可看见Emil就往姐姐身后躲。Emil把孩子抱进屋，他哭了半宿。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1937.04** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Emil走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.010000000000000009 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1937.10** `ml18-tax.audit-of-the-windfall::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hide-the-ledger` tags: tax, legal, money, windfall, bureaucracy, comic, vienna, urban
  Franz Huber 2把账本塞进钢琴后面的夹层，说等风声过去再拿出来。Gustav Lechner 2第二次来时带了一个记录员和一张搜查令；钢琴被挪开时，琴键响了一串不成调的音。Emil看见黑皮本翻开的那一页上，已经用红笔圈出了三笔利息。
  _effects: cash -10 (the accountant's fee for hiding the ledger); state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:464; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; flag +tax_ledger_hidden; knowledge “黑皮本上被红笔圈出的三笔利息都没有报税。”; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938, injury
  Emil在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Franz Huber 2记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Emil带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: health -0.12 (beaten in police custody); condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

- **1938.01** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/motion-fails` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  会议室里有人念了一份动议，说Emil把预算当成人情。Franz Huber 2把签名页传了一圈，最后停在自己手里：支持票比反对票少三张。Emil没有发言，只把下一季度的支出表放在桌上，表上每一笔都有收据。
  _effects: reputation 0.04000000000000001; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; flag +survived_removal_motion; knowledge “动议只拿到少数支持，三名原本签名的委员在投票前撤回了。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  房东把房租涨了一截，Emil在同一个星期里丢了工。夜里他把行李重新捆好，发现自己已经能听懂搬家公司问的那句德语，却还不足以跟人争一份租约；第二天清早，他把钥匙放在门房，没有回头。

- **1938.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/helps_fetch_doctor` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  隔壁Elisabeth Kral的小儿子烧到说胡话，额头烫得能烘手。Elisabeth Kral在楼道里抹眼泪，说请不起医生。Emil下楼叫了街角药房的伙计，花四十赫勒买了一包退烧药粉，又帮着用湿布敷了一整夜。天亮时孩子退了烧，Elisabeth Kral端来一碗热汤面，面底下卧着一个鸡蛋。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买退烧药粉); health -0.020000000000000018 (熬夜照料病人); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

  窗子推开只有一线天光，墙纸边角翘起，Emil用指甲一刮便掉下一层湿粉。看门人揣好定金，说这间是朝北的，冬天火炉要添得勤些。Mitzi Lechner站在楼梯口没进屋，只替Emil看着箱子。
  _effects: cash -4 (当场付下定金和钥匙钱); residence; health -0.030000000000000027 (在新屋里待久了关节发凉)_

  Emil只送来一罐杏子果酱，没有进门，把罐子放在门槛里边，说了句汤你们先喝。Klara在屋里听见了，走到门口时人已经下楼。果酱罐上没有标签，只裹着一层报纸，瓶口用蜡封着；这一罐甜食抵得上一克朗的吃食，却没能把两个人重新按到一张桌上。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Otto Stein攥着Emil的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08000000000000002 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

  Gustav Lechner 2没有捂鼻子，反而用撬棍敲了敲棺材底板。声音太实，他又敲了一下；Elisabeth Lechner 3转身就跑，Emil被留在原地。棺材打开时，卷烟码得整整齐齐，白布下面连个人形都没有。Gustav Lechner 2把Emil的名字写在封条背面。
  _effects: cash -10 (棺材夹层里的卷烟); condition; flag +arrested_with_contraband_coffin; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106; knowledge “the porter ran and left the subject at the checkpoint”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

  衣帽间的门打开以后，先掉出来的是一叠催缴单。Franz Huber 2把数目加了两遍，说遗产税、欠租和一位“贝尔格夫人”的年金都挂在房子上；Otto Stein退后一步，Ferdinand Huber 2把钥匙放回Emil手里，像放回一块烫过的铁。

  Emil没有等投票，而是让Mitzi Stein把委员会过去三年的差旅账目复印了十份。会议开始前，Gustav Lechner 2的动议被一份“程序审查”取代；审查没有找到Emil的问题，却找到了两次重复报销。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000013; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; flag +rival_audited; knowledge “委员会的差旅账目里有两次重复报销。”; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Emil仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Emil学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 44 -> 51; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 1 -> 2; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.009999999999999995; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.buy-for-elder` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  前面的老人掏零钱时手抖得厉害，数了三遍也没凑够。Emil替老人把四十赫勒递进窗口，老人连声道谢，硬塞回一枚十赫勒铜板。等Emil自己买票时，窗口已经挂出“售罄”的牌子，只好站在剧院门廊下听里面隐约的乐声，脚底冰凉；这一趟净少三十赫勒，却让队伍里的人都记住了这份体面。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (替排队的老人垫付站票钱); cash 0.1 (老人硬塞回的铜板); reputation 0.04000000000000001; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1940.05** `state_reaction:bc18-ridiculous-fame/exposure-backfires` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  Alois Kral把Emil并不是那个人的证据寄给报社，还附上自己的姓名。编辑把它们写成一篇“旧友回忆”，读者读到的却是两个人多年的交情；Emil看见报纸时，先笑了，随后把那一页折起来，放进抽屉最深处。

  Alois Winter 2请Emil顺便看看城里的水厂。Emil把一只标着“关闭”的阀门拧向另一边，半小时后，半条街的菜窖进了水。没有人受伤，镇公所赔了菜农六克朗，又给Emil两克朗车马费，请他尽快搭下一班车离开。Helene Kral在站台上说，这也算一种巡回演讲。

  一年过去，街上还有人记得Emil那阵子的排场。债主没有立刻上门，亲戚也还愿意在星期天来吃饭；Emil把新添的银器擦了一遍，发现其中一只底下刻着别人的名字。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.height 11 -> 15; state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.witnesses 45 -> 51; state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.fragility 94 -> 97; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Emil仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Emil学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 64 -> 71; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 2 -> 3; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.010000000000000002; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `agency:organize_meeting` tags: agency, agency:organize_meeting
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang召集了一次小范围会议，和Gustav 2把下一次行动、会费和分工写在纸上。
  _effects: project; historical development:initiative-building:person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:006250-1938 as organizer; historical_influence; knowledge “我召集了一次关于环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, geme的会议。”; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002415:child; reputation 0.02_

- **1941.01** `r2-law.will-reading::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/signature-water-damaged` tags: law, inheritance, will, family, probate, vienna, urban, crisis
  Franz Huber 2念到第三页时停下，说签名被水泡过，看不清。Maria Bauer立刻说这份遗嘱不算，房子该按老规矩分。Emil把纸举到窗边，认出父亲写“给照顾我的人”那一行；Alois Lechner在门口点头，说最后半年是Emil在端药。
  _effects: cash -6 (遗嘱第三页被水泡过); condition; flag +will_signature_water_damaged; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000675:child; knowledge “the will names whoever provided care in the last six months”_

- **1941.02** `hs18-occupation.piano::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/refuse-to-sign` tags: occupation, piano, requisition, witness, neighbor, history, vienna, urban
  Emil把笔推回去，说钢琴的主人就住在隔壁。Franz Huber 2没有争，只让工人把琴盖也搬走，又记下Emil的门牌号。卡车开走时，Emil Weiss家的窗子开了又关；Emil站在空出来的门洞前，听见自己的呼吸声。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:766; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; reputation 0.020000000000000004; flag +refused_piano_receipt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

- **1941.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil看Luise Winter 3盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Luise Winter 3愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Emil门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Emil打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104; food_

- **1942.01** `project_progressed` tags: project

  抬棺的人在第一级台阶上停了一下，说这棺材轻得不对。Alois Kral伸手敲了敲木板，里面传出的不是沉闷的回声；Berta Bauer 3把登记簿合上，Gustav Lechner 2的黑纱滑到肩膀上。当天下午，警察在火车站候车室里找到了Emil，手里还攥着一张去林茨的车票。

  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 90 -> 82_

- **1942.05** `ml18-gambling.card-system::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/casino-bans-the-system` tags: gambling, luck, money, casino, risk, absurd, vienna, urban
  赌场经理把Emil请到柜台后面，端来一杯咖啡，说这套法子已经在别的桌上被用过了。他们不查牌，只请Emil以后别再来；作为补偿，柜台退给Emil十二克朗的车马费。Gustav Lechner 2在门口笑出声，说这是Emil第一次靠赢钱被赶出去。
  _effects: cash 12 (the casino's travel compensation); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:832; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; reputation 0.009999999999999998; flag +banned_from_casino; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.02; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1942.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Josef Lechner一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Josef Lechner的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1942.09** `ml18-inheritance.conditional-will::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sell-the-workshop-and-forfeit` tags: inheritance, legal, family, money, conditions, cinema, quiet, vienna
  Emil把影院里的放映机、折叠椅和几卷旧片子一起卖给另一家影院，得了十二克朗；遗嘱里的六十克朗随即转给Ferdinand Stein 2。Franz Huber 2把文件收进皮包，说这是合法的，只是姑婆大概不会喜欢。Emil用那笔钱换了新窗帘，旧影院的门上钉了一块出租牌。
  _effects: cash 22 (sale of the workshop equipment and the forfeited first payment); reputation -0.05; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109; flag +broke_the_will_conditions; knowledge “只要影院关门，剩下的五十克朗就归那个旁支亲戚。”; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109_

  _effects: cash -1 (罢工第七天的汤锅); food; health 0.020000000000000018 (罢工第七天的汤锅); flag +work13_strike_soup_kitchen; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; organization_funds_

  Alois Lechner 3在门口摆了一只木箱，卖蜡烛和印着水渍的小卡片，说摸一摸就能好。Emil看着邻居把零钱收进围裙，没有阻止。几天后，楼道里排起了队；Emil从后门进出，怕被人认成那个“显灵”的女人。
  _effects: cash 2 (邻居分给subject的蜡烛钱); reputation 0.02; knowledge “邻居把我的水渍变成了卖蜡烛的摊子。”; flag +df18_water_stain_shrine; condition; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: relation friend with person-franz-lechner; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1943.11** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Emil Huber 2却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Emil把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; flag +still_looking_for_room_
