  Gustav Lechner 2没有到庭，案子只能延期。Emil在走廊里等到中午，Franz Huber 2说下个月再来。回家的电车上，Emil看见Gustav Lechner 2站在另一节车厢，两个人都没有动。
  _effects: flag +divorce_pending; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1944.09** `statistical_infection`
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1944.10** `statistical_recovery`
  Emil从肺结核中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1944.10** `r2-law.bank-run::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/withdrawal-capped` tags: finance, bank, panic, crisis, savings, vienna, urban, war
  Emil排到铁栅门前时，窗口只剩一条缝。Helene 2把存折翻到最后一页，说今天每人只兑五十克朗，其余等董事会通知。Grete Weiss在后面把存折举过头顶，喊自己存的是金币；Katharina Berg从二楼探出头，只说了一句“都排队”。
  _effects: cash -2 (铁栅门后的第三排); mood -0.07000000000000006; flag +savings_frozen_in_bank_run; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001419:child; knowledge “the bank limits withdrawals to fifty kronen a day”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/postwar-illness` tags: war, liberation, 1945, injury
  Emil在战后第二个月病倒，医院没有足够的药，医生只给了退烧粉。邻居每天送来一碗汤，放在门口就走。
  _effects: health -0.15000000000000002 (postwar illness); cash -4 (medicine); condition_

- **1945.01** `hs18-blackout.cellar::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/report-the-coal` tags: blackout, cellar, coal, neighbor, denunciation, history, vienna, urban
  Emil上楼时对巡逻队的人说地窖里有人囤煤。巡逻队下去，把两桶煤抬走，Grete Stein跟在后面，没有争辩。当天晚上，Emil家门口多了一小堆碎煤，像是从桶底扫出来的。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:954; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; reputation 0.02; flag +reported_hidden_coal; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1945.02** `ad20-stranger-request::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/refuse-and-walk-on` tags: stranger, envelope, request, everyday, obligation, risk, vienna, urban
  Emil把信封推回去，说今天不方便。Grete Stein没有生气，只把信封收回口袋，说“那就算了”。Emil走出巷口，回头看了一眼，Grete Stein还站在原地，像在等另一个人。
  _effects: mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +envelope_refused; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1945.07** `kernel-social.misunderstanding-apology::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/misunderstanding.jam-and-apology` tags: social, misunderstanding, apology, friendship, neighbor, reputation, vienna, urban
  话是从洗衣房传出来的：有人说Emil笑过Marie Huber死去的孩子。Emil提着一罐李子酱上楼，在门口把事情从头说了一遍，Marie Huber听完把酱收下，又拿出两只杯子。两人坐到天黑，谁也没再提那句话，只商量明天的煤该谁去买。
  _effects: relation friend with person-marie-huber; role; cash -1 (a jar of plum jam for the apology); flag +friendship_repaired_

- **1945.09** `office16-hire.protege::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/hired-competent` tags: office, hire, patronage, bureaucracy, work, power, vienna, urban
  Emil把Anton Lechner 2的名字写在编制表的空行上，又在旁边注了“试用三个月”。Alois Winter 2提醒说预算只够半个位置，Emil还是把名字留下了。第二天，Anton Lechner 2提前半小时到，把走廊里的旧档案按年份重新排了一遍。
  _effects: position 助理文书 @building:person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:006250; organization_funds; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118; reputation 0.03; flag +hired_protege; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118_

  Emil替Luise 2向Grete Stein请了病假，又把那件可能惹麻烦的东西藏进自己的工具箱。Grete Stein看了Emil一眼，说病假可以补，别的东西补不了；Luise 2在里屋听见，门没有关严。
  _effects: state_add pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:998; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003582:child; flag +covered_child_mistake; knowledge “病假可以补，别的东西补不了。”; flag +second_chance_open_

  Emil忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Emil听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 90 -> 82; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: historical development:initiative-building:person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:006250-1938 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.015_

- **1946.06** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil和Hermann Berg谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Emil家，钥匙只在白天给。Hermann Berg说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Grete Stein听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Emil把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.07** `ad20-loan::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/loan-refused` tags: loan, debt, credit, everyday, obligation, money, vienna, urban
  Josef Kral把Emil的收入证明翻来覆去看了两遍，说数目不够。Emil问能不能少借一点；Josef Kral说“少借也要还得起”。走出门时，Emil把证明信重新叠好，发现上面有一道自己刚才捏出的折痕。
  _effects: flag +loan_refused; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; cash -1 (paperwork and a wasted morning); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

  Franz Huber 2把笔记本合上，问Emil能不能再讲一件小事，不要那些大词。Emil说去年冬天在车站帮一个陌生人看过箱子；Franz Huber 2记下箱子的大小和颜色。第二天报纸出来，Emil的名字在第三版，旁边是一张没有拍好的侧脸。
  _effects: state_add ad20-public-attention state:world-vienna-1912:1048; reputation 0.04000000000000001; flag +interview_printed_kindly; mood 0.030000000000000027; knowledge “报纸第三版登了采访，旁边是一张没有拍好的侧脸。”; flag +public_attention_active_

  两个人没有握手，也没有关门。Luise Weiss把一包烟放在门槛上，Emil没有捡；过了半晌，Emil把自己的火柴盒踢过去，Luise Weiss接住，点了一支，谁都没有提当年的事。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 50 -> 47; flag +uneasy_truce_with_old_enemy; knowledge “门槛上有一包烟，火柴盒被踢过去又接住，谁都没有提当年。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116_

  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001876:child; food_

  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang召集了一次小范围会议，和Helene Huber把下一次行动、会费和分工写在纸上。
  _effects: project; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; reputation 0.01999999999999999_

- **1950.09** `r1-civ.resistance-cell::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/joined-resistance-cell` tags: politics, resistance, underground, map, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  地窖里没有灯，Anna Moser 2划了三根火柴，让每个人看清彼此的脸。任务是把一张地图送到城外，Anton Lechner 2说电车查得紧，最好走铁路。Emil被分到画路线，铅笔在纸上抖，Anna Moser 2把Emil的手按住，说“画慢一点，别画直线”。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +joined_resistance_cell; role; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118; knowledge “the route must follow the rail embankment, not the roads”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1950.10** `office16-appointment.patronage-ledger::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/list-rejected` tags: office, appointment, patronage, organization, politics, power, vienna, urban
  名单在委员会卡住了。Alois Lechner 2的名字被划掉，理由是“没有相关资历”；Emil没有争，只把名单收回公文包。晚上，Alois Lechner 2来问结果，Emil说再等一年；Alois Lechner 2没有坐，站了一会儿就走了。
  _effects: reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; flag +appointment_list_rejected; knowledge “委员会以“没有相关资历”为由划掉了联络员的名字。”_

- **1951.01** `project_progressed` tags: project
  「Emil召集的会议」继续推进了一阶段。
  _effects: project_

- **1951.01** `af17-wish.family-inheritance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-will-was-a-joke` tags: wish, love, family, inheritance, shop, debt, irony, vienna
  公证人念到最后一段，停了一下：死者把烟铺留给了“那个总在门口躲雨的年轻人”，落款处画着一只猫。铺子其实早就抵押出去，剩下的只有一只木腿、一本手写配方和两位没有血缘的老小姐。Emil帮她们把煤搬上楼，没有答应什么；Grete Huber却把配方塞进Emil的口袋，说冬天用得着。
  _effects: cash -1 (帮两位老小姐买煤); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; skill; condition; flag +kept_the_recipe; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `agency:recruit_members` tags: agency, agency:recruit_members
  Emil试图招募Luise Weiss，但对方担心工作和警察，没有给出承诺。
  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1951.01** `r1-fam.household-splitting::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/splitting.watch` tags: family, household, siblings, money, separate_hearth, vienna, urban, postwar
  Berta Bauer提出把父亲的怀表卖了，钱对半分，从此各过各的。Emil把怀表拿出来，放在桌上；Berta Bauer伸手去拿，Emil按住表链，说先算清楚这些年谁交的房租。两人算到半夜，表没有卖成，灶却分开了。
  _effects: cash 2 (分灶); flag +hearth_divided_over_the_watch; role; knowledge “欠下的房租比那只怀表值钱。”_

- **1951.10** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Helene 2先付了四克朗，Emil收好钱，又把作息讲清楚：早班时锅归谁先使，夜里的门几时落栓。Katharina Weiss笑说这屋里像多了个自家人，随即又提醒名声要紧。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household_

- **1952.01** `project_completed` tags: project
  「Emil召集的会议」完成了当前阶段，参与者留下了实际的记录和后续安排。

- **1952.01** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/blocked-the-permit` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  Emil把一份扩建许可证压在抽屉最下面，理由是排水和消防通道不合规。申请人Josef 2在走廊里等到下班，最后只拿到一张写着“补件”的纸条。Emil知道这份许可明年还会再来，但今年不会从自己的桌上出去。
  _effects: reputation 0.01999999999999999; skill; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003990:child; flag +blocked_permit; knowledge “扩建申请的排水图与消防通道不符合现行规定。”_

  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `r13-fam.foster-child::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::foster-postwar/orphanage-full` tags: task, care, foster, child, household, orphan, vienna, urban
  孤儿院的院长说孩子太多，床不够，让Emil再等一个月。Josef Lechner 2把Anna Weiss领回临时收容所，Emil每周去看一次。孩子渐渐肯说话了，却总是在Emil起身时抓住衣角，问下一次什么时候来。
  _effects: cash -2 (门口多出来的一双小鞋); condition; flag +waited_for_foster_placement; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:014207:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-135_

  Luise Bauer说只需要Emil在明天下午去一趟车站，把一个箱子交给穿灰外套的人。Emil问箱子里是什么；Luise Bauer说“你不需要知道”。Emil还是去了，因为当年Luise Bauer替Emil垫过一笔医药费。
  _effects: state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:1456; cash -1 (the trip to the station); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005545:child; flag +favor_done; mood -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “箱子里是什么，邻居没有说。”; flag +private_bargain_active_

  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 55 -> 61; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.leverage 50 -> 54; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.10** `ml18-gambling.card-system::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/stop-while-ahead` tags: gambling, luck, money, casino, risk, absurd, vienna, urban
  纸牌桌上连开三把庄。Emil按自己写在烟盒背面的翻倍法，把最后十五克朗押在闲上。庄家翻开一张四，闲家九点；Gustav Lechner 2的手在桌下抓住Emil的袖子；Emil把赢回的三十克朗换成现钱，数出十克朗塞进袜筒，剩下的放进内袋，说今晚到此为止。
  _effects: cash 30 (winnings taken off the card table); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:1457; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; reputation 0.01999999999999999; flag +left_the_table_ahead; mood 0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1953.01** `af17-wish.family-inheritance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/kept-the-shop-and-the-sisters` tags: wish, love, family, inheritance, shop, debt, irony, vienna
  遗嘱把烟铺、楼上的两位老小姐和十四克朗的赊账一起留给Emil。Alois Lechner第一次见面就叫Emil“侄子”，把钥匙、账本和一只总在柜台下打盹的猫交过来。Emil原本只想要一个家，现在有了一个需要每天清晨六点开门的家；Hermann Weiss在门口抽完一支雪茄，提醒Emil月底先还利息。
  _effects: role; cash -2 (先还一部分烟铺赊账); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-141; reputation 0.01999999999999999; condition; flag +kept_the_tobacco_shop; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-141_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:ad20-private-bargain/the-favor-ages/kept` tags: ad20-private-bargain, private, bargain, favor, debt, trust, leverage, exit
  那笔私下的安排又过了一年。没有人提起，但Emil在街上遇见对方时，两个人都多停了一拍。Emil知道，沉默本身也是利息。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 55 -> 59; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 61 -> 59; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.exit 20 -> 22; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Emil在石板上写自己的名字，写到第三遍才把最后一笔收住。Helene Huber把Emil的手按在粉笔上，说慢慢来。放学时，Emil把石板抱在怀里，经过肉铺时第一次看懂招牌上写的是“今日有肝”。那天晚上，Emil在账本封皮上又写了一遍，写到最后一笔时手没有抖。
  _effects: mood 0.07999999999999996; flag +learned_to_sign_name; skill_

- **1953.02** `ml18-inheritance.conditional-will::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/keep-the-candle-workshop-open` tags: inheritance, legal, family, money, conditions, cinema, quiet, vienna
  姑婆的遗嘱只有一页：六十克朗留给Emil，条件是那间放无声电影的小影院再开五年，并且把她的侄孙女送进学校。Lena把三个条件念了两遍；Ferdinand Lechner 2在门外等着，手里捏着另一份没有拆封的遗嘱。Emil先领了十克朗，把影院的钥匙挂在腰上。
  _effects: cash 10 (first payment under the conditional will); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:1548; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96; flag +conditional_heir; knowledge “遗嘱要求小影院再开五年，并把姑婆的侄孙女送进学校。”; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96; flag -sudden_fortune; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1953.07** `p21-mistaken-identity::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-photograph-is-wrong` tags: mistaken_identity, police, identity, arrest, public, secret, absurd, high_drama
  Gustav Lechner 2把通缉照片和Emil的脸并排放在灯下，发现照片上的人左眉有一道疤，而Emil没有。Franz Lechner在旁边作证，说那天晚上Emil一直在帮自己搬煤；Gustav Lechner 2把银怀表推回来，表盖内侧的名字却不是Emil的。灯罩里有一只死蛾。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; flag +mistaken_identity_cleared; knowledge “通缉照片上的人左眉有一道疤，而当事人没有；表盖内侧的名字也不是当事人的。”; cash 1 (compensation for a night in custody); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/fined` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Franz Huber 2在第三份合同上停住了：同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是Emil。罚款单当场开出，金额不算大，但足够让下一季度的办公用品全部改成最便宜的牌子。Emil没有争辩，只在收据背面写了日期。
  _effects: reputation -0.04999999999999999; mood -0.06000000000000005; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:1547; flag +fined_after_audit; knowledge “同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是我的。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137; flag +office_under_siege_

  对方在一个不合时宜的时刻提起旧事，语气很客气，意思却不客气。Emil听见自己答应得太快，挂断以后才发现手心有一层薄汗。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.leverage 54 -> 62; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 59 -> 63; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 59 -> 55; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Josef Lechner站在走廊尽头，脚边一只用绳子捆住的纸箱，问能不能借宿两晚。Emil把厨房的折叠床打开，又烧了一壶水。夜里，Josef Lechner在梦里说了一种他听不懂的话，Emil把门闩又检查了一遍。
  _effects: cash -2 (走廊里的陌生口音); food; flag +sheltered_refugee_two_nights_

- **1954.10** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Elisabeth Kral把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Elisabeth Kral在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +parish_refused_aid_

  肉商的账本上少了二十公斤马肉，Grete Weiss要Emil签字认账。Emil知道是夜班门房把肉转卖给了普拉特的香肠摊，但门房是Grete Weiss的小舅子。Emil不签，第二天被调去扫兽栏；臭味洗了三遍还在指甲缝里。

  纪念仪式上，Friedrich Bauer问Emil当年停战时在做什么。Emil说在擦枪，擦到一半听见外面安静了，还以为是耳朵坏了。Friedrich Bauer笑，Emil也笑，笑完把手里那支没点的烟放回口袋。

- **1956.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/chronic-cough` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil在诊所走廊的第三张长凳上坐到中午，膝上压着挂号纸和一块湿手帕。Luise Weiss把听诊器贴上来不到半分钟，说是潮气和烟，开了褐色止咳糖浆，又让下周再来。药费从当天的菜钱里扣走，回家时篮子里只剩半棵卷心菜。
  _effects: cash -3 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); health -0.030000000000000027 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); condition; flag +clinic_first_visit; knowledge “the clinic doctor called the cough chronic bronchitis”_

- **1956.07** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Emil把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Katharina Weiss放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Hermann 2在门口看了一眼，没多话。

  Emil把存折里的钱全取出来，装进一只面粉袋，直奔市场。Elisabeth Bauer说上午的面包价已经不作数，下午还要涨；Emil把袋子往柜台上一倒，纸币散了一地。Friedrich Hofer 2蹲下来帮忙捡，说这钱昨天还能买一双鞋。

- **1912.02** `p22-strange-doctor-prediction::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-doctor-is-optimistic` tags: infancy, family, doctor, illness, omen, strange, dark, drama
  医生说孩子只是瘦，开了一瓶味道很坏的补药，说“过了春天就好了”。Josefine Hofer把这句话记了很多年；每年春天，家里都要说一遍，像在核对一张旧账单，药瓶上还贴着手写的标签。

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

  走到桥洞前，Elisabeth Kral说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Lena站在原地看着Elisabeth Kral拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1935.01** `r13-work.rebuild-after-fire::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/only-boarded-up` tags: commerce, fire, rebuilding, shop, debt, vienna, urban, crisis
  Lena算了一遍，重铺屋顶的钱够租两年新铺面，于是只把临街的窗用木板钉上。货搬到后院，熟客从侧门进来，买完就走。Ferdinand Bauer来看了两次，说这样下去租约到期就不续；Lena把钉子又敲紧了一颗。
  _effects: cash -4 (烧黑的房梁); mood -0.10000000000000003; condition; flag +work13_shop_boarded_up_after_fire; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87_

- **1935.01** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.wedding-procession/wedding-procession.watch-outside` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, wedding, neighbor, celebration
  新娘的婚纱是租的，裙摆上有一块洗过的痕迹，可她还是走得很小心，像踩在水面上。Lena把粘在袖口的纸花弹掉，听见院里有人喊“新郎官唱一个”，接着是跑调的歌声和一阵拍手。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1935.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/leave-before-dessert` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  问到后来，Josefine Hofer提起别家有个更稳当的差事。Josef Lechner把餐巾往桌上一放，说吃好了，先走。两人在楼梯间站住，Josef Lechner说不是Lena的错，可下次再来还得一样坐这张桌子；Lena心里松了一点，也知道这顿饭没吃完，事情并没有过去。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000708:child; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.07** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena把通知念给Berta Weiss 2听，Berta Weiss 2摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Lena去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.10** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-picked-coal` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock, injury
  多瑙河码头的雾还没散，工头捏着名单念了Lena的名字。煤灰落在汗湿的脖颈上，Helene Winter在铁门边看着，说这活儿一天顶三天，可肩膀得疼上一个礼拜。
  _effects: cash 4.2 (卸煤半日的工钱); health -0.05 (扛煤筐磨破肩膀)_

- **1936.01** `r13-edu.literacy-class::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/signs-own-name` tags: education, literacy, adult_education, class, belonging, vienna, urban, crisis
  Lena在石板上写自己的名字，写到第三遍才把最后一笔收住。Gustav Hofer把Lena的手按在粉笔上，说慢慢来。放学时，Lena把石板抱在怀里，经过肉铺时第一次看懂招牌上写的是“今日有肝”。那天晚上，Lena在账本封皮上又写了一遍，写到最后一笔时手没有抖。
  _effects: mood 0.07999999999999996; flag +learned_to_sign_name; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1936.04** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.sheet-quarrel` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Lena发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Mitzi Lechner问是谁动的。Mitzi Lechner说是Maria Lechner嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1936.05** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Lena把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Otto Stein放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Berta Weiss在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

- **1936.11** `r3-mig.displaced-persons-camp::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hired-as-camp-clerk` tags: migration, refugees, camp, relief, papers, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi Stein问Lena会不会记账，Lena说在老家管过合作社的账。当天下午Lena就坐在仓库门口，给领饼干的人划勾，一天两克朗。Rosa Weiss来领饼干时，Lena多给了半块，两个人都没有说话。
  _effects: flag +hired_as_camp_clerk; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000519:child; knowledge “the relief team needs people who can keep accounts”; employment camp clerk @ org-die-fackel; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1937.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Lena没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.03 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006844_

- **1937.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Katharina Lechner。接生的人收六克朗，Josef Lechner在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Lena起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.08 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:410_

  _effects: cash -1 (厨房桌上的第三副碗筷); flag +parental_consent_given; role; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “the mother’s silver thimble is the family’s consent token”_

- **1937.07** `vienna-women-housework.care-old-parent/care.institution` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  医院走廊里一股石炭酸味，Lena把包袱交给护士，里面是两件换洗衣裳。Josefine Hofer拉着她的手说了几句，听不清。回来路过教堂，她没进去，只在台阶上坐了一会儿。
  _effects: cash -8 (支付母亲住院的押金与费用); health 0.019999999999999997 (夜间不再需要频繁起身，睡眠好转); mood -0.05999999999999994; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-depression/laid-off` tags: adulthood, depression, work, unemployment
  厂里把一张短工名单贴在厂门口。Lena的名字在第三行，旁边写着“下周等通知”。等到月底，通知没有来，工票也停了。
  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

- **1938.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Lena难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Maria Lechner；Lena一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Josef Lechner把钟表送进当铺。Lena落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.12000000000000001 (difficult childbirth); condition; cash -20 (midwife and hospital); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:465_

- **1938.01** `hs18-occupation.piano::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sign-the-receipt` tags: occupation, piano, requisition, witness, neighbor, history, vienna, urban
  两名工人把Berta Weiss家的钢琴抬上卡车，琴腿在门框上磕掉一块漆。Grete Bauer把一张纸塞给Lena，说只要签“屋内无人认领”，就不必再搬第二趟。Lena签了名，看见Berta Weiss站在对面窗帘后，手一直按在玻璃上。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:466; knowledge “签字比争辩更快，也比争辩更难忘。”; flag +signed_abandoned_piano_receipt; mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1938.01** `r13-civ.soup-kitchen::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::r13-civ.soup-kitchen-crisis/crisis-locals-only` tags: social, charity, soup_kitchen, hunger, volunteer, vienna, urban, crisis
  有人在门口喊，说汤是给本区人喝的，不能便宜了外来的。Lena看了看锅里剩的底子，又看了看队伍里那些没票的人。Luise Weiss 2让Lena把门关一半，说先紧着孩子和老人。门板合上时，外面有人用德语和另一种话一起骂。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.03999999999999998; flag +soup_kitchen_turned_away_outsiders; knowledge “the kitchen has no rule for outsiders and only enough for on”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

  五点刚过，Grete Bauer带着两个人敲门，出示一张没有法官签名的搜查令。抽屉被倒扣在床上，书信按日期排好，装进一个印着鹰徽的纸袋。Franz Winter披着外套站在楼梯口，Grete Bauer让让开；Lena被允许穿上鞋，但没有系鞋带，走到楼下时袜子已经湿了。
  _effects: cash -3 (清晨五点的敲门声); reputation -0.08000000000000002; condition; flag +arrested_1934; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1938.08** `ad20-lucky-coincidence::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-coin-on-the-track` tags: luck, coincidence, everyday, money, superstition, debt, vienna, urban
  电车刚过去，轨道边躺着一枚硬币，被车轮压得有点弯。Lena把它捡起来，发现还能花。那天下午，Lena用这枚硬币买了一小块肥皂，剩下的钱又放回口袋，像放回一个没有用完的运气。
  _effects: cash 2 (a coin found by the tram tracks); mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +lucky_coin; knowledge “电车刚过去，轨道边躺着一枚被压弯的硬币。”_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Gustav Lechner 2在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Lena排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition_

  Luise Weiss 2把几张钞票压在咖啡杯底下，说只要一段能上版的细节。Lena讲了钢琴、门洞和那件湿外套；对方记得很快，临走前问能不能用真名。Lena说用化名，记者笑了一下，说化名也得像真的。

  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 88 -> 90_

  Lena把第三十七页从本子上撕下来，碎纸丢进废纸篓，从第一人称重新起笔。三个月里她改成一部五十页的短稿，用挂号寄给Alois Lechner 3，信封和稿纸花掉一点二克朗，母亲的药费是向Emil借了五克朗才垫上的。
  _effects: skill; cash -1.2 (挂号寄稿的纸张与邮资（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); mood 0.040000000000000036_

  Lena觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Franz Huber 2在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

  _effects: skill; reputation 0.020000000000000018_

  Lena在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Lena去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为环城大道写字楼管理处的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000708:child_

- **1942.01** `p10-migration-tasks.return-home/return-ticket-bought` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, return, train
  他在南站买了一张三等车票，十二克朗四十赫勒，把木工工具留给了同屋的工友。车过布吕恩时换了一次车，哥哥站在站台上，手里拎着一壶水。第二天一早他就跟着下地，手上磨出了新泡。
  _effects: cash -13 (回乡三等车票与路上买面包的钱（其中13克朗记入债务）); mood 0.07000000000000006; relation kin with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; flag +worked_in_vienna_factory_

- **1942.02** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.organ-grinder/organ-grinder.follow-monkey` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, music, street
  走到街角，猴子突然蹿上窗台，把人家晾的一串洋葱碰掉了。乐师连声道歉，Lena帮着捡洋葱，两人笑了一阵。他说自己在二三区之间转，哪天转到她住的那条街，给她摇一支不收钱的。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1942.08** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.cousin-bed/three-days-only` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Lena只答应让Ferdinand Bauer住三天，腾出半张床，又托Anton去打听便宜床位。三天里Ferdinand Bauer白天出去找活，傍晚回来坐在行军床边上，藤箱始终没有打开。第三天晚上Anton带回消息，说有一处工人宿舍，一周一克朗；Lena数出一克朗硬币放在箱盖上，替Ferdinand Bauer垫了第一周。
  _effects: cash -1 (替表亲垫付工人宿舍第一周（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87_

- **1943.01** `state_expire:hs18-street-witness` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street

- **1943.01** `p10-family-tasks.find-missing-kin/find-missing-kin.missing-list` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, missing_kin
  警察局的桌子后面的人把哥哥的名字写进名册，撕下一张编号条。Lena在走廊里碰到一个女人，她找丈夫，手里也捏着一张条子。两人跑了两处收容所，登记簿翻到最后一页也没有。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; flag +brother_missing_registered; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1943.03** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena没有立刻答话，先去敲Alois Winter 2的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Alois Winter 2提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Lena把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1943.05** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/stay-and-tidy-chairs` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  店主来收煤气灯，Lena把铜灯擦净递过去，Katharina Kral 2在门外数着散场的人。有人回头问Lena下次站哪边，Lena说等椅子摆回原样再想。夜里风把纸板吹倒了。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.06** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.flour-loan/flour-loan.half-bag-with-note` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Lena只舀出半袋，说自家也不宽裕，要Franz Huber 2把上次的煤钱一并记在纸上。Franz Huber 2接过纸片，当着面把日期和数目重写一遍，两人各留一半。面粉少了，账却清楚了，Lena把纸片夹进账簿样的旧本子。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1943.09** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.far` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river
  Lena顺着河岸一直走，走过铁路桥又走回来，天已经黑透。回来时脚底发热，袜子磨在脚背上，她一路数着路灯走完最后一段巷子，进门先坐下脱了鞋。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044_

  字牌贴在石灰墙上，最后两行他实在分不清。Franz Huber 2用笔杆敲敲桌面，Hermann Stein把名字写进另一本册子，说有缺再叫。Lena把证明一张张叠好，折边已经发软。
  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

  Lena在后屋的会议上报出自己街区的名字：谁家缺煤，谁被房东催租，谁知道哪间印刷厂还在招人。第二区夜班出租合作社把这些事记进一本旧账本，散会时每个人带走一叠更小的纸条。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

- **1944.10** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Luise Huber把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Luise Huber在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; flag +parish_refused_aid; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

  苏联士兵进城时，Lena躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Lena去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1945.01** `p10-family-tasks.appeal-inheritance/appeal-inheritance.half` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, appeal
  二楼办事处里很冷，职员把信纸对着窗光照了照，说笔迹没错。四十二克朗用纸包好。兄弟在走廊里把纸包接过去，数了一遍，说以后各过各的。Lena把父亲的信收进证件夹。
  _effects: cash 42 (分得父亲存款的一半); relation_end kin with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1945.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Lena说胡话时念着车间的排班表，Elisabeth Lechner一句句应着，拿小勺往嘴里送温水。床头那半瓶退烧药早见了底，她没舍得倒最后一滴，留着天亮再说。
  _effects: condition_

- **1945.07** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/shifts-with-sibling` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Lena和Anton把值班时辰写在门后的粉笔板上：Anton守前半夜，Lena守后半夜，医生说静养二十一天。煤油灯芯拧小，药粉分成小包摆在床头，便盆换水时尽量不碰出响。二十一天熬下来，Lena的眼皮肿了，走路发飘，但Josefine Hofer每次起身总有人扶。
  _effects: flag +night_care_duty_

- **1946.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.cut-the-guarantee/refuse-to-sign` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  把那张字据推回去，说房租和母亲的药费都在桌上摆着，这名字不能签。前两次的借条从证件夹里翻出来放在一起，摊平让对方看清。对方把纸卷起来收进外套，门带得比平常重，此后在街上碰见只当没认出来。
  _effects: relation_end friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101_

  鱼头用报纸包着，报纸上还印着昨天的广告。Helene Winter 2一边刮冰一边说，回去多搁姜，别舍不得醋。Lena把纸包拎在手里，能感觉到凉气从指缝里钻出来，走到家时报纸已经洇湿了一层。
  _effects: food; health 0.019999999999999997 (喝上了热鱼汤); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124_

- **1946.02** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.rush` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  木盆边的水渍映着天光，Lena跪在石阶上揉搓床单，肘弯发酸。晾绳上的湿布滴答作响，Rosa Lechner探身把自家被单也挂上去，两人没多说话。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1946.08** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-sell-furniture-move` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  邻居用麻绳把锅和铺盖捆在一起，打了个死结。他说城南有更便宜的屋子，但离厂远。你蹲在地上把最后几件衣服塞进布袋，听见看门人在楼下把垃圾桶挪来挪去。
  _effects: cash 2.8 (卖旧家具所得); mood -0.06000000000000005_

  Lena说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Emil Lechner没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Lena听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); flag +funeral_society_left_

  _effects: relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; flag +trial_work_pending; mood 0.050000000000000044_

  _effects: health -0.03 (鞋底不平磨出水泡); skill_

  _effects: cash -3 (延迟偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; reputation -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

  炉子封小了，屋里凉得快，Lena把大衣盖在被子上。安娜收碗时看见窗台上一排药瓶，没说什么，第二天多带了半块黑面包。楼下的管道工敲了一下午铁皮，咳嗽声混在里面听不真切。

  _effects: reputation -0.14999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +knows_upstairs_debt_

### 215 subject events

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/colic-night` tags: infancy, family, first, warm, injury
  夜里Anton哭了很久，Josefine Hofer抱着孩子在屋里来回走，数着地板缝。天快亮时孩子终于打了个嗝，安静下来；Josefine Hofer坐在床沿，发现自己还保持着走路的姿势。
  _effects: mood -0.03; health -0.010000000000000009 (lost sleep)_

- **1913.02** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-more` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Anton把空碗推回Josefine Hofer面前，清清楚楚地说了一个字：“还要。”Josefine Hofer愣住，随后把锅底最后一点糊刮进碗里。孩子学会了用一句话让世界再转一圈。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.03_

- **1913.03** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-dog-gets-the-first-word` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban
  Anton追着炉边的狗爬了半间屋，忽然拍着地板喊出狗的名字。狗吓得钻到床下，Josefine Hofer笑得把水壶都放下了；邻居在门外问出了什么事。那天晚上，家里给狗多留了半块面包。
  _effects: mood 0.039999999999999994; food; flag +first_word_dog_name_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-toddler-wrong-cradle/the-neighbour-is-mistaken` tags: infancy, family, rumour, identity, comic, drama

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood 0.019999999999999997; skill_

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/lost-relative` tags: childhood, family, secret, story

  _effects: knowledge “家里有一张没有署名的照片，照片上的人去了很远的地方。”; flag +family_legend_photo; mood -0.01_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/shared-toy` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic

  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood 0.05_

  “月底一起结。”掌柜说完，把半筐煤推到柜台外。Anton用胸口顶住筐沿往家挪，煤灰落进领口，手心被麻绳磨得发亮。Josefine Hofer在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口等到人，先看了看筐底，再看了看Anton的手，把漏掉的碎渣扫进簸箕。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1918.05** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-bullied` tags: childhood, street, social, home

  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.05; condition_

  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Anton前面的大婶一直往后靠，Anton就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Anton的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.ask-for-trial/silent-follow` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work

- **1927.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.save-for-books/bought-used-book` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  回家路上Anton把书夹在腋下，怕雨点打湿纸页。进了门，Josefine Hofer问他哪来的书，他把粘好的书脊翻过来给母亲看，布条颜色跟袜子一样。
  _effects: cash -3 (旧书摊买《机械制图》（其中3克朗记入债务）); skill; mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1928.01** `task-vienna-youth-apprenticeship-search/trial-promised` tags: task, task:work, work, adolescence, apprenticeship
  Anton在印刷厂门口等到工头出来，把姓名、住址和会写的字报了一遍。Emil Huber 2让他下周一早来试半天，先不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1929.01** `task-vienna-poor-apprentice.foreman-introduction/relative-refuses-guarantee` tags: adolescence, apprentice, work, education, poverty, printshop, task, task:work
  亲戚临时反悔，不肯为一个刚离开旧作坊的学徒作保，说自己在厂里还要做人。他站在门口，手里攥着熨平的衬衫袖口，把路上想好的话又咽回去。
  _effects: mood -0.08; relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter_

- **1930.01** `task-vienna-family-care.ask-relative-money/loan-with-schedule` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Elisabeth Lechner 2听Anton把房租和药费的数目报完，从抽屉里数出二十克朗，推到桌上，同时拿出一张纸，要写还款日期。他说每月十五号来一趟，把账报一遍。Anton签字时手有点抖，把纸角压得很平，说好先从月底还三克朗。
  _effects: cash 20 (舅舅借出的钱); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; flag +owes_uncle; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1930.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Emil点了牛奶咖啡，Anton要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡（其中0.12克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.03_

- **1930.02** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/report-to-official` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Anton把传单交给楼里的门房。门房没有接，只让Anton自己去找警察局。那天傍晚，两个穿便衣的人在街口站了很久。
  _effects: role; reputation 0.02_

- **1930.02** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.peek` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Anton下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.03; skill_

- **1930.07** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/hire-care` tags: health, family, home
  Anton托人请了巷尾的看护妇，每天来两回给Josefine Hofer擦洗换药，六克朗花得清清楚楚。看护费不便宜，Anton晚上多接了一份缝补的活，手指被针扎了几回；Josefine Hofer的腿伤有人料理，Anton却连着几夜睡不够。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付看护妇费用)_

- **1931.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.deposit-refused` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  “十克朗押金，先交后干。”Luise Winter 2说得很平。Anton把两只口袋翻给人家看，柜台上只落下一张皱车票。工头收回话头，他也不再问，提着帽子走出栅栏。
  _effects: mood -0.05; reputation -0.01; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1931.02** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.stitched` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Anton用鞋蜡把裂缝填上，又拿粗针和麻线把鞋底缝了一圈。针脚歪，鞋底一高一低，走路时右脚落地总比左脚重。第二天上工回来，脚后跟磨出一个水泡，可鞋没再渗水。
  _effects: health -0.03 (鞋底不平磨出水泡); skill_

- **1931.03** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.soup-kitchen/soup-pot-empty` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, relief, food, church
  前面还有七八个人时，Franz Huber 2已经直起腰把锅盖扣上了。队伍没散，有人骂了一句，有人蹲下把罐子收进布袋。Anton走到巷口，Gustav Lechner 2从后面赶上来，罐子还温着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Josef Kral把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Anton留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Anton在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods); mood -0.06; role_

- **1932.01** `major-task.marriage-proposal/proposal-accepted` tags: task, task:family, family, marriage, courtship, household, partnership
  星期日下午，Anton把Mitzi Stein约到多瑙河边的长椅。两人先算房租、煤价和各自家里的债，Anton才从口袋里摸出那枚旧戒指，说想在下个季度把两家的东西搬到一处。Mitzi Stein把戒指收进衣袋，说先去见各自的母亲。月底，一只箱子和一卷铺盖搬进了Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; household; role; role; cash -8 (ring, moving costs and a small engagement meal); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1932.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Alois Lechner借给Anton一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Anton睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.060000000000000026_

- **1932.04** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.remake-coat/remake-coat.short-jacket` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, mending, clothing, household
  Anton把外套的肩线拆开，往里收了四指，袖口剪掉一截，改出一件到胯骨的短上衣。剩下的布头拼成两只手套，针脚粗，戴着能挡风。她在镜子前转了一圈，肩还是有点空，但总算能穿出门。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.05000000000000002_

- **1932.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  Anton说胡话时念着车间的排班表，Luise Winter 2一句句应着，拿小勺往嘴里送温水。床头那半瓶退烧药早见了底，她没舍得倒最后一滴，留着天亮再说。
  _effects: health -0.04000000000000001 (高烧硬熬伤身); condition; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1932.07** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/organize-relief-table` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  后屋的桌上摊着旧报纸，Anton用尺子画格子，一格一户，旁边写清煤价和面包房欠条。Luise Weiss 2指着第三格说那家孩子咳了整夜，先划过去。蜡烛烧到桌沿时，名单终于能看懂了。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; mood 0.03_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Anton在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Anton把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05000000000000002 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004790_

- **1933.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.skip-both` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Anton在影戏棚和音乐厅之间走了两趟，最后哪处都没进。Anton拐进常去的酒馆，花三十赫勒要了半升啤酒，听角落里有人拉琴，拉得不准但很响。Klara找到Anton时，桌上的杯子已经空了一半，省下的票钱还在口袋里叮当响；这一晚没看成戏，倒把半升啤酒喝出了几分自在。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (酒馆半升啤酒); mood 0.03; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1933.06** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Anton跑了一趟医院，把Josefine Hofer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Anton回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品); health 0.030000000000000027 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.06; flag +parent_hospitalized_

  Anton下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.03; skill_

- **1934.01** `major-work-first-employment/turned-away` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头翻完工票，问Anton上一个东家是谁。Anton说厂子关了，工头又问有没有辞工证明；没有证明，名册就不能写。后面的人挤上来，Anton被推到台阶下面。中午的钟响了，Anton站在街对面，看新来的人把名字一个个写上去。
  _effects: cash -2 (carfare and waiting); reputation -0.03; flag +no_work_references; role; mood -0.07; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

  Anton把Helene Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Helene Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Anton听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.01999999999999999_

  Anton的房租也到期了，只好把Elisabeth Lechner 2的缝纫机搬到自己屋里，说等还钱再还机器。Elisabeth Lechner 2来取针线盒时，看见缝纫机头上盖着布，站了一会儿就走了；第二天，整栋楼都知道这件事。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; role; reputation -0.04; flag +seized_sewing_machine; knowledge “缝纫机被扣作抵押，针线盒还留在邻居手里。”_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Luise Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Anton把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.01999999999999999_

  _effects: mood -0.16000000000000003; condition; flag +return_refused_party_card; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; knowledge “an old party card blocks the return visa”_

  孩子的名字从学校的名册上被划掉以后，家里的碗还多摆了一副。Anton没有把它收起来，直到有一天发现碗沿积了灰，才拿去洗。

  _effects: cash 12 (sale of the print shop subscription list); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:286; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; skill; flag +inherited_print_shop; mood 0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.03_

- **1934.09** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, injury
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Emil把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Anton把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Emil低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。

  Mitzi Stein在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个孩子，取名Mitzi Bauer。Anton在厨房和走廊之间来回，接生的人让烧水、拿布、付六克朗；孩子天亮后睡着了，Anton从此在户籍表上多了“父亲”一栏，手背上还留着烫水壶的红印。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.04000000000000014 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee); role; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:350_

- **1936.01** `ml18-family.fortune-split::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/keep-it-for-the-children` tags: family, money, windfall, inheritance, conflict, dark, vienna, urban
  Hedwig想把钱拿去盘下街角的小酒馆，Anton却把十五克朗直接交到学校，替孩子续了下一年的学费。Hedwig把餐巾摔在桌上，说这家人永远只会把钱埋进土里。Mitzi Stein没有劝，只把汤碗往Anton那边推了推。
  _effects: cash -15 (school fees paid from the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 18 -> 3; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 40; relation rival with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 5; flag +fortune_kept_for_child; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Anton被叫到学校，老师说Helene Bauer这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Anton没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 52 -> 47; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 57 -> 62; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Anton把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Anton来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 3 -> 4; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1936.01** `r13-work.repay-debt::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/paid-in-full` tags: commerce, debt, credit, obligation, household, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton把二十克朗一张张码在桌上，Ferdinand Bauer数完，在还款簿的最后一格画了红勾。Ferdinand Winter在旁边看着，直到Ferdinand Bauer把借据撕成两半才坐下。出门时Anton的口袋空了，但走路的声音比来时轻；那本还款簿被Ferdinand Bauer收进抽屉，压在别的借据下面。
  _effects: cash -20 (还款簿上的红格子); flag +work13_repaid_debt_in_full; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006893_

- **1936.02** `ad20-stranger-request::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/take-the-envelope` tags: stranger, envelope, request, everyday, obligation, risk, vienna, urban
  Hermann Kral在巷口拦住Anton，把一个没有字的信封塞过来，说“明天中午之前，别打开”。Anton问里面是什么；Hermann Kral说“你不需要知道”。信封很薄，捏起来却像有一块硬东西。
  _effects: state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:406; cash 2 (payment for carrying the envelope); relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; flag +envelope_taken; mood -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “信封很薄，里面像有一块硬东西。”; flag +private_bargain_active_

- **1936.04** `r2-rural.communal-oven::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/burned-bread-at-home` tags: rural, bread, community, scarcity, neighbor, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton舍不得那一铜板，在家里的灶上烤面团。火太旺，面包底焦成黑炭，烟从窗户往外冒。Grete Stein提着水桶跑来，以为失了火。Anton把焦面包掰开，里面还是生的。
  _effects: cash -1 (公共烤炉的排队); reputation -0.04; flag +burned_bread_at_home; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1936.06** `p22-goat-heir::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/goat-sold-at-auction` tags: inheritance, absurd, comic, goat, tenement, legal, rumor, public
  拍卖行把山羊牵上台，槌子落下时，它忽然挣脱绳子，在人群里撞翻了两个花盆。买主说这羊不吉利，只肯出半价；Anton拿到的钱刚够付律师费，楼契被银行收走，天井里只剩一截被啃过的牵绳。买主把它牵走时，它回头看了一眼那栋楼。
  _effects: cash -3 (lawyers and auction fees ate the goat price); state_add debt-bondage state:world-vienna-1912:407; reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; flag +goat_sold; mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1936.10** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Anton的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Anton家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007524_

  Mitzi Stein连着三个星期日没有回来。Anton去问工头，工头说Mitzi Stein跟着一批季节工去了下奥地利，铺盖已经托人搬进合租房。Anton把Mitzi Stein的靴子卖了八克朗，用这笔钱付了房租，又把Mitzi Stein的工票收进抽屉。门房问要不要留话，Anton说不用，顺手把门牌上的灰擦了。
  _effects: household; relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; role; flag +deserted_by_partner; cash 8 (sold the partner's boots to pay the rent); mood -0.09999999999999998_

  他被拒绝入境，理由是“没有固定住所”。他在边境站给Lena打电话，电话转到邻居家；对方说“先回来再说”，他买了一张反向车票。他在边境站的长椅上坐到天亮，才买下反向车票。
  _effects: move place-wien; cash -5 (反向车票和电话费); flag +border_refused; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; condition; health -0.020000000000000018 (在边境站过夜); knowledge “没有固定住址会被拒绝入境，亲戚的电话不能作为住址证明”_

- **1937.09** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Berta Weiss在门口替Anton捏着帽子，听见里面一声闷哼。弗朗茨把牙扔进搪瓷盘，说瞧这牙根都黑了。收钱时他多要了二十赫勒，算明矾水的钱，说下次别再拖成这样。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.030000000000000027 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.09** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/speak-with-rent-numbers` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Anton上台只讲本街区：一间套房月租二十六克朗，三个失业的男人挤在一张床上。台下有人点头，有人低头看鞋。Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)的书记把数字记进小册子，主持人把发言时间多给了一分钟。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/flees` tags: politics, persecution, 1938, migration
  Anton在夜里收拾了一只箱子，把证件缝进外套内衬，搭上往西的火车。Mitzi Stein第二天来查人，只看到空床和没关严的窗。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -20 (escape and lodging)_

- **1938.01** `kernel-family.care-sick-parent::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::care-sick-parent-war/care-sick-parent.paid-in-bread` tags: task, task:family, family, care, health, parent, work, vienna
  Anton用面包和煤票请Rosa Lechner每天来两次，Rosa Lechner把药碾碎拌在粥里。Josefine Hofer退烧后第一句话是问面包够不够；Anton说够，转身把柜子里最后一块糖收进药盒。药盒盖上沾着面粉，她用袖子擦了擦。
  _effects: cash -2 (coal tickets set aside for the neighbour nurse); food; relation friend with person-rosa-lechner; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; role; flag +nurse_paid_in_bread_

- **1938.05** `p22-goat-heir::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/goat-keeps-the-building` tags: inheritance, absurd, comic, goat, tenement, legal, rumor, public
  公证人念完遗嘱，整栋楼归一只叫“伯爵”的山羊所有，条件是它继续住在天井里。Anton和Emil Huber 2把租金记在羊的名下，每天轮流挤奶；半年后，房东太太的侄子来争产，看见山羊站在楼梯口，竟先脱帽行了个礼。那天夜里，羊睡在门房，钥匙挂在它的角上。
  _effects: state_add p22-wild-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:533; state_add p22-scandal-engine state:world-vienna-1912:534; cash 4 (rent surplus held in the goat name); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; flag +goat_kept_building; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +wild_fortune_active; flag +scandal_engine_active_

- **1938.05** `state_reaction:p22-scandal-engine/a-public-apology-cools-it` tags: p22-scandal-engine, scandal_engine, scandal, rumor, secret, leverage, shame, network
  Anton在报纸上登了道歉，流言却没有立刻停。第二天，有人在门口放了一束花，也有人把花拿走了；Anton把空花瓶留在窗台上，没有扔。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.heat 30 -> 22; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.reach 45 -> 42; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.shame 15 -> 11; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.leverage 35 -> 40; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008857_

- **1938.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Franz Winter拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1938.12** `ad20-landlord-visit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/rent-raised` tags: housing, rent, landlord, everyday, repair, public, vienna, urban
  Franz Lechner看完窗子，说这栋楼的地税涨了，房租也得跟着涨。Anton把租约摊在桌上，指给他看还有半年；Franz Lechner说“那就从下半年开始”。他走后，Anton把窗缝用旧报纸塞住，报纸上正好登着新房租的广告。
  _effects: cash -2 (rent increase after the landlord’s visit); relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; flag +rent_raised; mood -0.040000000000000036; knowledge “房东说地税涨了，房租从下半年开始加。”_

- **1939.01** `state_reaction:ad20-private-bargain/a-debt-returns` tags: ad20-private-bargain, private, bargain, favor, debt, trust, leverage, exit
  新的债没有走正式渠道，而是被带进了同一间厨房。Anton在桌边听完数目，发现对方连还款日期都替自己算好了。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 47 -> 53; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.leverage 63 -> 67; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  一个见证人要求Anton拿出证据。Anton翻遍抽屉，只找到一张被水浸过的收据；那天晚上，街上的故事换了主角，Anton的名字被挪到了最后一行。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-wild-fortune.height 55 -> 49; state_parameter p22-wild-fortune.witnesses 60 -> 55; state_parameter p22-wild-fortune.fragility 45 -> 52; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  一个曾经替Anton传话的人搬走了，带走了半条街的信任。第二天，信箱里多了一张没有署名的明信片，上面只写着“我还记得”；Anton把明信片翻过来，背面是空的。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.heat 22 -> 29; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.reach 42 -> 47; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.leverage 40 -> 36; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.shame 11 -> 17; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `r13-work.factory-gate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/turned-away` tags: work, hiring, factory, queue, cash, vienna, urban, crisis
  Helene Huber把名单卷起来，说今天只要会看图纸的。Anton在铁门外站到换班，听见里面皮带轮转了一整天。Katharina Moser分给Anton半块黑面包，两人谁也没提明天还来不来；电车票钱花掉了，口袋里的铜板只够买一小袋煤。
  _effects: cash -1 (铁门开了一条缝); mood -0.12; flag +work13_turned_away_at_factory_gate; knowledge “the factory takes only ten men from a gate queue of over a h”_

- **1939.01** `ad20-wedding::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-toast-travels` tags: wedding, family, celebration, everyday, public, gossip, vienna, urban
  婚礼在院子里的长桌上办，Berta Weiss家的女儿把捧花拆开，分给每个还没结婚的人。Anton被推到中间说两句，话没说完，后排已经有人把杯子举起来。那天下午，整条巷子都记住了Anton的声音，连修鞋摊都学着说了一遍。
  _effects: state_add ad20-public-attention state:world-vienna-1912:609; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; reputation 0.019999999999999997; flag +wedding_toast; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +public_attention_active_

  Anton的新故事登出来时，旧的那一页也被重新翻到桌面上。Anton发现两件事共用同一个证人，而那个证人上周还来借过盐。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.heat 29 -> 35; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.reach 47 -> 51; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.shame 17 -> 20; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.leverage 36 -> 38; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Luise Weiss说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Luise Weiss陪着Anton去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000508:child; mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1939.10** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Anton在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Josefine Hofer却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Anton把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1940.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Franz Winter嫌Anton的床单滴水滴到Franz Winter家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Anton下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Anton把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Franz Winter那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008856_

  Anton在街角被人认出来，对方没有要求什么，只说“上次那件事做得不错”。这句客气话被旁边的人听见，下午便有两个陌生人朝Anton点头。关注没有减少，但空气松了一点。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-public-attention.attention 27 -> 23; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.sympathy 57 -> 63; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.opportunity 23 -> 27; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `r13-civ.hide-fugitive::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/refused-shelter` tags: politics, fugitive, shelter, risk, solidarity, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton把门开了一条缝，看见Helene Huber袖口上的破口，还是说家里有孩子，不方便。Helene Huber点点头，问能不能给一双旧鞋。Anton把鞋放在门口，关上门，听见楼梯响了三层。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +refused_fugitive_shelter; knowledge “the fugitive went down the back stair without shoes”_

- **1941.05** `oj18-mortuary.first-body::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/coffin-coop` tags: death, mortuary, funeral, work, odd_job, night, everyday, vienna
  师傅Katharina Berg把一块冷毛巾塞进Anton手里，让Anton先擦死者的脸，再擦自己的。殡葬合作社的规矩写在门背后：先入殓，后结账；无名者也要有一块写着日期的木牌。Anton做完第一具，手抖得点不着烟。
  _effects: organization_found 圣马克斯殡葬合作社 (other); position 入殓工 @mortuary:person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:011121; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:762; cash 1 (first mortuary wages); skill; flag +mortuary_worker; knowledge “殡葬合作社的规矩：先入殓，后结账；无名者也要有一块日期木牌。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1941.07** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Elisabeth Huber在午休时把本子摊在Anton的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Anton从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Elisabeth Huber当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; reputation 0.039999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

- **1941.07** `curse15-betrayal.ledger-share::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-witness-stays-quiet` tags: betrayal, money, family, trust, curse, secret, vienna, urban
  Anna Bauer在办事处说了Anton一整个下午的坏话，从欠债说到那场没人看见的火灾。旁边的人没有替Anton说一句话。几天后，Anton发现自己的名字从互助会的名单上被划掉了，Anna Bauer只说“我也要养家”。
  _effects: reputation -0.08; relation rival with person-anna-bauer; cash -1 (互助会停止发放的补贴); condition; flag +reputation_damaged_by_betrayal_

  Anton把两条舍不得抽的烟塞给Berta Bauer 3，说这琴是替邻居保管的。Berta Bauer 3掂了掂烟，让工人把琴抬回门厅，却把琴凳留下了。Katharina Moser晚上来道谢，Anton说别谢，明天要是再来，就说不认识我。
  _effects: cash -3 (two packs of cigarettes for the officer); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:761; flag +bought_back_neighbors_piano; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

  Anton被叫到学校，老师说Helene Bauer这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Anton没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 10 -> 5; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 50 -> 55; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Anton的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Anton想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 25 -> 9; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 45; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1942.01** `kernel-migrate.refugee-aid::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/civic-refugee-search` tags: migration, refugee, missing_relative, care, postwar, vienna, urban, crisis
  名单墙前挤着十几个人，Luise Winter 3指着一行被划掉的名字，说那是他妹妹。他替对方抄下名字，又去Franz Huber 2的窗口问；窗口说档案在另一栋楼，他去时那栋楼已经锁门。他在档案楼门口等到锁门，只记下了一个门牌号。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104; knowledge “名单上被划掉的名字要查另一栋楼的档案，窗口关门后只能等明天”; cash -3 (抄名单的纸笔和跑两栋楼的车钱); flag +searched_refugee_list; health -0.010000000000000009 (在名单墙前站了一天); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_

- **1942.01** `ml18-gambling.card-system::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/stop-while-ahead` tags: gambling, luck, money, casino, risk, absurd, vienna, urban
  纸牌桌上连开三把庄。Anton按自己写在烟盒背面的翻倍法，把最后十五克朗押在闲上。庄家翻开一张四，闲家九点；Emil Lechner的手在桌下抓住Anton的袖子；Anton把赢回的三十克朗换成现钱，数出十克朗塞进袜筒，剩下的放进内袋，说今晚到此为止。
  _effects: cash 30 (winnings taken off the card table); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:831; reputation 0.02; flag +left_the_table_ahead; mood 0.07999999999999996; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.02; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1942.01** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Anton的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Anton家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011881_

- **1942.02** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/shelter` tags: war, homefront, shortage, injury
  警报响时Anton躲进地下室，和十几户人挤在长凳上。头顶传来闷响，灰尘从梁上落下。警报解除后，街上多了一堆碎瓦。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (shelter conditions); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

- **1942.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef Lechner嫌Anton的床单滴水滴到Josef Lechner家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Anton下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Anton把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Josef Lechner那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000708:child; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

- **1942.12** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/the-papers-hear-about-it` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  报纸的社会版把Anton家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.public true -> true; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 4; reputation -0.02; flag +windfall_went_public_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011892_

- **1943.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/fined` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Gustav Lechner 2在第三份合同上停住了：同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是Anton。罚款单当场开出，金额不算大，但足够让下一季度的办公用品全部改成最便宜的牌子。Anton没有争辩，只在收据背面写了日期。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation -0.05; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:830; flag +fined_after_audit; knowledge “同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是我的。”; flag +office_under_siege_

  Anton又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 80 -> 82_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-slips-away` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  钱在一年里薄了一层：屋顶要修，姐夫来借，彩票站的老主顾说有个稳赚的买卖。Anton把账本翻到最后一页，发现写下的支出比记得的多出四克朗；窗外有人在敲铁皮，声音像在催账。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 30 -> 25; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  话在喉咙里滚了几遍，终究没有等到对方进门。门一响，有人就把紫罗兰放在门槛边，转身往街角走。花被早晨的扫帚拨到墙根，对方捡起来闻了闻，追到巷口只看见一个拐弯的背影，午后托人捎来一句话，说钱的事再算。
  _effects: cash -0.24 (一束紫罗兰); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001876:child; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.02** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/organize-relief-table` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  后屋的桌上摊着旧报纸，Anton用尺子画格子，一格一户，旁边写清煤价和面包房欠条。Katharina Bauer 2指着第三格说那家孩子咳了整夜，先划过去。蜡烛烧到桌沿时，名单终于能看懂了。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

- **1943.03** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Anton在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Anton去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为普拉特动物园夜班队的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1943.04** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household, injury
  Anton把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Anton的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart); health -0.020000000000000018 (moving in winter); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94_

- **1943.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Anton在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Berta 2从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001585:child_

- **1944.03** `p22-goat-heir::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/goat-keeps-the-building` tags: inheritance, absurd, comic, goat, tenement, legal, rumor, public
  公证人念完遗嘱，整栋楼归一只叫“伯爵”的山羊所有，条件是它继续住在天井里。Anton和Grete Bauer把租金记在羊的名下，每天轮流挤奶；半年后，房东太太的侄子来争产，看见山羊站在楼梯口，竟先脱帽行了个礼。那天夜里，羊睡在门房，钥匙挂在它的角上。
  _effects: state_add p22-wild-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:952; state_add p22-scandal-engine state:world-vienna-1912:953; cash 4 (rent surplus held in the goat name); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +wild_fortune_active; flag +scandal_engine_active_

  Mitzi Weiss 3把Anton的收入证明翻来覆去看了两遍，说数目不够。Anton问能不能少借一点；Mitzi Weiss 3说“少借也要还得起”。走出门时，Anton把证明信重新叠好，发现上面有一道自己刚才捏出的折痕。
  _effects: flag +loan_refused; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; cash -1 (paperwork and a wasted morning); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79_

- **1947.01** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.servants-room` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Luise Huber的东家说得很清楚：结婚就得搬出佣人房，工钱也要减。那间房在厨房后面，冬天靠炉灶取暖。Luise Huber算了一夜，第二天把Anton送的围巾叠好送回来。两人在面包房门口站了一会儿，各自去上工；围巾上还留着烤炉的暖气。
  _effects: role; flag +marriage_blocked_by_lodging; cash -1 (bread from the bakery)_

  照片登出后，Anton收到十七封邀请函，都是请去讲“如何面对舆论”。一个工商业协会送来一笔“研究资助”，请Anton去讲一次闭门课；课酬刚好补上办公室这个月的缺口。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; organization_funds; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:1045; flag +scandal_turned_into_speaking_fee; knowledge “十七封邀请函里，有十二封问的是同一件事：能不能把丑闻讲成经验。”; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +office_under_siege_

## Hedwig

Born 1912.01, died 1946.01 (age 34).

- **1912.02** `p22-strange-doctor-prediction::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-doctor-asks-for-the-cradle` tags: infancy, family, doctor, illness, omen, strange, dark, drama
  医生问孩子睡在哪里，看见摇篮紧挨着炉子，皱起眉头。他让Josefine Hofer当晚就把摇篮挪到窗边；Hedwig换了位置，第一夜睡得比从前沉，窗边的风比炉边冷，孩子却睡得很沉。
  _effects: health 0.009999999999999898 (the cradle was moved away from the stove); mood -0.010000000000000002; state_add p22-child-omen state:world-vienna-1912:10; flag +cradle_moved_

- **1912.02** `state_reaction:p22-child-omen/a-stranger-touches-the-door` tags: p22-child-omen, child_omen, omen, strangeness, attention, protection, fear, superstition

- **1912.05** `p22-strange-cradle-secret::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-ticket-is-redeemed` tags: infancy, family, cradle, secret, inheritance, strange, dark, drama
  Josefine Hofer拿着当票去赎，取回一把银勺子，勺柄上刻着一个看不清的字母。回家后把勺子塞进Hedwig手里，说“用吧”；孩子敲着桌沿，勺子发出很轻的响声，像在回答什么。
  _effects: cash -5 (redeeming the pawn ticket); health 0.010000000000000009 (a silver spoon to cut the first teeth); state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:9; flag +spoon_returned_

- **1912.09** `vienna-life-birth-letter-from-the-dead/the-letter-is-real` tags: infancy, family, secret, letter, drama
  邮差把一封边角磨白的信塞进门缝，收信人是一个已经死了三年的名字。Josefine Hofer把Hedwig放在膝上，拆开信，里面只有一句：“孩子像不像他？”Emil Weiss在门口探头，问是不是寄错了；Josefine Hofer把信折回去，说“没有寄错”。
  _effects: flag +letter_from_dead_relative; knowledge “一封写给死去亲人的信问：孩子像不像他。”; mood -0.04_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/smile-at-the-lamp` tags: infancy, family, first, warm
  Josefine Hofer把Hedwig抱到窗边，煤汽灯罩上有一圈晃动的光。孩子盯着看了很久，忽然笑出声，口水滴在Josefine Hofer的袖口上。屋里的人都停了一下，像是听见了什么了不起的消息。
  _effects: mood 0.04; health 0.010000000000000009 (a good day)_

- **1913.01** `state_tick:p22-child-omen/the-omen-returns/the-coincidence-repeats` tags: p22-child-omen, child_omen, omen, strangeness, attention, protection, fear, superstition

- **1917.06** `vienna-early-childhood.fever-night/home-remedy` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  Hedwig烧得翻来覆去，Josefine Hofer把醋倒进温水，用布蘸着擦手心、脚心，又去灶间煮接骨木汁。勺子送到嘴边，Hedwig偏头躲，Josefine Hofer就等一会儿再喂。鸡叫时炉火压小，床脚用旧大衣堵住。两天后热度退了，嘴唇起了一层干皮。
  _effects: health 0.03 (土法退烧，热度渐退); mood -0.06000000000000001_

  工头说这个月赶印一批账簿，晚上加班每小时多给二十赫勒。Hedwig站在教室门口听完课程表，转身回厂里搬纸。Grete Stein追出来说座位可以保留两周，Hedwig点点头，手已经摸上了作坊的门把，夜里机器的声音把课本的事盖了过去。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (夜间加班多挣的工钱); skill; mood -0.039999999999999994_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/joined-and-borrowed` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Hedwig跟着Marie Huber走进印刷厂后面的屋子，交了十赫勒会费，在借书簿上按了手印。他挑了一本讲蒸汽机的册子，封面烫金已经磨掉；管理员说下月十赫勒要按时交，书才能续借。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (工人青年会月费); skill; relation friend with person-marie-huber; flag +workers_youth_member; mood 0.04000000000000001_

- **1927.01** `task-vienna-youth-apprenticeship-search/no-vacancy` tags: task, task:work, work, adolescence, apprenticeship
  Hedwig问了三家印刷厂，得到的回答都是“今年不收学徒”。最后一家让他把名字写在纸角上，说有空缺再通知，但谁都知道那张纸会被压在账本下面。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; knowledge “今年印刷厂大多不收新学徒。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.save-for-books/bought-used-book` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  回家路上Hedwig把书夹在腋下，怕雨点打湿纸页。进了门，Josefine Hofer问他哪来的书，他把粘好的书脊翻过来给母亲看，布条颜色跟袜子一样。
  _effects: cash -3 (旧书摊买《机械制图》（其中1克朗记入债务）); skill; mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1929.01** `task-vienna-youth-apprenticeship-search/trial-promised` tags: task, task:work, work, adolescence, apprenticeship
  Hedwig在印刷厂门口等到工头出来，把姓名、住址和会写的字报了一遍。Ferdinand Huber让他下周一早来试半天，先不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; flag +apprenticeship_trial_promised; mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1930.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.church-organ` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Hedwig数了数前面攒动的人头，把口袋里的硬币又攥紧了些，转身离开队伍。走到街角的教堂时，管风琴正从敞开的门里传出来，Hedwig在最后一排长凳上坐下，听完整段前奏曲，一个赫勒也没花，只跪得膝盖发酸。出门时与Elisabeth Bauer道了别，约好下回还来排队。
  _effects: mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer_

- **1930.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/wage-question-silence` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  Josefine Hofer问起工钱和房租，Hedwig报了个数，又补了一句冬天活少。桌上一时只剩刀叉声。Klara低着头把面包掰成小块，Josefine Hofer往汤里又加了一勺盐，谁都没再开口；这顿饭把两个人的将来问成了一个没答完的数目。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; relation partner with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1930.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Emil Huber 2在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Emil Huber 2敲开Hedwig的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

- **1930.02** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig和Katharina Moser一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Hedwig去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Hedwig先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1930.11** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.opened-letter/opened-letter-church-steps` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Hedwig请同住的Klara跑一趟，把Berta Huber约到教堂前的台阶上。石台阶还带着晨露，两人各占一级，把原信从头念完；念到“工钱”那处，Berta Huber说那是家里人的意思，不是自己的话。Hedwig把信纸折回原样，至少这一回没有隔着别人传话。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  Hedwig站在书架前抽出一本厚小说，就着门口的光读了半章，Gustav Kral 2在柜台后抬起眼皮看了一眼，没说话。读到要紧处，Hedwig摸出铅笔头把页码记在车票背面，把书插回原处，说下次带够押金再来；白读半章，管理员的眼神却凉了一点。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1931.03** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.fasching-dance/dance-first-polka` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  波尔卡重新响起时，Hedwig走到靠墙的长凳前向Gustav Hofer弯腰。姑母手里的扇子停了停，没说话。入场费和小费一共一克朗，Hedwig把找零塞回内袋；一支舞跳完，Gustav Hofer说了自己住在哪条街，Hedwig把名字和门牌在心里念了两遍。
  _effects: cash -1 (付给乐队的小费与舞会入场费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1931.04** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/stay-home-care` tags: health, family, home
  Hedwig跟工头请了三天假，在灶上熬粥，给Josefine Hofer换药。工钱少了一截，母亲的腿却慢慢消了肿；夜里Hedwig把椅子拼在床边，听见母亲呼吸平稳才合眼。
  _effects: cash -4 (请假三天损失的工钱); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1931.05** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Hedwig跑了一趟医院，把Josefine Hofer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Hedwig回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品); mood -0.06; flag +parent_hospitalized; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

  Hedwig把账本摊在灯下，一页一页念出日期和克朗数，念到买纸那笔时有人 From 门口咳嗽。Elisabeth Lechner低头拨弄围巾穗子，没有替谁说话。桌子上的旧墨水瓶被碰倒，蓝墨慢慢洇进木纹。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000094:child; reputation 0.04; skill_

  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +seeking_work; reputation -0.010000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1932.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜的汤锅外面包着旧毛巾，端进来时还烫手。Hedwig靠着枕头喝，喝一半咳一半。房东太太在走廊里故意大声说，肺病可不是闹着玩的，安娜探出头去，说医生讲了不传染。
  _effects: health 0.02999999999999997 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费); relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner_

- **1933.01** `major-family.marriage-pressure/marriage-pressure.shop-counter-bride` tags: family, marriage, dowry, household, major, lifecycle, partnership
  对方不要嫁妆，只要Hedwig婚后到他们家的杂货铺站柜台。婚礼很简单，Hedwig第二天就系上围裙，工钱直接交给婆婆，婆婆每天把铜板数两遍。晚上Alois Lechner 2给她留了一块面包，什么也没说；柜台下的木凳太高，她踮着脚才能够到秤。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; household; role; role; role; cash -8 (apron, shoes and wedding clothes); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1933.01** `p9-history-public-action.organize-relief/backyard-stove` tags: politics, union, party, activism, public_action, relief, leaflet, task
  Alois Winter 2 的话很简短：名单不能批。Hedwig 把表格折好，转头去搬砖搭灶。Berta Weiss 2 从家里抱来一捆柴，火星溅在围裙上，拍两下就灭了。
  _effects: cash -3 (买柴火和盐); food; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

- **1933.05** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/leave-before-dessert` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  问到后来，Josefine Hofer提起别家有个更稳当的差事。Anna Moser 2把餐巾往桌上一放，说吃好了，先走。两人在楼梯间站住，Anna Moser 2说不是Hedwig的错，可下次再来还得一样坐这张桌子；Hedwig心里松了一点，也知道这顿饭没吃完，事情并没有过去。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

  Hedwig把牌一推，说今晚不借也不玩。Elisabeth Weiss耸耸肩，把桌上的钱收回口袋。Hedwig走到街上，口袋里的硬币只够买一杯咖啡；但经过当铺时，Hedwig没有停。第二天上工，Hedwig的手指还在发痒，像少做了一件本该做的事。
  _effects: cash -1 (一杯咖啡); flag +df18_refused_card_money; mood 0.01999999999999999; knowledge “我拒绝了牌桌上的钱，但手指还记得那种感觉。”_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-divorce/separate-under-one-roof` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation
  Hedwig和Alois Lechner 2把床铺分开，米和煤也各算各的。两人仍住在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，因为谁都没有钱再租一间房；孩子在两床之间来回，三克朗买了隔开的锅碗和煤油，家里从此有两本账，夜里两盏灯先后熄灭。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; mood -0.08; mood -0.08; cash -3 (separate household goods)_

  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:220; reputation -0.06; relation rival with person-marie-huber; flag +denounced_by_known_person; mood -0.04999999999999999_

  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig听见墙里有一声很轻的响。第二天，Marie Huber病倒了；没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: health -0.09000000000000001 (the curse answers a real betrayal); condition; state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 0 -> 1; mood 0.01999999999999999_

  _effects: cash -2 (念错的名字); flag +found_missing_relative_on_list; role; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; knowledge “the missing relative was registered at a transit shelter”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

  Grete Bauer没有再看照片，只让Hedwig把口袋里的东西全放在桌上。银怀表、一张电车票和半包烟；表盖打开时，里面那张小照片上的人不是Hedwig。Elisabeth Lechner在门外想说什么，被另一个警察挡住了。拘留室的门关上时，Hedwig才发现自己一直攥着那张电车票。
