  _effects: cash -2 (上工前把小的送下楼); condition; flag +got_factory_creche_place; role; relation guardian with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005545:child; knowledge “the factory creche takes only night-shift mothers”_

- **1935.02** `ad20-police-stop::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/taken-in` tags: police, legal, street, everyday, obligation, risk, vienna, urban
  Grete Bauer让Hedwig靠墙站好，把口袋里的东西一件件放在地上。钥匙、手帕、半块面包，还有一张写着地址的纸。Grete Bauer把纸对着路灯看了很久，最后说“跟我们走一趟”。
  _effects: cash -2 (a fine after the police stop); reputation -0.030000000000000013; flag +police_stop_record; health -0.020000000000000018 (a night in a holding cell); mood -0.05000000000000002_

- **1935.06** `oj18-mortuary.first-body::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/refused-the-child` tags: death, mortuary, funeral, work, odd_job, night, everyday, vienna
  Hedwig跟着Luise Stein进到后间，看见一具孩子的棺材，尺寸只比鞋盒大一点。Hedwig把毛巾放回盆里，说自己做不了。Luise Stein没有劝，只把当天的工钱数给Hedwig；那枚铜板在口袋里响了一路，像有人在后面跟着。
  _effects: cash 1 (a day's mortuary pay); mood -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; condition; flag +refused_mortuary; knowledge “那具孩子的棺材只比鞋盒大一点，{master}没有劝我留下。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

- **1935.12** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/counts-the-interval` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  Hedwig把两次背叛和两次发烧的日期写在同一张纸上，发现中间都隔着十一天。Hedwig没有告诉任何人，只把纸折成很小的一块，塞进缝衣盒的夹层。那天夜里，Hedwig第一次试着在门口撒盐，动作熟练得让自己停了一下。
  _effects: knowledge “背叛与发烧之间隔着十一天。”; flag +curse_counted; skill; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `ml18-family.fortune-split::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/keep-it-for-the-children` tags: family, money, windfall, inheritance, conflict, dark, vienna, urban
  Lena想把钱拿去盘下街角的小酒馆，Hedwig却把十五克朗直接交到学校，替孩子续了下一年的学费。Lena把餐巾摔在桌上，说这家人永远只会把钱埋进土里。Anna Moser 2没有劝，只把汤碗往Hedwig那边推了推。
  _effects: cash -15 (school fees paid from the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 3 -> 0; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 40 -> 52; relation rival with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 5 -> 10; flag +fortune_kept_for_child; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/new-accuser` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  又一个人站了出来，说自家的母牛在Hedwig经过之后不再产奶。Hedwig没有辩解，因为辩解本身也需要被登记。Hedwig开始绕远路去市场，每月多出几枚铜币的车钱。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 35 -> 45; reputation -0.04000000000000001; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱); mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:p21-scandal-web/the-web-tightens/held` tags: p21-scandal-web, scandal_web, scandal, secret, blackmail, network, leverage, danger
  又过了一年，那几张纸仍锁在Hedwig的抽屉里。街上的问话没有变多，也没有变少；Hedwig在咖啡馆里数了数还能信任的人，发现名单比去年短，但每一个名字都还站得住。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.exposure 63 -> 60; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.loyalty 35 -> 39; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 55 -> 52; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1936.01** `r13-fam.care-sick-child::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::child-care-war/hospital-turned-away` tags: task, care, health, child, fever, night, vienna, urban
  Hedwig抱着Luise Bauer去医院，走廊里躺满缠绷带的士兵。挂号的人看了一眼孩子的脸，说病床先给伤员。Hedwig在门口站到天黑，最后用外套把孩子裹紧，走回家去；路上孩子问，医院为什么不收小孩。
  _effects: cash -1 (第三遍换湿布); flag +hospital_refused_child; knowledge “the hospital reserves beds for wounded soldiers”; health -0.050000000000000044 (the child was turned away from the overcrowded hospital); condition_

- **1936.01** `ml18-luck.strongbox-in-the-wall::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-inflation-ate-them` tags: money, luck, housing, windfall, inflation, ownership, vienna, urban
  铁盒里的纸币已经作废，银扣也只值几个铜板。Katharina Berg把纸币一张张摊在窗台上，说战前的纸现在只能糊墙；Emil Weiss笑得直不起腰，说这盒子在他家墙里藏了二十年，原来是一堆废纸。Hedwig把当票收好，去当铺换回一只银勺，勺柄上刻着外祖母的姓。
  _effects: cash 2 (the silver spoon redeemed with the old pawn ticket); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; flag +worthless_strongbox; knowledge “墙里的旧纸币已经作废，当票还能换回一只银勺。”; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97_

- **1936.08** `ml18-lottery.same-numbers::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-partner-bought-it-secretly` tags: lottery, luck, money, family, regret, windfall, vienna, urban
  Anna Moser 2在开奖前偷偷去了烟草铺，用买菜剩下的钱买了那组号码，把票藏在糖罐后面。号码中了，Anna Moser 2把票放在餐桌上，说本来想等Hedwig生日再讲。Hedwig先是不信，随后笑得把椅子往后推了一截；两个人坐在厨房里数奖金，数到一半又停下来烧水。
  _effects: cash 70 (the lottery prize bought with the grocery money); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:408; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; reputation 0.03; flag +partner_bought_the_ticket; mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.02999999999999997_

- **1937.01** `p21-scandal-auction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-hammer-falls` tags: blackmail, auction, scandal, letters, public, secret, humiliation, high_drama
  第七号信封被放在Alois Winter 2的拍卖台上，封口的火漆还是完整的。Hedwig举了三次牌，最后用六克朗买下自己的旧信；Emil Winter在最后一排没有动，Anna Moser 2在门口等着，手里拿着一只空手提袋。回家路上，Hedwig把信封拆开，发现少了一页。
  _effects: cash -6 (the sealed letters were bought at auction); state_parameter p21-scandal-web.exposure 60 -> 50; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.leverage 34 -> 44; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 52 -> 47; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; flag +bought_back_the_letters; knowledge “信封的封口火漆完整，回家拆开后发现少了一页。”; reputation -0.010000000000000009; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Hedwig的「被疑巫术」到期；相关安排结束。

  又过了一年，那几张纸仍锁在Hedwig的抽屉里。街上的问话没有变多，也没有变少；Hedwig在咖啡馆里数了数还能信任的人，发现名单比去年短，但每一个名字都还站得住。

  Hedwig看见Emil Weiss在楼梯口歇了三回，便把煤筐接过来，一筐一筐抬上三楼。最后一趟，Hedwig的腰在门框上撞了一下，Emil Weiss从柜里摸出一小瓶李子酒，硬倒了一盅。
  _effects: food; health -0.030000000000000027 (把煤筐抬上三楼); flag +carried_neighbour_coal; role; relation friend with person-emil-weiss_

- **1937.02** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household, injury
  Hedwig把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Hedwig的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart); health -0.020000000000000018 (moving in winter)_

- **1937.03** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig数出八赫勒交给Alois Winter 2，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Alois Winter 2说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Hedwig盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; flag +funeral_society_member_

- **1937.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Ferdinand Huber拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Hedwig把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.050000000000000044 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006534_

  Hedwig的「丑闻蛛网」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove p21-scandal-web state:world-vienna-1912:289; flag -scandal_web_active_

- **1938.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-teaching` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, vienna, urban, crisis
  医院派来一名实习医生，身后跟着两个学生。他们把Hedwig的胸口听了个遍，最后请来上级；药费减半，但Hedwig被当成了讲课的病例。学生们走后，实习医生把听诊器在手里捂了很久，才敢再贴上来。
  _effects: cash -4 (reduced fee for a teaching-house call); health 0.030000000000000027 (examined by a teaching team); condition; role; knowledge “教学出诊会有实习医生和学生轮流听诊，最后请上级定方案，药费可以减半。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

- **1938.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sells-the-mourning` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Anton Weiss请Hedwig去家里坐一夜，说只要Hedwig在灵堂待到天亮，那个欠钱的人就会自己上门。Hedwig收了钱，在灵堂坐到天亮，什么也没有发生。一个月后，那个人真的来了，送来一袋面粉；Anton Weiss把面粉分给Hedwig一半，没有解释。
  _effects: cash 3 (替人守灵的酬劳); reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000657:child; flag +sold_mourning_service; condition_

- **1938.08** `df18-dream.water-in-the-cellar::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/move-the-crates` tags: dream, omen, home, water, coincidence, eerie, everyday, vienna
  梦里，Hedwig看见地窖的水漫过脚踝，木箱一只只浮起来。第二天一早，Hedwig把箱子和煤筐搬到床上。中午，楼下的水管裂了，水沿着墙根流进地窖，只浸湿了一只空桶。Anna Bauer说Hedwig运气好，Hedwig没有解释。
  _effects: cash -1 (搬箱子时打碎了一只罐子); relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; knowledge “我梦见地窖进水，第二天水管真的裂了。”; flag +df18_moved_crates_before_flood; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Ferdinand Huber抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Hedwig把床单对折，腾出一半，Ferdinand Huber用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008161_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938, injury
  Hedwig在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Gustav Lechner 2记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Hedwig带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: health -0.12 (beaten in police custody); condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

- **1939.01** `fm17-cult.recruitment::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-child-is-enrolled` tags: faith, cult, recruitment, family, child, school, money, curse
  Josef Kral说晨星会在城北租了一间带院子的房子，白天教孩子识字，晚上替人祷告。Hedwig去看过一次：桌上没有十字架，只有一盏总也不灭的灯。Hedwig在名册上写下Helene Bauer的名字，又付了第一个月的煤钱。
  _effects: state_add fm17-cult-initiate state:world-vienna-1912:535; cash -2 (晨星会学堂的第一个月费用); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005120:child; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +fm17_cult_school_signed; flag +fm17_cult_joined_

- **1939.01** `r13-fam.foster-child::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/child-taken-in` tags: task, care, foster, child, household, orphan, vienna, urban
  Grete Bauer把Maria 2领到门口，说孩子的母亲去了外地做工，几个月没寄钱回来。Hedwig看见孩子脚上那双鞋小了一指，脚趾顶得发白，便让他们进屋。当晚，Hedwig把自家孩子的旧鞋找出来，又往锅里多加了一勺面糊；Maria 2坐在灶边，一直没有开口。
  _effects: cash -3 (门口多出来的一双小鞋); flag +fostered_a_child; role; relation guardian with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006045:child; knowledge “the child’s mother left with an evacuation train”; household_

- **1939.02** `r2-uni.dormitory-bed::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/shared-the-floor` tags: education, housing, students, overcrowding, landlady, vienna, urban, crisis
  Luise Winter把两张床之间的布帘拉开，说“第三个人睡地板，早上卷起来”。Elisabeth Huber把自己的大衣铺在草垫上，算是让出一半。Hedwig数了数房钱，发现自己付的是床位的价，不是地板的价。夜里有人翻身，三个人都醒着。
  _effects: cash -4 (宿舍里的第三张床); condition; flag +shared_dormitory_floor; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1939.04** `oj18-zoo.night::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/broken-tooth` tags: zoo, animals, night, keeper, work, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  夜里十一点，母狮不肯碰桶里的马肉。Hedwig举着煤油灯绕到兽栏后面，看见它右槽牙断了一截，牙龈肿得发亮。Hedwig把肉切碎，又去敲Alois Winter 2的门；第二天兽医来了，Franz Huber 2在晨会上第一次记住Hedwig的名字。
  _effects: organization_found 普拉特动物园夜班队 (other); position 夜班饲养员 @zoo:person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:009604; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:613; skill; reputation 0.03; flag +zoo_night_shift; knowledge “母狮的右槽牙断了，牙龈肿得发亮，夜里不肯吃马肉。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1939.12** `r2-law.savings-passbook::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/smudged-deposit` tags: finance, bank, savings, household, contract, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hedwig把婚礼剩下的钱存进储蓄银行，Alois Winter 2在蓝格子里写下数目，墨水洇开一小块。Hedwig让Alois Winter 2重写，Alois Winter 2说存折不能换页，洇开的部分由储户自己负责。Anna Moser 2在门口等，问利息够不够买一只新锅。
  _effects: cash -8 (存折上的蓝格子); flag +deposited_wedding_savings; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; knowledge “the savings bank will not replace a smudged passbook page”_

  Hedwig带着Helene Bauer去学校登记。孩子的名字被写在名册最后一行，墨水还没有干；回家的路上，Helene Bauer一直捏着那张课程表，Hedwig则在算这个月还能剩下多少煤钱。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 45; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 15 -> 20; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 58 -> 55; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 2 -> 0; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled true -> true; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; cash -1 (the first school books and a slate); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005120:child; mood 0.03_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:fm17-cult-initiate/the-circle-year/signs-again` tags: fm17-cult-initiate, faith, cult, belonging, obedience, doubt, exit, religion
  会长让Hedwig在一张空白纸上签名，说这是“把旧名字交出去”。签完，Hedwig才发现那页纸被夹进了会员名册；这个月的奉献又涨了一克朗，没有人解释为什么。
  _effects: state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.obedience 45 -> 53; state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.exitCost 25 -> 31; cash -1 (上涨的月度奉献); mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Hedwig的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Hedwig想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 14; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 45; mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1940.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Hedwig走到Berggasse 19, Wohnung und Ordination门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1940.04** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  收破烂的把两克朗数在门槛上，顺手敲了敲桌板看木料。Hedwig把铁盒扣好，那里面只有照片和几封旧信。Maria Bauer替Hedwig扶着门，说旧床架值不了几个钱，能脱手就不亏。

### 228 subject events

- **1930.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Grete Huber就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Hermann Kral摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.04999999999999999 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-hermann-kral_

- **1930.05** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Grete Huber在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Grete Huber去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role_

- **1930.12** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Grete Huber和Luise Winter 2谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Grete Huber家，钥匙只在白天给。Luise Winter 2说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Franz Huber 2听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Grete Huber把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); mood 0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1931.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Luise Winter 2把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Grete Huber留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Grete Huber在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06; role_

- **1931.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Gustav Kral 2在门口替Grete Huber捏着帽子，听见里面一声闷哼。弗朗茨把牙扔进搪瓷盘，说瞧这牙根都黑了。收钱时他多要了二十赫勒，算明矾水的钱，说下次别再拖成这样。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1932.04** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  二楼的老木匠指着裂开的踏板骂起来。Grete Huber手里还捏着念珠，Alois Lechner挡在中间说木板本来就朽了。房东太太在门后听着。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; reputation -0.03; mood -0.06_

- **1932.04** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/second-bridge-promise` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  在第二个桥洞下，Marie Huber先停下来，用鞋尖碾一颗烤栗子壳，说下星期天还是这个时候、还是这座桥。Grete Huber点头，掏出四赫勒买了两小袋栗子，两人分着剥，手上都是糖渍；桥洞上的水还在滴，谁也没有提万一明天下雨怎么办。
  _effects: relation partner with person-marie-huber; cash -0.04 (两小袋烤栗子); mood 0.06_

- **1932.08** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Anton排在前面，回头替Grete Huber看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Grete Huber用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03_

- **1932.12** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  手推车每走十几步就要停一下，楼道里的门半开着，有人探头看热闹。Grete Huber把照片铁盒交给Alois Lechner 2抱着，自己低头把最后一箱锅具拖上楼。天擦黑时，新屋里才勉强归置出下脚的地方。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销)_

- **1933.01** `oj18-cinema.reel::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/show-the-reel` tags: cinema, film, projection, night, censorship, work, odd_job, vienna
  Grete Huber没有剪，让那卷新闻片继续走。观众先以为这是加映，直到有人站起来喊“这不是我们买的票”。警察在散场前堵住侧门；Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung说是放映员接错了本，Grete Huber被记了名字。
  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000004; cash -2 (lost wages and paid a fine); condition; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:211; mood -0.03; flag +showed_banned_reel; knowledge “那卷新闻片里有一张被按下去的脸，散场前警察就堵住了侧门。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1933.01** `ad20-shop-opening::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-street-watches` tags: shop, business, opening, everyday, public, obligation, vienna, urban
  Grete Huber把新店的招牌挂上去时，街对面已经站了几个人。第一位客人买了一包针，第二位问能不能赊账，第三位什么也没买，只说“看看”。到傍晚，Grete Huber发现自己记住了每一张脸，却忘了算今天的账。
  _effects: state_add ad20-public-attention state:world-vienna-1912:212; cash 3 (the first day’s takings); reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000175:child; flag +shop_opened; mood 0.04000000000000001; flag +public_attention_active_

- **1933.11** `r3-mig.political-exile::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/met-compatriot-in-exile` tags: migration, exile, politics, safe_house, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  Grete Bauer认出了Grete Huber，在楼梯间低声问家里怎么样。Grete Huber说不知道，出来以后没有收到信。Grete Bauer把半包烟塞过来，说“别在餐厅吃饭，那里有人记名字”。Grete Huber把烟收下，没有告诉他自己的真名。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +met_compatriot_in_exile; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; knowledge “the hotel restaurant is watched”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1934.01** `p22-child-matchmaker::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-album-is-shown-at-school` tags: child, matchmaker, family_album, enemy, warm, ironic, reconciliation, scandal
  孩子把相册带到学校，指着照片说Ferdinand Berg是家里的亲戚。老师把这件事告诉了街坊，街坊又在菜市场讲了一遍；到下午，Grete Huber已经听见三个版本，其中一个说Ferdinand Berg要来分房子。孩子回家时，书包里多了一张同学画的“全家福”。他把那张全家福夹进课本，没有还给同学。
  _effects: state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:209; state_add p22-scandal-engine state:world-vienna-1912:210; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001668:child; reputation -0.03; flag +album_shown_at_school; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; flag +scandal_engine_active_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  两年过去，Grete Huber已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Grete Huber也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 20 -> 30; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 30 -> 40; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:ad20-public-attention/the-attention-cycle/cools` tags: ad20-public-attention, public, attention, reputation, media, everyday, risk, opportunity
  Grete Huber发现最近没有人再提那件事。报纸换了头版，邻居也换了话题；本该轻松，Grete Huber却在买菜时反复整理衣领，好像还在等一个不会来的目光。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-public-attention.attention 40 -> 32; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.sympathy 45 -> 42; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.opportunity 50 -> 48; mood -0.009999999999999995_

- **1934.01** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜的汤锅外面包着旧毛巾，端进来时还烫手。Grete Huber靠着枕头喝，喝一半咳一半。房东太太在走廊里故意大声说，肺病可不是闹着玩的，安娜探出头去，说医生讲了不传染。
  _effects: health 0.02999999999999997 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1934.09** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  二楼的老木匠指着裂开的踏板骂起来。Grete Huber手里还捏着念珠，Berta Bauer 2挡在中间说木板本来就朽了。房东太太在门后听着。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; mood -0.06_

- **1934.09** `state_reaction:public-scandal/gossip` tags: public-scandal, scandal, reputation, social, state, state_reaction, faith, family
  Grete Huber走进咖啡馆时，邻桌的声音低了一拍。没有人当面说什么，但每个人都知道该说什么。
  _effects: mood -0.02999999999999997; reputation -0.03_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006195_

- **1934.09** `r2-law.inflation-savings::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/bought-goods-immediately` tags: finance, inflation, savings, scarcity, market, vienna, urban, crisis
  Grete Huber把存折里的钱全取出来，装进一只面粉袋，直奔市场。Gustav Kral 2说上午的面包价已经不作数，下午还要涨；Grete Huber把袋子往柜台上一倒，纸币散了一地。Lena蹲下来帮忙捡，说这钱昨天还能买一双鞋。
  _effects: cash -18 (一麻袋钞票买一块面包); food; condition; flag +spent_savings_before_prices_rose_again; relation colleague with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “prices are reposted twice a day”_

- **1935.01** `p21-duel-at-dawn::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-second-second` tags: duel, honor, violence, public, scandal, rival, politics, high_drama
  枪声在石壁之间折了一次，才传回来。Karl Kraus往后退了一步，手在胸前按了按，像在找一只掉进口袋里的手套；Gustav Lechner 2跑过去时，石头上已经有一小片深色。Grete Huber站在原地，枪口还冒着烟，直到远处传来巡警的哨声。
  _effects: death (duel wound); state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:274; state_add p21-scandal-web state:world-vienna-1912:275; reputation -0.08; cash -6 (doctor, carriage and silence money); flag +killed_in_duel; condition; knowledge “巡警的哨声从运河桥那边传来时，对方已经不再说话。”; mood -0.08; role; flag -odd_job_apprentice; flag +scandal_web_active_

  Grete Huber在报纸上登了道歉，流言却没有立刻停。第二天，有人在门口放了一束花，也有人把花拿走了；Grete Huber把空花瓶留在窗台上，没有扔。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.heat 40 -> 32; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.reach 45 -> 42; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.shame 35 -> 31; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.leverage 20 -> 25; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Grete Huber的新故事登出来时，旧的那一页也被重新翻到桌面上。Grete Huber发现两件事共用同一个证人，而那个证人上周还来借过盐。

  那个替Grete Huber保管信件的人把副本卖给了报社。Grete Huber在报摊上看见自己的名字，标题不大，旁边却配了一张旧照片；照片里的门牌还认得出来，门后的灯却已经换了主人。

- **1935.01** `state_tick:ad20-public-attention/the-attention-cycle/cools` tags: ad20-public-attention, public, attention, reputation, media, everyday, risk, opportunity
  Grete Huber发现最近没有人再提那件事。报纸换了头版，邻居也换了话题；本该轻松，Grete Huber却在买菜时反复整理衣领，好像还在等一个不会来的目光。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-public-attention.attention 42 -> 34; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.sympathy 37 -> 34; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.opportunity 48 -> 46; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1935.02** `statistical_infection`
  Grete Huber在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1935.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Grete Huber的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1935.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Grete Huber的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1935.07** `ml18-tax.audit-of-the-windfall::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/pay-and-regularize` tags: tax, legal, money, windfall, bureaucracy, comic, vienna, urban
  Grete Bauer把黑皮本翻到Grete Huber那一页，说邮局储蓄的利息没有报，数目不大，但漏了就是漏了。Luise Huber在旁边把计算尺推来推去，最后算出一个八克朗的数目。Grete Huber当天去缴了款，把收据贴在账本最后一页；回家的路上，账本第一次没有在口袋里折着。
  _effects: cash -8 (back taxes and a small fine on the windfall's interest); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 12 -> 4; reputation 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; flag +taxes_paid; knowledge “邮局储蓄的利息也要报税，漏报的八克朗已经补齐。”; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1935.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Grete Huber熬过了这场肺结核，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1935.10** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Grete Huber对Josef Lechner说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Josef Lechner把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Grete Huber的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000708:child_

- **1935.10** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Grete Huber把通知念给Josef Bauer听，Josef Bauer摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Grete Huber去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1936.01** `p21-suspicious-death::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-apothecary-jar` tags: death, poison, investigation, family, dinner, secret, suspicion, high_drama
  Ferdinand Bauer把药瓶举到窗边，瓶底有一圈白色粉末，瓶口却只有两个人的指纹。Emil Winter说那天厨房里只有樱桃馅饼和一瓶止咳糖浆；Katharina Moser忽然想起，死者生前曾把钥匙借给过一个收账的人。Ferdinand Bauer把收账人的名字记在袖口上。
  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 40 -> 20; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; flag +poison_jar_traced; knowledge “药瓶底有一圈白色粉末，瓶口只有两个人的指纹；死者曾把钥匙借给收账的人。”; reputation 0.01999999999999999; cash -1 (the apothecary charged for a written opinion); mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:p21-scandal-web/the-web-tightens/a-strand-snaps` tags: p21-scandal-web, scandal_web, scandal, secret, blackmail, network, leverage, danger
  一个证人搬走了，带走了他那一份记忆。Grete Huber在旧地址门口站了半个下午，回来时发现信箱里多了一张没有署名的明信片，上面只写着“我还记得”。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.exposure 86 -> 94; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.leverage 60 -> 54; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 79 -> 84; reputation -0.01999999999999999; flag +scandal_witness_left_

- **1936.08** `hs18-radio.crystal::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/charge-pennies` tags: radio, neighbor, comic, daily, media, shortage, vienna, urban
  Grete Huber把矿石收音机放在窗台上，邻居们自带小板凳，挤得屋里没有下脚处。有人往桌上放两枚铜币，有人带半块糖。Ferdinand Winter说这比电影院便宜；话音刚落，天线碰到铁栏，声音又没了。
  _effects: cash 2 (neighbors paid a few coins to listen); relation friend with person-ferdinand-winter; reputation 0.020000000000000004; skill_

- **1936.09** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.sell` tags: home, market, food, social
  鞋匠敲后门问有没有热汤，Grete Huber把剩下的半锅倒进他的罐子里，收下两个十字币。Gustav Kral提着空碗上来时，只看见Grete Huber把硬币塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1936.10** `ml18-business.bottle-cap-patent::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sell-the-patent-outright` tags: business, patent, money, windfall, invention, comic, vienna, urban
  破产拍卖会上，Grete Huber用四克朗买下一张没人举牌的瓶盖专利，只因为图纸画得好看。三个月后，Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung的矿泉水厂找上门：那圈软木垫正好能解决他们的漏气问题。Franz Huber 2核对完专利号，Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung当场开出八十克朗的支票。
  _effects: cash 80 (sale of the bottle-cap patent); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:397; reputation 0.06; flag +sold_patent; mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.02999999999999997_

  Franz Lechner把借据铺在桌上，先让Grete Huber看利息，又让Grete Huber看还款日期。Grete Huber用指头一行行划过去，在最后一行停住：若逾期，担保人承担。Franz Lechner说只是走个形式；Grete Huber还是把担保人的名字问清楚，才签了字。
  _effects: cash 6 (a loan was signed with a private creditor); state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:454; flag +loan_signed; mood 0.010000000000000009; knowledge “借据上写着若逾期，担保人承担。”; flag +private_bargain_active_

- **1937.04** `p21-returned-enemy::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-hand-extended` tags: enemy, return, betrayal, revenge, debt, blacklist, history, public
  Alois Kral在普拉特街的咖啡馆里坐到Grete Huber对面，把一张发黄的借据推过来，却没有提还钱。他说自己接了一笔搬运生意，缺一个信得过的人；Grete Huber看见借据背面还留着当年两个人的签名，墨迹已经发灰。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; flag +old_enemy_alliance; cash 2 (an advance on the joint commission); knowledge “借据背面还留着当年两个人的签名，墨迹已经发灰。”; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.leverage 45 -> 57; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 50 -> 40; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.exit 15 -> 10; mood -0.03999999999999998_

  新的债没有走正式渠道，而是被带进了同一间厨房。Grete Huber在桌边听完数目，发现对方连还款日期都替自己算好了。

- **1937.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Grete Huber看Rosa Lechner盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Rosa Lechner愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Grete Huber门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Grete Huber打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-rosa-lechner; food_

- **1938.01** `ml18-family.school-gate-car::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/keep-the-child-and-face-the-talk` tags: family, money, child, school, reputation, dark, vienna, urban
  Grete Huber去参加了家长会，把中奖的票据、邮局收据和那张登了地址的报纸一起放在桌上，说钱是运气，不是偷的。没有人鼓掌，但也没有人再提转学。Anna 2在门外等，书包带子绕在手指上；回家路上，Grete Huber第一次听见孩子主动说起学校的事。
  _effects: cash -5 (the parents' meeting and a small donation to the school); state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 25 -> 33; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001621:child; reputation -0.009999999999999995; flag +child_stayed_in_school; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:p21-scandal-web/the-web-tightens/held` tags: p21-scandal-web, scandal_web, scandal, secret, blackmail, network, leverage, danger
  又过了一年，那几张纸仍锁在Grete Huber的抽屉里。街上的问话没有变多，也没有变少；Grete Huber在咖啡馆里数了数还能信任的人，发现名单比去年短，但每一个名字都还站得住。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.exposure 100 -> 97; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.loyalty 17 -> 21; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 89 -> 86; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.leverage 61 -> 69; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 66 -> 70; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 40 -> 36; mood -0.02999999999999997_

- **1938.02** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼还滴着水，Grete Huber在走廊里分成两份。一份拿进自家，一份搁在Helene 2的门口。傍晚Helene 2来还盘子，盘子里是一小袋面粉。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鱼); food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001419:child_

- **1938.03** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Grete Huber在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Rosa Winter却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Grete Huber把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1938.05** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/quiet-solidarity` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Grete Huber没有发言，只把带来的面包分成几份。散会时，一个不认识的女人把她的地址塞给Grete Huber，说那条街还需要有人继续收会费。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

- **1938.07** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Grete Huber把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Grete Huber走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.010000000000000009 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  对方在一个不合时宜的时刻提起旧事，语气很客气，意思却不客气。Grete Huber听见自己答应得太快，挂断以后才发现手心有一层薄汗。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.leverage 69 -> 77; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 70 -> 74; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 36 -> 32; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/helps_fetch_doctor` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  隔壁Josefine Hofer的小儿子烧到说胡话，额头烫得能烘手。Josefine Hofer在楼道里抹眼泪，说请不起医生。Grete Huber下楼叫了街角药房的伙计，花四十赫勒买了一包退烧药粉，又帮着用湿布敷了一整夜。天亮时孩子退了烧，Josefine Hofer端来一碗热汤面，面底下卧着一个鸡蛋。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买退烧药粉); health -0.020000000000000018 (熬夜照料病人); relation friend with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1939.05** `ad20-rent-renewal::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/a-year-of-quiet` tags: rent, housing, renewal, everyday, obligation, money, vienna, urban
  Elisabeth Huber看完租约，没有加价，也没有提补充条款。Grete Huber把房租数好，放在桌上；Elisabeth Huber数了一遍，又从里面拿出一枚硬币，说“上次多收了”。Grete Huber接过硬币，觉得这一年总算有一件事没有变。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid on time); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; flag +rent_renewed; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.08** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Grete Huber说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Anna Bauer没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Grete Huber听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1940.01** `pc20-family.parent-mistake::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/intervene` tags: parent, child, mistake, second_chance, family, school, repetition, intervention
  Grete Huber在Berta 2出门前拦住他，没有讲自己当年的故事，只把一件具体的事说清楚：那家店里的人不会替他签字，也不会替他坐牢。Berta 2听完，把口袋里的纸掏出来；Helene Huber在门口等着，没有催。
  _effects: state_add pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:598; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001585:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; flag +stopped_child_mistake; knowledge “那家店里的人不会替{child}签字，也不会替{child}坐牢。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; flag +second_chance_open_

- **1940.01** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  二楼的老木匠指着裂开的踏板骂起来。Grete Huber手里还捏着念珠，Alois Winter 2挡在中间说木板本来就朽了。房东太太在门后听着。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; reputation -0.03; mood -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

- **1940.02** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Grete Huber没有立刻答话，先去敲Elisabeth Kral的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Elisabeth Kral提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Grete Huber把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1940.10** `hs18-radio.rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/switch-it-off` tags: radio, blackout, rumor, panic, neighbor, politics, vienna, urban
  Grete Huber伸手关掉收音机，说电池要留给夜里的急救。Grete Bauer不同意，两人在黑暗里摸到同一只旋钮。最后谁也没有再开；楼道里只剩邻居的怀表在走，每一下都像在催人下楼。
  _effects: state_add hs18-blackout-rumor state:world-vienna-1912:681; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +silenced_radio_during_blackout_

- **1941.01** `pc20-friend.betrayal-again::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/expose` tags: friend, betrayal, second_chance, list, denunciation, trust, consequence, vienna
  Grete Huber把名单交给Gustav Lechner 2，没有添一句解释。Alois Lechner 3在走廊里拦住Grete Huber，问是不是一定要这样；Grete Huber说名单上不止自己一个名字，然后从他身边走过去，脚步比想象中稳。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 35 -> 43; reputation 0.03; flag +exposed_old_friend; knowledge “名单上不止自己一个名字，{subject}把它交给了{official}。”_

- **1941.01** `state_expire:ad20-private-bargain` tags: ad20-private-bargain, private, bargain, favor, debt, trust, leverage, exit

  _effects: state_remove ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:454; flag -private_bargain_active_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/kept-word` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Grete Huber把答应过的事又做了一遍：按时出现，把欠下的小钱还掉，没有在旧话上添新的解释。对方没有鼓掌，只把杯子往Grete Huber这边推了推；这一次的沉默比上次轻。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 50 -> 56; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 43 -> 38; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 60 -> 64; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.08** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Emil Weiss用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Grete Huber才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; flag +parish_registered_

- **1941.11** `r2-leisure.secret-society::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::secret-society-crisis/recopied-the-list` tags: leisure, secret_society, conspiracy, police, trust, vienna, urban, crisis
  Grete Huber把名单重新抄了一遍，把真名换成住址，把住址换成母亲的名字。Hermann Berg问这样还能找到人吗，Grete Huber说能，只要我们还活着。抄到最后一个名字时，楼下的警棍又敲了三下水管。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; condition; flag +recopied_society_list; role; skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; knowledge “the list now uses mothers names instead of members names”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1941.12** `bc18-inherit.parrot-condition::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/parrot-dies` tags: inheritance, family, condition, absurd, death, legal, quiet, vienna
  鹦鹉死在第二个冬天。窗缝里灌进来的风把笼布吹开，它缩在食盆旁边，像一团被揉皱的灰纸。Franz Huber 2说条件没有完成，十二克朗归了姑妈的教子；Grete Huber把鸟埋在院子里，留下那只笼子。春天，笼子里长出一丛野草，Grete Huber没有拔。
  _effects: cash -2 (鹦鹉的药费和笼布); reputation -0.009999999999999995; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; condition; flag +parrot_died_before_the_will; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

  Grete Huber在Berta 2出门前拦住他，没有讲自己当年的故事，只把一件具体的事说清楚：那家店里的人不会替他签字，也不会替他坐牢。Berta 2听完，把口袋里的纸掏出来；Grete Stein在门口等着，没有催。

  Grete Huber把听到的消息带到街角，换了两支蜡烛和一包盐。不到半小时，杂货店门口排起队；巡街的人赶来问消息从哪里来，Grete Huber说收音机里，大家都听见了。
  _effects: state_add hs18-blackout-rumor state:world-vienna-1912:816; cash 1 (candles and salt for the news); food; reputation -0.020000000000000004; flag +spread_radio_rumor_

- **1942.02** `state_reaction:pc20-second-chance/public-doubt` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  旧事被人在街上重新提起，Grete Huber的名字和另一件事绑在一起。有人替Grete Huber辩解，也有人只笑了一声；Grete Huber回家后把当天的收据一张张摊开，像在给自己准备证词。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 35 -> 43; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 50 -> 46; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung把Grete Huber的证明信从头看到尾，最后说店里不缺人。Grete Huber站起来时，椅子在地板上刮出一声长响；Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung送到门口，又补了一句“以后有机会”。街上正在下雨，Grete Huber把证明信折成四折，塞进内袋。
  _effects: flag +interview_rejected; mood -0.030000000000000027; cash -1 (tram fare to the interview); knowledge “面试后，店主说店里不缺人。”_

- **1942.10** `df18-luck.borrowed-at-the-card-table::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/marked-deck` tags: luck, debt, bargain, gambling, obligation, night, supernatural, vienna
  Grete Huber赢了三克朗，起身时才发现牌背有指甲划出的记号。Grete Weiss 2笑着把牌收进袖子里，说这叫“借来的手气”。Grete Huber没有把钱退回去，只把那张有记号的牌偷偷带走。第二天，Grete Huber在当铺门口看见Grete Weiss 2正在和一个收旧货的人分钱。
  _effects: cash 3 (牌桌上赢来的三克朗); state_add df18-borrowed-luck state:world-vienna-1912:817; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119; flag +df18_marked_deck; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119; flag +df18_luck_borrowed; knowledge “我得到了一点不该白拿的好运，代价还没有说清。”_

- **1943.01** `sm18-object.tram-lost::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/says-it-was-only-metal` tags: object, loss, child, tram, family, everyday, vienna, urban
  Grete Huber摸了摸空口袋，说那不过是块旧铜，丢了就丢了。晚上，Rosa 2睡着以后，Grete Huber又去电车站走了一趟，沿着白天的路线低头看每一块石板。
  _effects: state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:815; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004014:child; flag +searched_the_tram_route_at_night; flag +something_is_missing_

  Grete Huber晚了半个钟头，进门时先解释路上的电车，又解释口袋里的收据。对方没有追问，只把已经凉掉的汤端回厨房；Grete Huber听见锅盖碰在灶台上，知道这次不是天气的问题。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 46 -> 39; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 43 -> 51; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.debt 30 -> 33; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  第六个月，债主在楼梯口拦住Grete Huber，说利息不能总按原来的算法。Grete Huber看着对方手里的账本，忽然发现那上面的墨迹比上次新，数字也比上次多。
  _effects: cash -2 (利息被重新计算); state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 15 -> 21; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 15 -> 10; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Elisabeth Huber把铁盒递过来时，手没有松开。他说女儿在疗养院，下个月要交一笔钱。Grete Huber看了看那只盒子，又看了看墙上新开的洞，说墙是他敲开的，盒子也该归他。Luise Weiss想说什么，最后只说了一句“你们商量好了就行”。
  _effects: reputation 0.04; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; flag +gave_strongbox_to_mason; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1943.01** `state_reaction:df18-borrowed-luck/luck-windfall` tags: df18-borrowed-luck, luck, debt, obligation, superstition, risk, bargain, transfer
  Grete Huber在路边捡到一枚硬币，或是在旧外套里摸出一张没花完的票子。高兴只持续了很短；Grete Huber随即想起，那本账还在别人手里。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 10 -> 15; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 31 -> 36; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  肉商的账本上少了二十公斤马肉，Berta Bauer 3要Grete Huber签字认账。Grete Huber知道是夜班门房把肉转卖给了普拉特的香肠摊，但门房是Berta Bauer 3的小舅子。Grete Huber不签，第二天被调去扫兽栏；臭味洗了三遍还在指甲缝里。
  _effects: reputation -0.029999999999999992; cash -1 (lost the night-shift supplement); mood -0.040000000000000036; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:885; flag +zoo_night_shift; flag +refused_meat_ledger; knowledge “少掉的二十公斤马肉被夜班门房卖给了香肠摊，门房是园长的亲戚。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

  Franz Huber 2在广场上摆了一排椅子，说昨晚的破坏者不出来，就按名单抓人。Grete Huber看见自己的名字在第五个，Franz Huber 3在第七个。Alois Winter 2站在椅子旁，手里的帽子捏得变了形；Grete Huber走过去坐下，椅子腿在石板上刮出一声。

  Grete Huber把账本合上，说运气已经用掉了，债主爱找谁找谁。Emil没有争，只在门口留了一枚旧硬币；Grete Huber把它放进抽屉，第二天抽屉里多了两张催款单，字迹和硬币上的年份一样旧。

  Grete Huber把听到的消息带到街角，换了两支蜡烛和一包盐。不到半小时，杂货店门口排起队；巡街的人赶来问消息从哪里来，Grete Huber说收音机里，大家都听见了。
  _effects: state_add hs18-blackout-rumor state:world-vienna-1912:947; cash 1 (candles and salt for the news); food; reputation -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

- **1944.11** `r2-law.eviction-hearing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/repair-order` tags: law, eviction, housing, court, tenement, vienna, urban, crisis
  Grete Huber没有争欠租，而是把一叠照片摊在桌上：天花板的裂缝、发霉的墙、冬天结冰的窗。Alois Winter 2问Berta Hofer修过没有，Berta Hofer说修过；Grete Huber拿出三封挂号信，都是催修的回执。Alois Winter 2判Berta Hofer先修房，欠租暂缓，修完再开庭。Alois Lechner 3在走廊里说，这招比哭有用。
  _effects: cash -6 (驱逐听证的十五分钟); condition; flag +court_ordered_repairs_before_eviction; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65; knowledge “three registered repair requests were ignored”_

- **1945.01** `oj18-zoo.escape::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/chimp-on-the-prater` tags: zoo, animals, escape, night, blame, work, odd_job, vienna
  黑猩猩“卡斯帕”用一根弯钉挑开第二道门，顺着排水沟跑进普拉特。Grete Huber提着一桶苹果追到旋转木马后面，看见它坐在售票亭顶上，正把一张张票根往嘴里塞。Hedwig赶来时，Grete Huber已经用苹果把它引进了送货马车。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 28 -> 36; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 32 -> 37; cash -1 (apples used to lure the chimpanzee); relation colleague with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; flag +monkey_returned; knowledge “卡斯帕会用弯钉挑门闩，苹果比网子管用。”_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/a-receipt-in-the-lining` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  半年过去，Grete Huber又翻了一遍那只装旧纽扣的铁盒，在盒盖夹层里摸到一小片发黄的收据。上面的日期和那件东西最后一次出现的那天对得上，但字迹已经淡得只剩半个印章。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 8 -> 14; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 8 -> 6; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Grete Huber把答应过的事又做了一遍：按时出现，把欠下的小钱还掉，没有在旧话上添新的解释。对方没有鼓掌，只把杯子往Grete Huber这边推了推；这一次的沉默比上次轻。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 45 -> 51; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 45 -> 40; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 45 -> 49; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1945.04** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/read-and-decline` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Grete Huber把传单读了两遍，记下上面的地址，却没有去。第二天照常上工；午休时听见两个工友低声谈论同一场集会。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1945.05** `r3-mig.deportation-appeal::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/appeal-dismissed-no-new-facts` tags: migration, deportation, law, appeal, papers, vienna, urban, postwar
  复核结果下来，申诉驳回，理由是“未提出新事实”。Alois Winter 2把新的离境日期念了一遍，Josefine Hofer站在楼梯口，说愿意作证Grete Huber在这里住了九年。Berta Huber把证词夹进档案，说“九年不算新事实”。
  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999983; condition; flag +appeal_dismissed; relation friend with person-josefine-hofer; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; knowledge “nine years of residence is not new evidence”_

- **1945.09** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.cold-day` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  孩子放学回来搓着手说冷，Grete Huber把仅有的半捧煤末压进炉底，火苗细得像灯芯。她没接Grete Weiss 2递来的煤块，只说等明天煤店开门。屋里哈气成雾，晚饭的土豆是凉的。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (屋里整天没生旺火，受冻); mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119_

- **1945.11** `oj18-zoo.night::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/child-at-the-bear-pit` tags: zoo, animals, night, keeper, work, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  一个男孩翻过熊池的矮栏去捡帽子。Grete Huber没有喊，怕惊动母熊，只提着装苹果的桶绕到下风处，把桶敲得咚咚响。母熊转身时，孩子被Alois Lechner 3一把拽了上来；Grete Huber的腿在后来的很多年里还会在梦里发软。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (the bear-pit rescue left the legs shaking); reputation 0.06; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; cash 2 (reward from the child's parents); state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:996; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +saved_child_at_zoo; knowledge “母熊被苹果桶引开时，孩子已经挂在{keeper}的胳膊上了。”_

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Grete Huber的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Grete Huber想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 38 -> 22; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 65 -> 55; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1946.07** `r3-war.refugee-train::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::repatriation-train/filled-repatriation-form` tags: war, refugee, train, station, migration, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton Weiss的村子已经划到另一面旗子下，Berta Huber说这趟车只到边界。Grete Huber帮她把行李搬下车，站台上全是等着换乘的人，孩子坐在箱子上吃冷土豆。Gustav Lechner 2过来发登记表，Anton Weiss不识字，Grete Huber替她填了名字。
  _effects: flag +filled_repatriation_form; role; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000657:child; knowledge “the border has moved across her village”_

- **1946.09** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Grete Huber把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Anton Weiss站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Grete Huber手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; reputation 0.03_

- **1946.10** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  Grete Huber跪在冰冷的亚麻油毡上念完三遍圣母经。Luise Weiss 2端来一碗热汤，说楼下老太太咳了一整夜，问明天能不能替她捎药。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1947.01** `oj18-zoo.escape::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/hid-the-chimp` tags: zoo, animals, escape, night, blame, work, odd_job, vienna
  马戏班老板Elisabeth Bauer在普拉特门口拦住Grete Huber，说愿意出三十克朗买那只黑猩猩，还缺一个懂它脾气的驯养人。Grete Huber没有答应，也没有报告；夜里，Grete Huber把卡斯帕藏进废弃的鸟舍，每天多带一份面包。
  _effects: cash -1 (bread for the hidden chimpanzee); relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 18 -> 26; state_add oj18-workplace-legend state:world-vienna-1912:1037; flag +hid_chimp; knowledge “卡斯帕藏在废弃鸟舍里，听见面包纸响就会从梁上下来。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; role; flag +workplace_legend_

- **1947.01** `state_expire:sm18-missing-object` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Grete Huber的「一件说不清下落的东西」到期；相关安排结束。

  Grete Huber晚了半个钟头，进门时先解释路上的电车，又解释口袋里的收据。对方没有追问，只把已经凉掉的汤端回厨房；Grete Huber听见锅盖碰在灶台上，知道这次不是天气的问题。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 57 -> 50; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 35 -> 43; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.debt 35 -> 38_

  Ferdinand Bauer 2说Maria 2这学期退步，是因为家里晚上太吵。Grete Huber想说自己夜里在做计件活，话到嘴边又咽回去；Maria 2站在走廊尽头，书包带子断了一边。回家的路上，Grete Huber把书包带打了个结，谁也没有提老师的话。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 21 -> 17; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006045:child; cash -1 (extra exercise books after the meeting); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137_

- **1947.11** `r3-war.liberation-flag::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::liberation-anniversary/refused-election-pressure` tags: war, liberation, occupation, flag, aftermath, vienna, urban, postwar
  解放后的第一场选举，Grete Huber在投票站门口遇见Berta Huber。Berta Huber已经换上西装，问Grete Huber投谁；Grete Huber说“投那个没在占领期开过会的”。Berta Huber笑了笑，把一支烟递过来，Grete Huber没接。
  _effects: flag +refused_partisan_election_pressure; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; knowledge “the partisan is backing a candidate”_

- **1948.01** `ad20-child-teacher::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-teacher-has-a-question` tags: family, child, school, teacher, everyday, public, vienna, urban
  Helene Winter 2问Grete Huber，Maria 2最近为什么总在课上打瞌睡。Grete Huber说家里晚上在赶一批活；Helene Winter 2点点头，说“别让孩子熬太晚”。Grete Huber应了一声，心里却在算那批活还能不能按时交。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 48 -> 50; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +teacher_asked_homework_

  又有人来问那个老故事。Grete Huber把烟按灭，从头讲了一遍，连哪天下雨、哪盏灯坏了都没有改。听的人散了以后，学徒在门口说“跟上次一样”；Grete Huber知道，至少这一版还能再撑几年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-workplace-legend.legend 20 -> 23; state_parameter oj18-workplace-legend.truth 55 -> 57; state_parameter oj18-workplace-legend.toldCount 0 -> 1; reputation 0.020000000000000004_

  两年过去，Grete Huber已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Grete Huber也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 22 -> 32; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 26 -> 36; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1948.10** `r3-war.war-memorial::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/added-unofficial-names` tags: war, memorial, grief, stone, community, vienna, urban, postwar
  Grete Huber提议把自杀者和逃兵的名字也刻上去，Berta Bauer 3说“名单是上面定的”。Maria Lechner没有争，只把一束野花放在碑脚；Luise Winter低声说，他可以在碑背刻一行小字，不收费。那天夜里，Grete Huber看见碑背多了三个名字，刻得很浅。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +added_unofficial_names_to_memorial; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; knowledge “three names are carved on the back of the memorial”_

- **1948.11** `ml18-lottery.same-numbers::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-partner-bought-it-secretly` tags: lottery, luck, money, family, regret, windfall, vienna, urban
  Marie Huber在开奖前偷偷去了烟草铺，用买菜剩下的钱买了那组号码，把票藏在糖罐后面。号码中了，Marie Huber把票放在餐桌上，说本来想等Grete Huber生日再讲。Grete Huber先是不信，随后笑得把椅子往后推了一截；两个人坐在厨房里数奖金，数到一半又停下来烧水。
  _effects: cash 70 (the lottery prize bought with the grocery money); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:1148; relation partner with person-marie-huber; reputation 0.03; flag +partner_bought_the_ticket; mood 0.09999999999999998; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1948.11** `absurd15-stage.cast-the-king::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/cast-as-king` tags: performance, theatre, power, irony, work, pretend, vienna, urban
  Josef Kral把一顶掉漆的铅皮王冠扣在Grete Huber头上，说不用演得像国王，只要像一个人突然被允许坐下。首演那天，Grete Huber在台上忘了两句台词，观众却以为那是停顿。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:1149; cash 2 (首演酬劳); role; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-142; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

  Grete Huber在Luise 2出门前拦住他，没有讲自己当年的故事，只把一件具体的事说清楚：那家店里的人不会替他签字，也不会替他坐牢。Luise 2听完，把口袋里的纸掏出来；Helene Huber在门口等着，没有催。
  _effects: state_add pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:1147; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003582:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; knowledge “那家店里的人不会替{child}签字，也不会替{child}坐牢。”; flag +second_chance_open_

- **1949.04** `r2-law.rent-contract::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/signed-with-repair-clause` tags: law, rent, contract, housing, tenement, vienna, urban, postwar
  Helene Winter 2把租约摊在桌上，第十三条写着“小修由房客自理”，旁边一栏是六克朗的钥匙押金。Grete Huber问屋顶漏雨算不算小修，Helene Winter 2说看漏多大。Gustav 2在旁边插嘴，说去年冬天水从三楼一直滴到院子里；Helene Winter 2把笔递过来，说签了才给钥匙。Grete Huber数出六克朗，在第十三条下面签了名。
  _effects: cash -6 (租约上的第十三条); flag +signed_lease_with_repair_clause; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; knowledge “clause thirteen makes the tenant pay for minor repairs”_

- **1949.09** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.quarrel` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  铁桶磕在石沿上，水晃出来打湿了Grete Huber的裙角。Mitzi Moser的声音拔高，说轮到她就是轮到她。Grete Huber咬着嘴没再喊，把盆往自己门前拖，湿石板上一道水痕。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

- **1950.01** `ml18-family.school-gate-car::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/keep-the-child-and-face-the-talk` tags: family, money, child, school, reputation, dark, vienna, urban
  Grete Huber去参加了家长会，把中奖的票据、邮局收据和那张登了地址的报纸一起放在桌上，说钱是运气，不是偷的。没有人鼓掌，但也没有人再提转学。Maria 2在门外等，书包带子绕在手指上；回家路上，Grete Huber第一次听见孩子主动说起学校的事。
  _effects: cash -5 (the parents' meeting and a small donation to the school); state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 19 -> 27; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006045:child; reputation -0.010000000000000009; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/kept-word` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Grete Huber把答应过的事又做了一遍：按时出现，把欠下的小钱还掉，没有在旧话上添新的解释。对方没有鼓掌，只把杯子往Grete Huber这边推了推；这一次的沉默比上次轻。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 50 -> 56; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 35 -> 30; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 60 -> 64; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `state_expire:stage-sovereign` tags: stage-sovereign, performance, theatre, pretend, status, irony, power, no-real-authority
  Grete Huber的「舞台上的君主」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:1149; flag -stage_sovereign_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/kept-word` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Grete Huber把答应过的事又做了一遍：按时出现，把欠下的小钱还掉，没有在旧话上添新的解释。对方没有鼓掌，只把杯子往Grete Huber这边推了推；这一次的沉默比上次轻。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 56 -> 62; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 30 -> 25; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 64 -> 68; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.02** `em18-late-bloom.shop-sign::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/a-repair-bench` tags: elderhood, late_life, shop, craft, work, reinvention, neighborhood, vienna
  修鞋铺隔壁的小门面空了出来，Grete Huber用两克朗付了押金，把旧车床搬进去。招牌是请街角的老漆匠写的，只写了“修理”两个字；第一天来了三个人，一个修伞，一个修锁，还有一个只是进来躲雨。
  _effects: state_add em18-late-bloom state:world-vienna-1912:1267; cash -2 (the deposit on the narrow shop); skill; reputation 0.01999999999999999; flag +late_bloom_active_

- **1951.02** `sm18-legend.well-light::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-light-comes-back` tags: legend, well, light, neighborhood, uncanny, unresolved, vienna, urban
  Grete Huber把镜片扔掉以后，第二个星期井口又亮了。这次碎的是另一块，边缘还带着新茬。Josef Kral说别再去了；Grete Huber站在院门口看了很久，直到那点白光被雾吞掉。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; knowledge “井口又出现了一块新的碎镜片，边缘带着新茬”; flag +the_well_light_came_back_

- **1951.02** `kernel-health.chronic-pain::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pain-electric-belt` tags: health, pain, elderhood, morphine, household, vienna, urban, postwar
  报上广告说电腰带能治百病。Grete Huber花了二十五先令买回来，绑在腰上，第一天就被烧出两排水泡；推销员已经去了下一座城市，地址是邮局信箱。他把腰带剪断，铜片和弹簧滚了一地。
  _effects: cash -25 (mail-order electric belt advertised as a cure-all); health -0.050000000000000044 (burns from an electric quack device); condition; role; knowledge “报纸上的电腰带治不了膝盖，第一天就能烧出水泡，推销员只留一个邮局信箱。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-146_

- **1951.02** `vienna-elder-care.winter-stairs/stay-indoors` tags: elderhood, health, family, social, injury
  Grete Huber看了看结冰的台阶，退回屋里，用剩下的面粉和水糊了一顿面糊。
  _effects: food; mood -0.040000000000000036; health -0.020000000000000018 (缺乏新鲜食物); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1951.03** `eh19-bridge.question-arrives::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/ask-a-neighbor-first` tags: elderhood, testimony, history, memory, bureaucracy, ordinary, vienna, urban
  Grete Huber没有立刻填，拿着问话单去敲隔壁的门。开门的人比她年轻二十岁，说那年还没搬来；两个人站在楼道里把纸看了一遍，最后只确认了门牌号。回家后，Grete Huber把问话单压在糖罐下面。
  _effects: state_add eh19-late-testimony state:world-vienna-1912:1341; flag +late_testimony_neighbor; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +late_testimony_active_

- **1951.03** `state_reaction:eh19-late-testimony/official-return` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  法院的人又来了，这次带着上一回的笔录。Grete Huber看见自己的句子被打印成另一种语气，旁边还留着书记员画的一个问号；对方说只改一处，改完就可以回家。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 20 -> 28; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.danger 10 -> 15; mood -0.030000000000000027_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018626_

- **1951.03** `em18-late-bloom.shop-sign::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-lease-is-refused` tags: elderhood, late_life, shop, craft, work, reinvention, neighborhood, vienna
  房东把Grete Huber的押金推回来，说这年纪要有担保人，还要一次付清半年房租。Grete Huber在门口站了一会儿，把写好的招牌尺寸纸折起来；回家路上，Grete Huber经过那扇空门面，里面已经挂上了成衣铺的布帘。
  _effects: cash -1 (the deposit was returned after a day); mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +lease_refused; knowledge “房东要求半年房租一次付清，才肯把空门面租给六十岁以上的人。”_

- **1951.06** `p21-elder-testimony::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-name-is-restored` tags: elderhood, testimony, death, family_secret, history, court, public, suspicious_death
  Katharina Weiss把那份旧证词从档案袋里抽出来，放在窗边的桌上。Grete Huber用蓝铅笔改了三处，把死者的名字从“无名”改回“卡尔·魏斯”；Berta Lechner在旁边把每一页按顺序排好，Maria Lechner 2站在门口，手里的帽子一直没有戴上。签完字时，窗外正好有人敲响下午四点的钟。
  _effects: state_remove eh19-late-testimony state:world-vienna-1912:1341; state_add p21-fortune-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:1343; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000552:child; reputation 0.05000000000000002; flag +dead_name_restored; knowledge “蓝铅笔改了三处，死者的名字从“无名”改回“卡尔·魏斯”。”; cash 2 (the court refunded part of the costs); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108; flag -late_testimony_active; flag +fortune_reversal_active_

- **1951.06** `state_reaction:em18-late-bloom/public-eyes` tags: em18-late-bloom, elderhood, late_life, reinvention, love, work, legacy, risk
  有人在报纸的社交栏里看见Grete Huber的名字，连旧住址都写对了。第二天，杂货店老板多问了一句，Grete Huber笑着把话岔开；回家后，Grete Huber把窗帘拉上，又打开。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.publicness 20 -> 26; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018630_

- **1951.06** `state_reaction:em18-late-bloom/a-death-in-the-household` tags: em18-late-bloom, elderhood, late_life, reinvention, love, work, legacy, risk
  家里少了一个人以后，Grete Huber把新生活里最花钱的那一项划掉了。不是因为不想，而是因为账单、葬礼和空出来的房间都需要有人管；Grete Huber把划掉的那一行又抄到另一张纸上。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 30 -> 40; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.stamina 55 -> 51; mood -0.050000000000000044_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018630_

- **1951.11** `pc20-testimony.new-caption::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/no-correction` tags: elderhood, testimony, newspaper, photograph, public_reversal, memory, family, vienna
  Otto Winter说版面已经排好，改一个字要挪三栏。Grete Huber没有争，只把报纸带回家，用铅笔在照片下面画了一道横线；Josef Lechner问那是什么意思，Grete Huber说意思是这张照片还没有说完。
  _effects: state_add pc20-public-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:1342; relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter; flag +caption_unchanged; knowledge “照片下面被画了一道铅笔横线，意思是这张照片还没有说完。”; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +public_reversal_active_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/small-relapse` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Grete Huber晚了半个钟头，进门时先解释路上的电车，又解释口袋里的收据。对方没有追问，只把已经凉掉的汤端回厨房；Grete Huber听见锅盖碰在灶台上，知道这次不是天气的问题。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 62 -> 55; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 25 -> 33; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.debt 30 -> 33; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:em18-late-bloom/late-life-year/takes-root` tags: em18-late-bloom, elderhood, late_life, reinvention, love, work, legacy, risk
  又一年过去，Grete Huber把新生活的开销记在一本旧账簿背面：房租、药、车票，还有一件为公开场合买的衬衫。事情没有变轻，但Grete Huber已经知道每周哪几天有力气，哪几天只适合坐在窗边。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.nerve 55 -> 59; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.stamina 51 -> 48; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 40 -> 38; cash 1 (small receipts from the new work); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:pc20-public-reversal/file-moves/the-file-disappears` tags: pc20-public-reversal, public_reversal, exoneration, accusation, testimony, proof, pressure, support
  Grete Huber按约定时间去调卷，窗口的人说编号不对，又说管库的人病了。走廊尽头的门开着，里面只剩空架子和一层灰；Grete Huber把手伸进去，摸到的是一截断掉的麻绳。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.proof 40 -> 32; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.danger 45 -> 51; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.pressure 60 -> 65; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:p21-fortune-reversal/the-crowd-recounts/the-scaffold-shakes` tags: p21-fortune-reversal, fortune_reversal, fortune, reversal, inheritance, ruin, public, witnesses
  一笔该到的钱没有到。Grete Huber在账房里坐到天黑，听见隔壁把算盘珠子拨得很快；回家时，门口已经有人等着，手里拿着一份折过三次的账单。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.height 60 -> 52; state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.fragility 50 -> 58; state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.debt 10 -> 15; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +fortune_scaffold_shook_

- **1952.02** `eh19-bridge.question-arrives::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/put-it-in-the-box` tags: elderhood, testimony, history, memory, bureaucracy, ordinary, vienna, urban
  Grete Huber把问话单折成四折，塞进装旧证件的铁盒。盒子已经满了，她挑出一张一九三八年的配给证放在上面，又放回去；第二天，区里又寄来一张，同样的纸，同样的第三个问题。
  _effects: state_add eh19-late-testimony state:world-vienna-1912:1442; knowledge “区里第二次寄来同样的问题，说明他们还在查。”; flag +late_testimony_second_letter; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +late_testimony_active_

- **1952.02** `state_reaction:pc20-public-reversal/someone-defends` tags: pc20-public-reversal, public_reversal, exoneration, accusation, testimony, proof, pressure, support
  一个旧同事在公开场合替Grete Huber说了话，没有把Grete Huber说成无辜，只把当年那份名单的次序讲清楚。第二天，另一个人在面包店门口拦住Grete Huber，说自己一直觉得哪里不对。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.support 30 -> 37; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.proof 32 -> 36; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019477_

- **1952.06** `vienna-elder-bereavement.grave-visit/grave-visit.turn-back` tags: elderhood, bereavement, family, health, memory, housing
  面包铺的炉子就在街角，Grete Huber买了刚出炉的一个，用纸包着，烫手。他和Gustav Stein站在檐下吃完，谁也没再提墓园的事。
  _effects: food; cash -0.2 (买面包和去程车钱); mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_

- **1953.01** `p21-elder-testimony::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-witness-dies-before-signing` tags: elderhood, testimony, death, family_secret, history, court, public, suspicious_death
  Rosa Lechner在签字前一夜死在公寓里，桌上放着半杯水和一只没有盖上的药瓶。Gustav Lechner 2把死亡证明夹进卷宗，说“证人不能签了”；Josef Lechner在走廊里听见这句话，手里的伞掉在地上。Grete Huber坐在窗边，看见运河上的雾把对岸的灯一盏盏吃掉。
  _effects: death (sudden illness); state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:1443; state_add p21-scandal-web state:world-vienna-1912:1444; relation_end rival with person-rosa-lechner; flag +witness_died_before_signing; knowledge “桌上放着半杯水和一只没有盖上的药瓶，死亡证明被夹进卷宗。”; reputation -0.03; mood -0.06999999999999984; flag -public_reversal_active; flag -late_bloom_active; flag -fortune_reversal_active; flag +scandal_web_active_

  成年子女来了一趟，把账本翻到最后一页，说这年纪不该再折腾。Grete Huber没有争，只把钥匙从桌上收进围裙口袋；那天晚上，新生活仍按原计划继续，只是没有再提。

  又过了半年，Grete Huber把证词在心里走了一遍。顺序还在，但每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人补上的颜色；Grete Huber把最重要的日期写在火柴盒背面，睡前又看了一次。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.memory 50 -> 53; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 40 -> 38; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 45 -> 47; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1953.03** `p21-elder-testimony::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-name-is-restored` tags: elderhood, testimony, death, family_secret, history, court, public, suspicious_death
  Berta Huber把那份旧证词从档案袋里抽出来，放在窗边的桌上。Grete Huber用蓝铅笔改了三处，把死者的名字从“无名”改回“卡尔·魏斯”；Berta Lechner在旁边把每一页按顺序排好，Alois Huber站在门口，手里的帽子一直没有戴上。签完字时，窗外正好有人敲响下午四点的钟。
  _effects: state_remove eh19-late-testimony state:world-vienna-1912:1442; state_add p21-fortune-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:1532; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000552:child; reputation 0.04999999999999999; knowledge “蓝铅笔改了三处，死者的名字从“无名”改回“卡尔·魏斯”。”; cash 2 (the court refunded part of the costs); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-128; flag -late_testimony_active; flag +fortune_reversal_active_

- **1953.03** `state_reaction:raising-child/child-death-in-the-household` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  孩子的名字从学校的名册上被划掉以后，家里的碗还多摆了一副。Grete Huber没有把它收起来，直到有一天发现碗沿积了灰，才拿去洗。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 55 -> 25; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 57; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +lost_child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:020351_

- **1953.11** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Grete Huber的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Grete Huber家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:020405_

- **1953.12** `em18-late-bloom.rent-increase::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/moves-to-the-courtyard` tags: elderhood, late_life, housing, rent, shop, money, risk, vienna
  Grete Huber把工具装进两只木箱，搬到后院一间更小的棚屋。新地方没有临街的窗，但房租少了一半；Gustav Hofer 2来帮忙，说这里冬天会冷，Grete Huber说先把招牌挂上，冷了再想办法。
  _effects: cash -2 (the move and the new sign); state_parameter em18-late-bloom.publicness 32 -> 22; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.nerve 55 -> 60; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117; flag +moved_workshop; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117_

- **1954.01** `pc20-testimony.new-caption::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/no-correction` tags: elderhood, testimony, newspaper, photograph, public_reversal, memory, family, vienna
  Emil Huber 2说版面已经排好，改一个字要挪三栏。Grete Huber没有争，只把报纸带回家，用铅笔在照片下面画了一道横线；Josef Lechner问那是什么意思，Grete Huber说意思是这张照片还没有说完。
  _effects: state_add pc20-public-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:1531; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; knowledge “照片下面被画了一道铅笔横线，意思是这张照片还没有说完。”; flag +public_reversal_active_

- **1954.01** `state_reaction:eh19-late-testimony/official-return` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  法院的人又来了，这次带着上一回的笔录。Grete Huber看见自己的句子被打印成另一种语气，旁边还留着书记员画的一个问号；对方说只改一处，改完就可以回家。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 47 -> 55; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.danger 20 -> 25_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:020349_

- **1954.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Alois Hofer这周主动擦了黑板。Grete Huber把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 31 -> 34; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:020566_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:em18-late-bloom/late-life-year/takes-root` tags: em18-late-bloom, elderhood, late_life, reinvention, love, work, legacy, risk
  又一年过去，Grete Huber把新生活的开销记在一本旧账簿背面：房租、药、车票，还有一件为公开场合买的衬衫。事情没有变轻，但Grete Huber已经知道每周哪几天有力气，哪几天只适合坐在窗边。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.nerve 60 -> 64; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.stamina 48 -> 45; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 46 -> 44; cash 1 (small receipts from the new work); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:under-investigation/case-progress/evidence` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick
  调查员又来找Grete Huber谈了一次。问话比上次具体，记录也比上次长。
  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 45 -> 60; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:p21-scandal-web/the-web-tightens/a-strand-snaps` tags: p21-scandal-web, scandal_web, scandal, secret, blackmail, network, leverage, danger
  一个证人搬走了，带走了他那一份记忆。Grete Huber在旧地址门口站了半个下午，回来时发现信箱里多了一张没有署名的明信片，上面只写着“我还记得”。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.exposure 83 -> 91; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.leverage 54 -> 48; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 70 -> 75; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:p21-fortune-reversal/the-crowd-recounts/the-accountant-calls` tags: p21-fortune-reversal, fortune_reversal, fortune, reversal, inheritance, ruin, public, witnesses
  会计带着第二本账来了。那本账上没有Grete Huber的名字，只有一串日期和几笔“待核”的款项；会计把钢笔帽拧上，说这次不是来道喜的。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.debt 10 -> 22; state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.witnesses 70 -> 65; state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.fragility 50 -> 60; flag +fortune_called_in; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Grete Huber把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Grete Huber来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 3; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Grete Huber被叫到学校，老师说Maria 2这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Grete Huber没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 31 -> 26; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 47; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1954.03** `em18-late-bloom.shop-sign::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/a-repair-bench` tags: elderhood, late_life, shop, craft, work, reinvention, neighborhood, vienna
  修鞋铺隔壁的小门面空了出来，Grete Huber用两克朗付了押金，把旧车床搬进去。招牌是请街角的老漆匠写的，只写了“修理”两个字；第一天来了三个人，一个修伞，一个修锁，还有一个只是进来躲雨。
  _effects: state_add em18-late-bloom state:world-vienna-1912:1604; cash -2 (the deposit on the narrow shop); skill; reputation 0.01999999999999999; flag +late_bloom_active_

- **1954.04** `p9-gap-elderhood-daily.old-colleague-funeral/p9-gap-elderhood-daily.old-colleague-funeral.reunion` tags: elderhood, daily, pension, housing, care, funeral, colleague, memory
  葬礼上，Grete Huber在人群里认出了多年不见的Helene Winter 2，两人在公墓门外的小酒馆檐下站着说话，说起旧车间的班次和食堂的汤。Helene Winter 2把自己地址写在纸片上，约好下月到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang附近的咖啡馆喝茶，Grete Huber把纸片压在工资册里夹好。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1954.04** `eh19-bridge.question-arrives::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/ask-a-neighbor-first` tags: elderhood, testimony, history, memory, bureaucracy, ordinary, vienna, urban
  Grete Huber没有立刻填，拿着问话单去敲隔壁的门。开门的人比她年轻二十岁，说那年还没搬来；两个人站在楼道里把纸看了一遍，最后只确认了门牌号。回家后，Grete Huber把问话单压在糖罐下面。
  _effects: state_add eh19-late-testimony state:world-vienna-1912:1605; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +late_testimony_active_

- **1954.04** `state_reaction:pc20-public-reversal/someone-defends` tags: pc20-public-reversal, public_reversal, exoneration, accusation, testimony, proof, pressure, support
  一个旧同事在公开场合替Grete Huber说了话，没有把Grete Huber说成无辜，只把当年那份名单的次序讲清楚。第二天，另一个人在面包店门口拦住Grete Huber，说自己一直觉得哪里不对。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.support 30 -> 37; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.proof 40 -> 44; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:021216_

  Grete Huber没有改口，把上一次的说法一字一句重复完。Katharina Weiss问他是否确定，Grete Huber说记得的不是纸上的日期，是门开后屋里那股煤气味；Alois Lechner 2在旁听席上抬起头，第一次没有躲开他的目光。

  又过了一年，那几张纸仍锁在Grete Huber的抽屉里。街上的问话没有变多，也没有变少；Grete Huber在咖啡馆里数了数还能信任的人，发现名单比去年短，但每一个名字都还站得住。

  一笔该到的钱没有到。Grete Huber在账房里坐到天黑，听见隔壁把算盘珠子拨得很快；回家时，门口已经有人等着，手里拿着一份折过三次的账单。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.height 60 -> 52; state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.fragility 60 -> 68; state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.debt 22 -> 27; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

  钱在一年里薄了一层：屋顶要修，姐夫来借，彩票站的老主顾说有个稳赚的买卖。Grete Huber把账本翻到最后一页，发现写下的支出比记得的多出四克朗；窗外有人在敲铁皮，声音像在催账。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 20 -> 15; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1955.01** `p9-gap-elderhood-daily.landlord-heat/p9-gap-elderhood-daily.landlord-heat.more-hour` tags: elderhood, daily, pension, housing, care, heating, winter
  Friedrich Berg把温度计从窗台拿下来给Grete Huber看，说煤价涨归涨，老人屋里不能只烧两小时。房东松口说早晚各多烧半小时，月租却加了一克朗二十赫勒，当场记进小本子；Marie Huber蹲在炉边没抬头，只把敲下来的烟灰扫进铁簸箕。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (暖气加时后上涨的月租); condition; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001876:child_

- **1955.02** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Franz Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Grete Huber把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 34 -> 37; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:021452_

- **1955.02** `em18-late-bloom.shop-sign::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-lease-is-refused` tags: elderhood, late_life, shop, craft, work, reinvention, neighborhood, vienna
  房东把Grete Huber的押金推回来，说这年纪要有担保人，还要一次付清半年房租。Grete Huber在门口站了一会儿，把写好的招牌尺寸纸折起来；回家路上，Grete Huber经过那扇空门面，里面已经挂上了成衣铺的布帘。
  _effects: cash -1 (the deposit was returned after a day); mood -0.020000000000000018; knowledge “房东要求半年房租一次付清，才肯把空门面租给六十岁以上的人。”_

- **1956.01** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Grete Huber的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Grete Huber家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 5 -> 6; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:021934_

- **1956.01** `pc20-testimony.retraction::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/retract` tags: elderhood, testimony, court, retraction, public_reversal, memory, witness, politics
  Grete Huber在法庭上把上一次的日期改了回来，说那天先响的是门，不是窗。Luise Weiss让他确认，Grete Huber说确认；Luise Winter 2在旁听席上低下头，手指在膝盖上敲了两下，像在数一件只有他记得的事。
  _effects: state_add pc20-public-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:1674; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 43 -> 28; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 38 -> 48; flag +retracted_testimony; knowledge “那天先响的是门，不是窗；{subject}在法庭上把日期改了回来。”; flag -public_reversal_active; flag +public_reversal_active_

- **1956.01** `state_reaction:pc20-public-reversal/someone-defends` tags: pc20-public-reversal, public_reversal, exoneration, accusation, testimony, proof, pressure, support
  一个旧同事在公开场合替Grete Huber说了话，没有把Grete Huber说成无辜，只把当年那份名单的次序讲清楚。第二天，另一个人在面包店门口拦住Grete Huber，说自己一直觉得哪里不对。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.support 30 -> 37; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.proof 30 -> 34; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:022136_

  一个证人搬走了，带走了他那一份记忆。Grete Huber在旧地址门口站了半个下午，回来时发现信箱里多了一张没有署名的明信片，上面只写着“我还记得”。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.exposure 88 -> 96; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.leverage 48 -> 42; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 72 -> 77; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:p21-fortune-reversal/the-crowd-recounts/the-scaffold-shakes` tags: p21-fortune-reversal, fortune_reversal, fortune, reversal, inheritance, ruin, public, witnesses
  一笔该到的钱没有到。Grete Huber在账房里坐到天黑，听见隔壁把算盘珠子拨得很快；回家时，门口已经有人等着，手里拿着一份折过三次的账单。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.height 52 -> 44; state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.fragility 68 -> 76; state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.debt 27 -> 32; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Grete Huber把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Grete Huber来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 6 -> 7; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:eh19-late-testimony/version-drift/rehearsed` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  又过了半年，Grete Huber把证词在心里走了一遍。顺序还在，但每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人补上的颜色；Grete Huber把最重要的日期写在火柴盒背面，睡前又看了一次。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.memory 43 -> 46; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 28 -> 26; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 48 -> 50; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Grete Huber把Maria 2的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Maria 2今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Grete Huber听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 29 -> 37; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 47 -> 44; mood 0.020000000000000018_
